Abstract: As LLMs increasingly assist in moral reasoning, omission bias, the tendency to prefer inaction even when equivalent framings reverse substantive outcomes, poses a significant risk of skewed decision-making. Yet omission bias remains underexplored in LLM evaluation, with the few existing studies limited in scale and focused largely on utilitarian-deontological conflicts. To address this gap, we introduce OMIT, a benchmark consisting of 218 paired-frame scenarios across 10 conflict types, constructed by leveraging disagreement patterns from an LLM-based, five-perspective philosophical persona panel (utilitarianism, deontology, virtue ethics, care ethics, and contractualism). Evaluating eight LLMs, we find that omission bias is pervasive but inversely correlates with model size within families. We further evaluate four inference-time interventions and find that interventions encouraging models to consider moral principles before committing to a yes/no answer reduce omission bias and increase frame-consistent responses, although lower omission bias rates can also coincide with shifts toward action-biased responses. Ultimately, this work contributes not only the OMIT benchmark, but also a methodology for using diverse philosophical disagreement signals to evaluate framing-sensitive inaction preferences and the distributional effects of mitigation attempts in LLMs under complex moral conflicts.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07848)
*Dayan Pan,Jingyuan Wang,Xie Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DyPAM：一种针对注意力位置信息的动态、结构化参数高效微调方法，在数学与常识推理基准上优于现有PEFT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法普遍采用统一、静态的适配方式，忽略了注意力在维度、头、层和输入token上的结构异质性；同时RoPE等位置编码引入与维度相关的位置结构，使得统一适配并非最优。

Method: 提出DyPAM（动态位置注意力调制），直接作用于query与key表示，调控位置信息对注意力的贡献。其结合输入条件化的维度级调制，以及头级与层级的结构调制，在不改动预训练骨干的前提下，按RoPE诱导的结构实现细粒度的位置注意力适配。

Result: 在多个骨干模型的数学与常识推理基准上，DyPAM持续优于现有强PEFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过对位置注意力进行动态且结构化的细粒度调制，可更充分地利用注意力的异质性，为PEFT提供有效且与RoPE结构对齐的新范式。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) has become a standard approach for adapting large language models to downstream tasks. However, most existing PEFT methods rely on uniform and static adaptations, without accounting for the structured heterogeneity of attention across dimensions, heads, layers, and input tokens. In practice, attention representations exhibit non-uniform behavior, and positional encoding mechanisms such as rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) induce dimension-dependent positional structure, making uniform adaptation suboptimal. In this work, we propose DyPAM (Dynamic Positional Attention Modulation), a PEFT method that adapts how positional information contributes to attention by operating directly on the query and key representations. DyPAM combines input-conditioned, dimension-wise modulation with head-wise and layer-wise structural modulation, performing fine-grained adaptation of positional attention aligned with the RoPE-induced structure without modifying the pretrained backbone. Extensive experiments on mathematical and commonsense reasoning benchmarks across multiple backbone models demonstrate that DyPAM consistently outperforms existing strong PEFT baselines.

</details>


### [170] [ReFold: Training-Free Reversible Inter-Turn Context Folding for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07863)
*Yupeng Su,Jiayi Tian,Zheng Zhang,Souvik Kundu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReFold是一种免训练的渲染层方法，通过可逆地压缩模型渲染上下文（去除重复内容与已完成轮次）来缓解长程LLM智能体的上下文与成本增长问题，在不降低任务成功率的前提下显著减少token消耗、KV缓存内存和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体在每步都会重新发送追加式交互历史，导致上下文及其成本随步数增长，直至超出上下文窗口。现有方法依赖上下文需求预测（额外模型调用、启发式规则或训练策略）来管理上下文，但存在运行时开销、使前缀缓存失效、且永久丢弃内容无法保证恢复等问题。

Method: 提出ReFold：一种免训练的渲染层，保留底层交互历史，仅压缩模型渲染的上下文。无需辅助预测器即可去除两类轮次间冗余：（1）早前轮次已展示的内容，替换为桩（stub）；（2）智能体自身报告已完成的轮次，折叠为一行注释。两个算子均采用分块渲染，每隔数步重写一次缓存前缀而非每步重写。每次移除严格可逆，误删仅需从历史中恢复一次，而非永久丢失内容。因作用于渲染层，可即插即用于标准ReAct式框架。

Result: 在五个长程基准和两个前沿LLM上评估：token消耗最多减少2.5倍，每会话KV缓存内存减半，且不降低任务成功率；在受限上下文预算下避免最多92%的强制压缩；在并发服务负载下，请求排队延迟最多降低100%，推理加速最多1.7倍，推理成本最多降低3.4倍。

Conclusion: ReFold通过渲染层的可逆压缩，在不牺牲任务表现的情况下有效解决长程智能体的上下文膨胀与成本问题，且具备即插即用、无需训练的优势，适配并发服务场景。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents act on an append-only interaction history that is re-sent to the model at every step, so the context and its cost grow with steps until the sessions exceed the context window. Existing methods manage the context through context requirement prediction, relying on additional model calls, heuristic rules, or trained policies. However, these predictive approaches introduce runtime overhead, invalidate prefix caches, and permanently discard content with no guarantee of recovery. To overcome these limitations, we introduce ReFold: a training-free rendering layer that preserves the underlying interaction history while compressing only the model's rendered context. It removes two kinds of inter-turn redundancy without an auxiliary predictor: content an earlier turn already displayed, replaced by a stub, and turns the agent itself reports finished, folded into a one-line note. Both operators use chunked rendering, rewriting the cached prefix once every few steps rather than at every step. Every removal is strictly reversible, a wrong removal costs one restore from the history rather than permanent content loss. Because it operates at the rendering layer, ReFold is plug-and-play across standard ReAct-style harnesses. Evaluations across five long-horizon benchmarks and two frontier LLMs demonstrate that ReFold reduces token consumption by up to 2.5x and halves the KV-cache memory per session without degrading task success rates. Under capped context budgets, it avoids up to 92% of forced compactions. Under concurrent serving workloads, it reduces request queuing delays by up to 100%, accelerating inference by up to 1.7x, while cutting inference costs by up to 3.4x.

</details>


### [171] [Visual Abstention in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07887)
*Chufan Shi,Cheng Yang,Tiannuo Yang,Isadora White,Yiwei Chen,Taylor Berg-Kirkpatrick,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"视觉拒答"（visual abstention）概念与DoD基准，揭示统一多模态模型的编辑能力与拒答能力相互独立；并提出VisTA训练方法，使模型在生成前先判断任务可行性，从而在高成功率编辑的同时大幅提升对不可行请求的拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）虽整合了理解与生成，但其生成行为很少受"对任务的理解"所支配。当请求的视觉变换在任务规则下不可能实现时，模型应识别并声明无解、拒绝生成，但现有模型普遍缺乏这一能力。

Method: 1）形式化"视觉拒答"任务，提出DoD（Draw-or-Decline）基准：1,050 对可行-不可行请求对，覆盖 7 类任务，同时衡量编辑成功率与对不可行请求的拒绝率；2）评估 8 个 UMMs，并分析其推理过程；3）测试显式提示报告不可行性的效果；4）提出VisTA（Visual Transformation and Abstention）训练方法，配对可行与不可行样本，使模型在决定是否生成前先判断可行性，并据此训练 VisTA-BAGEL。

Result: 在 8 个 UMMs 上，编辑能力与拒答能力相互独立：最强编辑器编辑准确率 68.4%，但在普通指令下仅拒绝 0.4% 的不可行请求。推理显示模型很少察觉冲突，反而按请求可行的方式规划编辑，常描述图中不存在的物体，或悄悄将请求改为可完成者。显式提示可提高文本拒绝率，但会降低编辑准确率。VisTA-BAGEL 在无提醒情况下拒绝 93.0% 的不可行请求（远高于最强编辑器的 0.4%），对可行请求的误拒率仅 0.8%；且不牺牲编辑能力，完成 74.3% 的可行编辑，优于评估的全部 8 个 UMMs。

Conclusion: 模型对任务可行性的判断应成为生成的前置环节。通过配对训练（VisTA），可在不依赖外部提醒、不损害编辑性能的前提下，显著提升统一多模态模型的视觉拒答能力，实现"要么绘制、要么拒绝"的可靠生成行为。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) integrate understanding and generation, yet their generative behavior is rarely governed by what they understand about the task. We formalize visual abstention: when a requested visual transformation is impossible under the task's rules, the model should recognize that no valid solution exists, state this, and decline to generate. We introduce Draw-or-Decline (DoD), a benchmark of 1,050 feasible-infeasible request pairs across 7 task categories that jointly measures editing success and the refusal of infeasible requests. Evaluating 8 UMMs, we find that editing ability and abstention are distinct capabilities: even the strongest editor, at 68.4% editing accuracy, refuses only 0.4% of infeasible requests under ordinary instructions. Their reasoning shows why: the models rarely notice the conflict, and instead plan the edit as if the request were possible, often describing objects that are not in the image, or quietly change the request into one they can complete. Explicitly prompting these UMMs to report infeasibility increases textual refusals but reduces editing accuracy. We propose VisTA (Visual Transformation and Abstention), a training method that pairs feasible and infeasible examples so that a model judges feasibility before deciding whether to generate. We train VisTA-BAGEL to perform feasible edits and decline infeasible requests. Without any reminder, it refuses 93.0% of infeasible requests, up from 0.4% for the strongest editor, while falsely refusing only 0.8% of feasible ones. Unlike a reminder, this does not cost editing accuracy: VisTA-BAGEL completes 74.3% of feasible edits, more than any of the 8 evaluated UMMs.

</details>


### [172] [Rethinking Faithfulness in LLMs: A Pairwise Context-Sensitive Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07894)
*Zizhuo Zhang,Xiong Peng,Jingwei Sun,Rong Yao,Borui Jiang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出成对忠实性基准 PFaithBench，评估模型在同一问题下能否随支撑/非支撑上下文切换回答与弃答，发现忠实性本质是回答-弃答的权衡，且训练结果对数据构成高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有忠实性评估通常孤立地考察单个"问题-上下文"实例，无法捕捉忠实行为的核心要求：模型应根据可用上下文的变化调整响应。即充分证据时给出正确答案，证据不足时弃答。

Method: 提出成对忠实性基准 PFaithBench，针对同一问题在支撑性与非支撑性上下文下评估模型能否在回答与弃答之间切换。在7个模型族共39个模型上评测，并开展不同数据构成下的忠实性训练研究。

Result: 评测显示忠实性本质上是回答与弃答之间的权衡，多数现有模型存在强烈的"偏好回答"偏差，忠实性错误多源于过度回答（上下文不足时仍编造答案）。训练研究显示：训练结果对回答/弃答数据的具体构成高度敏感；用不匹配来源构建两类数据会使模型依赖数据集特有捷径而非真正的上下文充分性；增加回答监督数据提升回答性能但加剧过度回答，增加弃答数据减少幻觉但导致过度弃答。

Conclusion: 忠实性评估应超越实例级、转向对上下文变化适应能力的成对评估；忠实性训练的关键在于回答与弃答数据的合理配比与来源一致性，以避免捷径依赖、过度回答与过度弃答。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are expected to answer questions faithfully based on the provided context, abstaining when the context information is insufficient to answer the questions. Existing faithfulness evaluations typically assess each question-context instance in isolation; however, such instance-level evaluation fails to capture a fundamental requirement of faithful behavior: the ability to adapt model responses to changes in available contexts. In particular, a model should provide correct answers when sufficient evidence is present and abstain when it is not. In this work, we propose a Pairwise Faithfulness Benchmark (PFaithBench) that evaluates whether a model can switch between answering and abstaining for the same question under supporting versus non-supporting contexts. Our evaluations across thirty-nine models with seven model families demonstrate that faithfulness fundamentally involves a trade-off between answering and abstaining, and that most current models exhibit a strong bias toward answering, with most faithfulness errors arising from over-answering, i.e., models tend to fabricate a response even when the provided context is insufficient. We further conduct a series of studies on faithfulness training under different data constructions. Our results show that training outcomes are highly sensitive to the specific composition of answering and abstaining data. Constructing answering and abstaining data from mismatched sources can cause models to rely on dataset-specific shortcuts rather than actual context sufficiency. Moreover, increasing answer-supervised data improves answering performance but exacerbates over-answering, while increasing abstaining data reduces hallucination but leads to over-abstention. The code and data are released at https://github.com/tmlr-group/PFaithBench.

</details>


### [173] [ARIA: Audio-Driven Melody-Tone Relation Modeling for Cantonese Lyric Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07902)
*Shengyu Li,Jinting Wang,Li Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ARIA框架，从带时间戳的演唱音频直接生成粤语歌词，通过两阶段建模音高与声调关系，并构建大规模对齐数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粤语歌词创作要求字调与旋律音高紧密对齐。现有旋律引导的歌词生成方法依赖符号化旋律，但真实创作场景中旋律常表现为原始演唱音频或哼唱录音，其音高隐含、含噪且非结构化，导致现有方法难以直接应用。

Method: ARIA为两阶段音频驱动的旋律-声调关系建模框架，输入带字符级时间戳的演唱录音。第一阶段设计三流关系感知声调估计器（TRATE），通过建模多流声学线索与关系性声调结构，从时间戳演唱音频预测0243序列；第二阶段提出解耦检索增强的声调条件歌词生成器（DRA-TCLG），以预测的声调计划为条件，结合检索增强的词汇引导生成流畅歌词。此外，构建了大规模对齐的音频-粤拼-0243数据集。

Result: 实验表明，ARIA在0243预测和声调一致的歌词生成两项任务上均取得强劲性能。

Conclusion: 结果验证了所提框架的有效性，展示了从原始演唱音频进行粤语歌词创作的可行性。

Abstract: Cantonese lyric writing requires close alignment between lexical tones and melodic pitch. Existing melody-guided lyric generation methods typically rely on symbolic melody to generate lyrics. However, in real songwriting scenarios, melodies are often expressed as raw singing audio or hummed recordings, where pitch is implicit, noisy, and unstructured, making these methods difficult to apply directly. To address this limitation, we propose ARIA, a two-stage audio-driven melody-tone relation modeling framework for Cantonese lyric authoring that generates Cantonese lyrics from singing recordings with provided character-level timestamps. Specifically, we first design a Tri-Stream Relation-Aware Tone Estimator (TRATE) to predict 0243 sequences from timestamped singing audio by modeling multi-stream acoustic cues and relational tonal structure. We then propose a Decoupled Retrieval-Augmented Tone-Conditioned Lyric Generator (DRA-TCLG) to generate fluent lyrics conditioned on predicted tonal plans with retrieval-enhanced lexical guidance. Moreover, we construct a large-scale aligned audio-Jyutping-0243 dataset from real Cantonese singing recordings to support this new task. Experimental results demonstrate that ARIA achieves strong performance in both 0243 prediction and tone-consistent lyric generation, validating the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [174] [Pseudowords as probes: Large Language Models show little of the sublexical sensitivity that governs human pseudoword processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07936)
*Jing Chen,Giulia Loca,Simona Amenta,Marco Marelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究用两项意大利语二选一假词实验比较了五个大语言模型与人类行为基线，发现LLM仅在含真实词熟悉性线索时与人类对齐较好，在纯假词条件下显著低于fastText字符n-gram模型，且驱动人类-fastText一致性的亚词汇余弦相似度线索未能稳定迁移到人类-LLM对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言的系统性（形式到意义的概率映射）贯穿各语言层级，亚词汇线索已被证明支配人类的假词加工。但LLM是否对这类线索具有可比敏感性尚不明确，因此需要将LLM反应与人类行为基线直接对照，检验二者是否共享相同的亚词汇加工机制。

Method: 在两项意大利语二选一强制选择假词实验中测试五个LLM，并与其人类行为基线进行比较；同时引入fastText（字符n-gram模型）作为对照，考察亚词汇余弦相似度线索在人类-fastText一致性与人类-LLM一致性中的传递情况，并分析推理token消耗与人类加工难度的关系。

Result: 当真实词选项提供词汇熟悉性线索时，LLM与人类的对齐更可靠；而在纯假词条件下，LLM表现显著低于fastText。可靠驱动人类-fastText一致性的亚词汇余弦相似度线索并未稳定迁移至人类-LLM对齐，推理token消耗与人类加工难度之间也无一致关系。

Conclusion: LLM未必共享支配人类假词加工的亚词汇线索；作者将分词机制与训练数据覆盖度作为候选解释加以讨论。

Abstract: Systematicity, the probabilistic mapping of form to meaning, permeates language at all levels, and sublexical cues have been shown to govern human pseudoword processing. Yet whether LLMs exhibit comparable sensitivity to these cues remains unclear. We tested five LLMs on two Italian two-alternative forced-choice pseudoword experiments and compared their responses with a human behavioural baseline. LLMs aligned more reliably with humans when real-word options provided a lexical familiarity cue than in the pseudoword-only condition, where they fell substantially below fastText, a character-n-gram model. In addition, the sublexical cosine-similarity cue that reliably drove human--fastText agreement did not consistently transfer to human--LLM alignment, and reasoning-token expenditure bore no consistent relation to human processing difficulty. These findings suggest that LLMs do not necessarily share the sublexical cues that govern human pseudoword processing; we discuss tokenization and training-data coverage as candidate explanations.

</details>


### [175] [Leveraging a four-quadrant approach for evaluating Redpine Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07937)
*Filip Dorm,Leonora Vesterbacka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本报告从检索相关性与模型答题质量两个层面评估 Redpine Science（通过 MCP/API 访问同行评审文献），结合公开基准与专家验证问题集，共给出四项评测结果：ScholarQABench SciFact 答题正确率 94.4%，专家验证集必需论断覆盖 80.1%，检索 Recall@10 达 83.1%，专家盲评 Precision@5 为 75.2%，均优于对照。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准虽被广泛接受且便于跨实验室比较，但存在饱和与记忆化风险。为弥补这一缺陷，需引入专家验证的问题集，从检索相关性和模型答题质量两个层面综合评估 Redpine Science 的实际价值。

Method: 在 Redpine Science 上开展四项评测：一是 ScholarQABench SciFact 公开答题质量基准；二是专家验证问题集，对比使用 Redpine Science 的智能体与仅限网络搜索的智能体；三是在 Redpine 持有金标准论文的 668 条公开检索查询上、剥离模型推理后测 Recall@10；四是由盲评专家组评估 Precision@5，并与 PubMed 检索工具对比。

Result: SciFact 上使用 Redpine Science 的智能体正确回答 94.4% 的论断，无检索时为 87.6%；专家验证集上 Redpine Science 覆盖 80.1% 的必需论断，仅用网络搜索为 70.2%；668 条检索查询中 Recall@10 为 83.1%，基准创建者为 79.3%；专家盲评 Precision@5 为 75.2%，PubMed 检索工具为 39.8%。

Conclusion: Redpine Science 在答题质量与检索相关性上均优于无检索、网络搜索、基准创建者及 PubMed 等对照，且公开了专家验证问题集与全部复现说明，评测结果可复现。

Abstract: Redpine Science gives models and agents a single access point to a wide range of peer-reviewed literature, queried directly through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) and an API. This report evaluates Redpine Science on two levels: the relevance of the retrieved chunks, and a model's answer when it has access to Redpine Science compared to web search. Both public and expert-validated benchmarks are used. Public benchmarks are a widely accepted way to test model development and are comparable across labs, but risk saturation and memorization. To address this, we complement them with an expert-validated question set. In total, this report presents four evaluations. On ScholarQABench SciFact, the public answer-quality benchmark reported here, an agent with Redpine Science answers 94.4% of claims correctly against 87.6% with no retrieval. On the expert-validated question set, an agent with Redpine Science states 80.1% of the required claims against 70.2% for an agent restricted to web search. On the 668 queries of a public retrieval benchmark whose gold paper Redpine holds, stripped of any model reasoning, Redpine Science places the correct source paper in its top ten results for 83.1% of queries (Recall@10), against 79.3% for the benchmark's creator. A blinded expert relevance panel places Redpine Science's Precision@5 at 75.2% against 39.8% for the PubMed search tool. We release the expert-validated question set and instructions to reproduce every headline result above, at https://github.com/redpine-ai/benchmarks.

</details>


### [176] [Hybrid Latent Attention for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07940)
*Yuhan Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Nan Wang,Feiyang Kang,Ruoxi Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出混合潜在注意力（HLA），在循环语言模型中保留窗口内精确KV、将更早token压缩为潜在表示供查询直接读取，从而在几乎不损失精度的情况下大幅缩小KV缓存并提升解码吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型对每个token重复应用同一层堆叠T次，能在不增加参数的情况下加深模型，但会使KV缓存扩大T倍。更大的缓存限制了GPU可同时解码的序列数，并使每步解码因需读取整个缓存而变慢，因此亟需一种在不牺牲精度前提下压缩循环模型KV缓存的方法。

Method: 提出Hybrid Latent Attention（HLA）：在最近W个token的滑动窗口内保留精确的键和值，对更早的token则存储为紧凑的潜在表示，每层循环的查询直接读取该潜在表示，而不重建键和值。在Ouro循环模型（T=4，1.4B与2.6B参数）上进行uptrain，冻结预训练权重，仅训练新增参数以复现原始注意力行为。

Result: 每token缓存缩小10.7倍，单GPU可容纳的并发序列数提升4.0-8.8倍；解码吞吐在1K上下文提升2.5倍，16K上下文最高提升7.4倍。在数学、知识和推理基准上保留超过97%的原始精度，在最长16K token的长上下文检索上保留96-100%。经监督微调后，在竞赛级数学任务上与微调后的原始模型表现相当。

Conclusion: HLA通过窗口精确KV加历史潜在表示的混合注意力设计，有效解决了循环语言模型KV缓存膨胀问题，在显著降低显存占用、提升并发与解码吞吐的同时保持了接近原始的精度和长上下文能力，为循环模型的实用部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Looped language models apply the same stack of layers T times to each token, which deepens the model without adding parameters but multiplies its key-value (KV) cache by T. The larger cache limits how many sequences a GPU can decode at once and slows each decoding step, which reads the whole cache. We propose Hybrid Latent Attention (HLA), which keeps exact keys and values within a sliding window of W recent tokens and stores each older token as a compact latent that the query of each loop reads directly, without reconstructing keys and values. We uptrain HLA on Ouro looped models (T=4) with 1.4B and 2.6B parameters, keeping the pretrained weights frozen and training only the added parameters to reproduce the original attention. The cache shrinks by 10.7x per token, fitting 4.0-8.8x as many concurrent sequences per GPU, and decoding throughput improves by 2.5x at 1K-token contexts and by up to 7.4x at 16K. HLA retains over 97% of the original accuracy on math, knowledge and reasoning benchmarks, and 96-100% on long-context retrieval up to 16K tokens. After supervised fine-tuning, it performs on par with the fine-tuned original model on competition-level math.

</details>


### [177] [Structured but Silent: Probing Capability Requirements in LLM Hidden States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08018)
*Kyojun Choo,Minsoo Song,Yunju Kang,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM能否在生成前从隐藏表示中线性解码查询所需的能力需求，提出TACIT框架（Source、Transformation、World Effect三轴八类），在四个开源模型上训练线性探针，发现细粒度能力结构可高精度线性解码，但模型在显式言语分类上可靠性显著更低，即"结构化但沉默"的表示—言语鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的工具使用不仅依赖触发机制或查询与API描述的匹配，还需在选具体工具前先推断查询隐含的能力需求。论文旨在探究这些查询侧能力需求能否在生成前从LLM隐藏表示中线性解码，以及与显式言语分类相比，该隐藏状态的可及性如何。

Method: 提出TACIT框架，将外部需求沿Source（来源）、Transformation（转换）、World Effect（世界效应）三个基本轴分解，定义八个结构上不同的能力类别。使用来自基准、合成样本和新领域场景的1,600条平衡训练查询，在四个开源权重LLM家族的生成前隐藏状态上训练线性探针。

Result: 实证结果表明，细粒度能力结构在所有模型上均可高精度线性解码。然而，模型在要求以自然语言显式分类相同查询时可靠性显著较低，暴露出表示—言语化鸿沟。

Conclusion: 所需外部能力的信息在LLM隐藏表示中线性可及，但不能被可靠表达，作者将这一现象定义为"structured but silent"（结构化但沉默）。

Abstract: Reliable tool use requires more than triggering a mechanism or matching a query to an API description. Before selecting a specific tool, an agent must first infer the capability requirements implied by the user query. In this paper, we investigate whether these query-side capability requirements are linearly decodable from LLM hidden representations prior to generation, and how this hidden-state accessibility compares with explicit verbal classification. We introduce TACIT, a framework that decomposes external requirements along three fundamental axes: Source, Transformation, and World Effect, defining eight structurally distinct capability classes. Using 1,600 balanced training queries from benchmarks, synthetic examples, and new domain scenarios, we train linear probes on pre-generation hidden states from four open-weight LLM families. Our empirical results demonstrate that fine-grained capability structures are linearly decodable with high accuracy across all models. Crucially, however, we expose a representation-to-verbalization gap: these same models are significantly less reliable when asked to explicitly classify the same queries in natural language. This disconnect indicates that information about required external capabilities is linearly accessible in LLM hidden representations but not reliably expressed, a phenomenon we define as "structured but silent."

</details>


### [178] [The Labeling Problem in Hallucination Detection Benchmarks: An Empirical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08026)
*Jorma Valjakka,Juhani Kivimäki,Juha Mylläri,Jukka K. Nurminen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了开放域QA幻觉检测基准中自动标注策略存在的"标准错配"问题：参考忠实性与事实正确性两个准则被混用，导致自动标注与人工标注严重不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年大量LLM幻觉检测方法以开放域QA数据集为基础，通过将LLM生成答案与数据集中的短参考答对比来自动标注幻觉。然而"参考忠实性"（答案是否被参考完全支持）与"事实正确性"（答案是否无矛盾、无虚假具体断言）是两种不同准则，自动标注器常在意图采用后者时实际应用了前者，形成方法学歧义。作者希望量化这种准则错配的程度与后果。

Method: 构建900个经人工标注的问答对，覆盖三个常用QA数据集和三个生成模型，标签以答案级事实正确性为目标。将词汇相似度指标、参考蕴含NLI基线以及七种LLM评判者（在受控提示变体下）作为自动标注器进行对比评估，考察其与人工标注的一致性和错误方向性偏差。

Result: 各自动标注策略之间及其与人工标注之间均存在显著分歧；多数策略表现出强烈的方向性错误偏差。对大多数"评判者—生成器"组合，将面向忠实性的提示替换为面向事实正确性的提示后，与人工标注的一致度提升，假阳性主导现象减弱。

Conclusion: 自动幻觉标签强烈依赖于目标准则的表述方式。标签来源的选择应被视为基准设计的核心组成，必须显式声明、经过验证，并与基准目标相匹配。

Abstract: In recent years, several methods for detecting when large language models (LLMs) hallucinate have been developed. These methods are often benchmarked with open-domain question answering (QA) datasets containing questions and corresponding short reference answers. First, an LLM is used to generate answers to questions within the QA dataset. Then, some automated labeling strategy is used to label these answers as hallucinated or not by comparing them with the reference answers in the dataset. This evaluation setting creates a methodological ambiguity between two criteria: reference faithfulness (whether the answer is fully supported by the reference) and factual correctness (whether the answer is free from contradictions and factually false specific claims). In practice, automated labelers may apply the former criterion even when the intended target is the latter. We study this potential criterion mismatch using 900 human-labeled question-answer pairs spanning three commonly used QA datasets and three generator models, with labels targeting answer-level factual correctness. We evaluate lexical similarity metrics, a reference-entailment NLI baseline, and seven LLM judges under controlled prompt variants as automated labelers. Our experiments reveal substantial disagreement both among automated labeling strategies and between these labels and human annotations. Many strategies also exhibit strong directional error biases, and for most judge-generator pairs, replacing a faithfulness-oriented prompt with a factual-correctness prompt improves agreement with human annotations and reduces false-positive dominance, indicating that automated hallucination labels depend strongly on how the target criterion is specified. Label-source choice should therefore be considered a fundamental part of benchmark design and made explicit, validated, and matched with the benchmark goal.

</details>


### [179] [Are Language Models Script-Aware?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08037)
*David Kletz,Sandra Mitrović,Ljiljana Dolamić,Fabio Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究测试小/大语言模型是否具备"文字系统知识"，通过两个实验发现模型普遍具有显著的文字系统知识：拉丁字母保真度接近完美（>98%），且能高频遵循文字系统指令；大模型表现优于小模型，非标准文字组合亦如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常输出非预期语言或文字（off-target generation）。已有研究多聚焦"语言选择"，而"文字系统知识"维度研究不足——用户必须先识别模型回复中的图形符号，才能进行任何语言理解。

Method: 以多文字系统语言为测试对象，对小型与大型语言模型（SLMs/LLMs）设计两个互补实验：(1) 评估模型能否将输出文字系统适配输入文字系统；(2) 评估模型能否遵循显式指令生成指定文字系统的文本。

Result: 所测模型均展现出实质性的文字系统知识：全部模型达到近乎完美的拉丁文字保真度（>98%），并以高频率遵循文字系统指令；同时观察到LLM与SLM之间存在差异，LLM在整体及非标准文字组合上得分更高。

Conclusion: 语言模型（尤其是LLM）具备较强的文字系统知识，能够适配输入并遵循指令；文字系统维度值得作为独立研究方向，SLM与LLM在该能力上存在系统性差距。

Abstract: Language models frequently generate outputs in unintended languages or scripts, a phenomenon known as off-target generation. While existing research has focused on language selection, the dimension of script knowledge remains understudied: before any linguistic understanding can occur, users must recognize the graphic symbols in a model's response. We investigate whether Small and Large Language Models (SLMs and LLMs) possess script knowledge by testing them on multi-scriptic languages. Through two complementary experiments, we evaluate whether models (1) adapt their output script to match the input, and (2) follow explicit instructions to generate text in a specified script. The models we tested demonstrate substantial script knowledge: they all achieve a near-perfect Latin script fidelity (more than 98%) and follow script instructions with high frequency. Nevertheless, we notice differences between LLMs and SLMs, with higher scores for LLMs including for non-standard script combinations.

</details>


### [180] [Language Carries the Expert's Impression: Instrument-Anchored LLM Judges Transfer Counseling-Quality Assessment and Beat In-Domain Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08055)
*Tobias Hallmen,Elisabeth André*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在三个德语模拟咨询语料上，跨域迁移（留一域）预测专家总体印象优于域内训练，决定性特征为小规模开源LLM提取的会话级构念分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元咨询对话沟通质量的自动评估受限于数据：专家评分的语料规模小且扩充成本高，因此需要研究能否跨域迁移专家总体印象预测。

Method: 使用三个德语模拟咨询语料（两个全科医学、一个学校相关的家长-教师对话；n=195个专家评分会话，其中一个语料经过量表等值化）。采用留一域交叉验证评估跨域迁移；使用小型开源权重LLM阅读双说话人转录文本，提取会话级构念分数（构念主要源自专家评分工具），并对比通用对话质量特征；进一步评估三模型家族集成及非言语-二元特征块的增益；同时量化录音设置问题（丢失的逐说话人音频、16%错误说话人标注）及修复收益。

Result: 留一域迁移达到嵌套Spearman ρ=0.54，而目标域内训练≤0.48，配对会话级差距为+0.15；在训练集规模匹配时差距仍为+0.12，说明并非单纯数据量效应。构念电池将单一评判者从0.32提升至0.41（相比通用对话质量）；三个模型家族集成后纯语言达到0.51；非言语-二元特征块额外增加+0.03，但在该样本量下无法与噪声区分。修复说话人标注错误收益为+0.07。

Conclusion: 在可实际获得的语料规模下，专家的总体印象主要由"说了什么"承载，且其他沟通项目的数据比自身数据更有价值。

Abstract: Automatic assessment of communication quality in dyadic counseling conversations is bottlenecked by data: expert-rated corpora are small and expensive to grow. We study cross-domain transfer of expert overall-impression prediction across three German corpora of simulated counseling (two general-practice medical, one school-related parent-teacher; $n=195$ expert-rated sessions, one corpus after scale equating). Training on the other domains beats training in-domain: leave-one-domain-out transfer reaches nested Spearman $ρ= 0.54$ against $\le 0.48$ within the target domain, a paired session-level gap of $+0.15$ that holds at $+0.12$ when the training-set sizes are matched, so it is not simply data volume. The decisive features are session-level construct scores from small open-weight LLMs reading the two-speaker transcript, with the constructs largely derived from the experts' rating instruments: the instrument-derived battery lifts a single judge from $0.32$ to $0.41$ over generic dialogue qualities, judges from three model families ensemble to $0.51$ language-only, and a nonverbal-dyadic block adds $+0.03$ more, not separable from noise at this sample size. We also price the recording setup: one corpus lost its per-speaker audio, 16% of its diarised segments carry the wrong speaker, and repair is worth $+0.07$ there. At practically attainable corpus sizes, the expert's overall impression is carried by what is said, and by other communication programs' data more than by one's own.

</details>


### [181] [DirectSpeech2LLM: A Simple End-to-End Framework to Mitigate Prompt Overfitting in Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08085)
*Hemant Yadav,Sunayana Sitaram,Roger Zimmermann,Rajiv Ratn Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 DirectSpeech2LLM，一个端到端框架，通过在冻结的 LLM 嵌入矩阵上计算基于距离的 CTC 损失，并利用贪心 CTC 标签生成几何与时序对齐的语音嵌入，从而在仅用 ASR 指令训练的情况下保留 LLM 对未见任务的指令跟随能力。该方法仅在 960 小时 LibriSpeech ASR 数据上训练，即在 ASR（已见任务）上超越级联系统，又能零样本泛化到语音翻译与情感识别（两个未见任务），接近级联系统上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Speech-LLM 存在提示过拟合问题：仅在 ASR 指令上训练的模型无法泛化到语音翻译等新指令，仍主要表现为 ASR 系统，即丧失了 LLM 原有的指令跟随能力。

Method: 提出 DirectSpeech2LLM 端到端框架。在冻结的 LLM 嵌入矩阵上计算基于距离的 CTC 损失，并用贪心 CTC 标签分别导出几何对齐与时序对齐的语音嵌入，作为 LLM 的输入。仅使用 960 小时 LibriSpeech ASR 数据训练。

Result: 在 ASR（已见任务）上超越级联系统；零样本泛化到语音翻译与情感识别（两个未见任务），在这两项新指令上接近级联系统上界，尽管训练中从未见过二者。此外发现几何对齐强度的作用小于以往假设——改进的 CTC 损失无需显式回归损失即可提供充分的隐式几何 grounding。结果在两个 LLM 系列上一致，并随训练数据量与模型容量扩展。

Conclusion: 基于距离的 CTC 损失结合贪心 CTC 标签可有效保留 LLM 的指令跟随能力并实现零样本跨任务泛化；显式几何回归损失并非必需；方法具备跨模型系列一致性与良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Speech-LLMs often exhibit prompt overfitting, where models solely trained on automatic speech recognition (ASR) instruction fail to generalize to new instructions such as speech translation and continue to behave primarily as ASR system. We propose DirectSpeech2LLM, a simple end-to-end framework that preserves the instruction-following ability of the LLM on unseen tasks when conditioned on speech. It computes distance-based CTC loss over the frozen LLM embedding matrix and uses greedy CTC labels to derive geometrically and temporally aligned speech embeddings respectively as an input to the LLM. Trained solely on 960 hours of LibriSpeech ASR data, DirectSpeech2LLM outperforms the cascaded system on ASR (seen task) and generalizes zero-shot to speech translation and emotion recognition (two unseen tasks), closely matching the cascaded system upper bound on these two new instructions despite seeing neither during training. We also find that geometric alignment strength plays a smaller role than previously assumed, as our modified CTC loss is shown to provide sufficient implicit geometric grounding without requiring an explicit regression loss. Results are consistent across two LLM families and scale with both more training data and model capacity.

</details>


### [182] [SAGE: Semantic Anchor-Guided Evolution for Grounded Medical QA Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08093)
*Chuan Li,Chengyu Wang,Cen Chen,Ye Lyu,Mingyuan Fan,Ming Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAGE数据合成框架，利用MeSH等轻量分类体系作为语义锚点，通过原子与关联合成交替迭代，使本地小模型在低资源临床环境下生成高质量医学QA训练数据，性能优于自生成与文档式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学问答等临床任务建模受限于高质量专家标注数据稀缺，同时隐私合规要求严格、临床环境资源受限，难以使用大规模开源语料或云端专有API，亟需可在本地部署的低成本数据合成方案。

Method: SAGE（Semantic Anchor-Guided Evolution）以MeSH等公开轻量分类体系作为语义锚点，形成结构化先验以约束并落地数据生成过程；在核心上交替迭代两类合成——原子合成（基于单个概念）与关联合成（基于概念间关系），从极少量种子数据自举扩展，无需大规模医学文档或外部API。

Result: 在多个医学问答基准上的大量实验表明，使用SAGE合成数据微调的模型一致优于采用自生成数据或传统基于文档范式训练的模型，在数据效率与资源利用方面取得可观测提升。

Conclusion: SAGE为本地化、隐私友好的医学LLM数据构建提供了实用路径，证明结构化语义先验可有效替代大规模语料与云端API，提升低资源临床场景下的数据效率。

Abstract: Developing reliable models for clinical tasks, such as Medical Question Answering (QA), is severely constrained by the limited availability of high-quality, expert-annotated training data. This challenge is exacerbated by stringent privacy requirements and the impracticality of utilizing large open-source corpora or proprietary cloud APIs within resource-limited clinical settings. To address these obstacles, we introduce SAGE (\textit{Semantic Anchor-Guided Evolution}), a novel data synthesis framework that enables small, locally deployed models to generate high-quality medical training data. SAGE leverages lightweight, publicly available taxonomies such as MeSH as semantic anchors, imposing a structured prior to effectively guide and ground the data generation process. At its core, SAGE iteratively interleaves atomic (individual concept-based) and associative (relation-based) synthesis, bootstrapping training data from minimal seeds. This approach eliminates the need for large collections of medical documents or reliance on external APIs, providing a practical solution for on-premises data creation. Extensive experiments across multiple medical question-answering benchmarks demonstrate that models fine-tuned with SAGE-synthesized data consistently outperform those trained using self-derived or conventional document-based paradigms, highlighting tangible improvements in data efficiency and resource utilization for medical LLM development. Code is available at https://github.com/DIaacKr/SAGE.

</details>


### [183] [Penalty-Framed No-Valid-Option MCQA: Analyzing LLM Abstention under Invalid Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08153)
*Jinhyeok Kim,Hye-Young Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"惩罚框架下的无有效选项多选题"（penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA）：在MMLU-Pro数学子集上删除正确选项，允许模型选择剩余选项或输出ABSTAIN，并对无效强制选择进行惩罚；同时提出"正确条件化分析"。结果显示高MCQA准确率并不能保证弃答可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MCQA评估假设选项中必有一个正确答案，仅以答案选择准确率衡量模型能力。但真实部署中，用户或检索系统可能提供无有效选项的选项集，此时若模型强行选择会带来下游代价。因此需要研究这种"无有效选项"场景下模型的弃答可靠性，并揭示标准准确率未能捕捉的模型可靠性维度。

Method: 使用MMLU-Pro的数学子集，删除标注的正确选项，使选项集中不存在正确答案；允许模型在剩余选项中选择或输出ABSTAIN；对无效的强制选择响应施加惩罚，构成"惩罚框架下的无有效选项MCQA"。此外提出"正确条件化分析"（correct-conditioned analysis），仅在模型原本回答正确的实例上评估其弃答表现。

Result: 实验表明，高MCQA准确率并不能完全保证弃答可靠性：即便在明确的"无有效选项感知"指令和基于惩罚的评分下，模型仍会在一部分原本回答正确的实例上产生无效的强制选择响应。

Conclusion: 惩罚框架下的无有效选项MCQA揭示了标准答案选择准确率无法捕捉的模型可靠性维度；高准确率不等于可靠的弃答能力，该设定可作为评估模型可靠性的补充手段。

Abstract: Multiple-choice question answering (MCQA) is commonly used to evaluate large language models under the assumption that one of the provided options is correct, typically using answer-selection accuracy. However, in real deployments, users or retrieval systems may provide invalid option sets in which none of the listed choices is correct, and selecting one of them may incur downstream cost. We study this setting as penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA. Using the mathematics subset of MMLU-Pro, we remove the labeled correct option, allow models to either choose a remaining option or output ABSTAIN, and penalize invalid forced-choice responses. We further introduce correct-conditioned analysis, evaluating abstention only on instances that the model originally answered correctly. Experiments show that high MCQA accuracy does not fully guarantee abstention reliability: even under explicit no-valid-option-aware instructions and penalty-based scoring, models still produce invalid forced-choice responses for a subset of originally correct instances. These results show that penalty-framed no-valid-option MCQA reveals an aspect of model reliability not captured by standard answer-selection accuracy.

</details>


### [184] [Making COMET Comparable Across Scripts: Diagnosis and Correction of Tokeniser-Induced Script Bias in Indic MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08159)
*G. L. John Salvin,Swapnil Hingmire*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文检验COMET的"脚本不变性"假设，发现目标语言书写系统本身可解释22.9%的COMET方差，将其归因于分词器，并提出归一化方案COMET-QN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COMET把翻译质量压缩为单一分数，并被跨不同文字系统的目标语言直接横向比较。这种比较隐含"脚本不变性"假设：分数不应依赖承载目标语言的书写系统。作者认为该假设从未被系统检验，而一旦不成立，跨脚本比较便失去依据。

Method: 在IndicMT Eval上将目标文本转写为拉丁字母（改变正字形式、保持内容与人工评分不变），比较原生脚本与拉丁转写下的COMET分数；把效应追溯到分词器，并用三个无标签诊断量对其度量。提出COMET-QN，将每个（语言，脚本）对的分数分布映射到共享参考分布以消除范围不可比；再用基于 parity 特征的回归器恢复部分灵敏度损失。

Result: 脚本身份可解释原生脚本COMET方差的22.9%，且所研究五种语言与人工标注者的一致性全部下降。偏差包含两类故障：不同脚本的分数落在不相容的区间；在同一脚本内，指标对译文的排序准确度也下降，且第二类故障无法用任何保序变换修复。COMET-QN精确消除第一类故障，使混合一致性由0.300升至0.399，并严格保序；parity特征回归器额外恢复17.1%的损失灵敏度，剩余部分归因于编码器，后处理无法触及。

Conclusion: 跨脚本比较COMET分数在实证上不成立。建议同时发布归一化分数、三个诊断量及其所依分词器的身份，使读者能够判断分数中有多少来自翻译质量、多少来自书写系统。

Abstract: COMET reports translation quality as a single number, and that number is routinely compared across target languages written in different scripts. Such a comparison assumes Script Invariance: the score should not depend on the writing system that carries the target. We test it on IndicMT Eval by re-encoding the target into Latin script, which changes orthographic form while holding content and human ratings fixed. Script identity then accounts for 22.9% of native-script COMET variance, and agreement with annotators falls in all five languages studied. We trace the effect to the tokeniser and measure it with three label-free diagnostics. The bias is two faults, not one. Scores from different scripts occupy incompatible ranges, and within a single script the metric orders translations less accurately. No order-preserving transform of the score can repair the second fault. The first is removed exactly by COMET-QN, which maps the score distribution of each (language, script) pair onto a shared reference. Pooled agreement with annotators rises from 0.300 to 0.399, which is what makes scores from different scripts safe to place on one axis, and every within-language ordering is provably preserved. A regressor over parity features recovers a further 17.1% of the lost sensitivity. The remainder belongs to the encoder, and no post-processing can reach it. We therefore recommend publishing the normalised score, the three diagnostics, and the identity of the tokeniser they were computed against, so that a reader can tell how much of a score reflects translation quality and how much reflects the writing system.

</details>


### [185] [STRUCTURALCOST: A controlled reading time dataset for modeling human sentence processing difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08208)
*Nina Nusbaumer,Iria de-Dios-Flores,Corentin Bel,Christophe Pallier,Guillaume Wisniewski,Benoît Crabbé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出STRUCTURALCOST数据集（475名被试、40,800条观测），在NLP规模上复现了长距离主谓依存加工成本的心理语言学发现，并检验多种语言模型对难度梯度的拟合情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长距离主谓依存解析的加工成本此前主要来自低统计功效的心理语言学实验；同时，语言模型的"认知合理性"评估缺乏大规模、可复用的行为数据支撑。作者希望以NLP规模复现该效应，并系统对比不同架构语言模型是否再现人类的加工难度。

Method: 构建自定步速阅读数据集STRUCTURALCOST：475名被试、40,800条观测，通过控制线性距离与句法嵌套分离长距离主谓依存的加工成本。随后用n-gram、状态空间模型（SSM）和Transformer等多类语言模型对阅读时间难度梯度进行拟合与比较。

Result: 成功复现"主句动词处阅读时间随依存长度增加"的效应，且该效应由句法嵌套驱动而非纯线性距离。各语言模型仅部分匹配这一渐进难度曲线，普遍低估人类的整合成本，且该差距跨架构与模型规模持续存在。

Conclusion: 语言模型捕捉了人类加工的预测性成分，但未体现工作记忆施加的完整整合成本。STRUCTURALCOST为评估语言模型的认知合理性提供了所需数据基础。

Abstract: We introduce STRUCTURALCOST, a self-paced reading dataset of 475 participants and 40,800 observations isolating the processing cost of long-distance subject-verb dependency resolution. We replicate a low-powered psycholinguistic finding at NLP scale, namely that human reading times at the main verb increase with dependency length, driven by syntactic embedding beyond linear distance. Different language models -- spanning n-gram models, SSMs, and transformers -- partially mirror this graded difficulty profile, yet underestimate the integration cost humans incur, with a gap that persists across architectures and model sizes. This suggests these models capture the predictive component of human processing but not the full integration cost that working memory imposes. STRUCTURALCOST provides data needed to drive progress toward evaluating the cognitive plausibility of language models.

</details>


### [186] [Memory Depth and Reconstructed Context Width: A Controlled Evaluation of Hierarchical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08300)
*Michael Andreev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文实验研究了长期对话记忆系统中结构深度与上下文宽度两个参数的交互作用，发现扩大上下文宽度能显著提升准确率，而增加结构深度并无单调收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话记忆已成为现代LLM系统的重要组成部分，现有架构通过话题、事件分组、层次与图结构、因果与时间关系来组织记忆。然而结构深度与上下文宽度这两个记忆参数之间的交互作用尚缺乏实验研究。

Method: 使用EverMemBench基准，评估结构深度D1-D4、核心上下文预算1,024/2,048/4,096 tokens，以及扩展至完整档案的Production与Oracle条件，考察深度与宽度参数对性能的影响。

Result: 上下文宽度从1K增至4K使准确率提升10.11-17.98个百分点，而增加深度未带来单调增益。超过8-16K后，Production性能进入平台期，但每个正确答案的token消耗持续增加；Oracle在68-71K tokens的完整档案上仍能保持质量。

Conclusion: 结果不支持逐步加深记忆结构，而支持进一步研究大规模、连贯的上下文块。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory is becoming an integral component of modern LLM systems. Proposed architectures group records by topics and events, construct hierarchies and graphs, and connect facts through causal and temporal relations. We experimentally study the interaction between two memory parameters: structural depth and the width of context supplied to the answer model. Using EverMemBench, we evaluate depths D1-D4, core budgets of 1,024/2,048/4,096 tokens, and additional Production and Oracle conditions up to the full archive. Increasing width from 1K to 4K improves Accuracy by 10.11-17.98 percentage points, whereas increasing depth provides no monotonic gain. Beyond 8-16K, Production performance reaches a plateau while tokens per correct answer continue to increase; Oracle preserves quality on full archives of 68-71K tokens. These results motivate further investigation of large, coherent context blocks instead of progressively deeper memory structures.

</details>


### [187] [Language Unalignability: Why Some Concepts Resist Cross-Cultural Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08303)
*Shu-Kai Hsieh,Da-Chen Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文质疑多语言LLM评测中隐含的"翻译同构假设"(TIA)，提出α-不可对齐性概念，并构建包含四个维度的诊断画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言LLM评测默认跨语言语义结构一致且可无损互映(TIA)，但该假设对语用标记、敬语、历时分层词汇等概念既不成立也非良定义，亟需形式化刻画这一失败。

Method: 提出usage-cloud框架，将概念表示为语境化嵌入的点集；定义α-不可对齐性为无法同时保持词汇忠实性(质心对应)与结构忠实性(局部邻域拓扑)的映射；给出行为层(FLORES-200翻译失败、LOBSTER推理分数)与机制层(九模型Yami案例中的表示-干预鸿沟RIG)证据；并操作化为四维诊断画像。

Result: FLORES-200翻译失败可由语系与资源类别预测而与文字系统无关；LOBSTER推理分数随语系变化；Yami案例中模型激活虽编码了语言规律性，但针对语言特异神经元的干预相较随机掩码无优势，即规律"可见但不可用"。

Conclusion: 将文化能力压缩为单一标量会激励"概率扁平化"；承认不可对齐类是实现尊重而非抹除文化差异的AI的前提；多语言对齐不是单一良定义目标，而是一组互不相容的投影。

Abstract: Current evaluation of multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) rests on an implicit Translation-Isomorphism Assumption (TIA): that semantic structures across languages are congruent and mutually mappable without loss of information. We argue that this assumption is not merely violated in practice, but ill-posed in principle for a typologically identifiable class of concepts, including pragmatic markers, honorifics, and diachronically stratified terms. We formalize this failure using a usage-cloud framework, representing concepts as point sets of contextualized embeddings. We define $α$-unalignability as the impossibility of any mapping that simultaneously preserves lexical faithfulness (centroid correspondence) and structural faithfulness (local neighborhood topology). We provide three layers of evidence. Behaviorally, we show that FLORES-200 translation failures are predicted by language family and resource class but not by script, and that LOBSTER reasoning scores vary by family. Mechanistically, we report a Representation-Intervention Gap (RIG) in a nine-model case study on Yami: the models' activations encode a regularity along which Yami groups with other low-resource and Austronesian languages, yet interventions on language-specific neurons show no demonstrated advantage over random masks: the regularity is visible but not usable by this intervention. Finally, we operationalize these findings into a multidimensional diagnostic profile: Cycle-Consistency, Pragmatic-Load Disagreement, Manifold-Curvature Mismatch, and RIG. We argue that collapsing cultural competence into a single scalar incentivizes "probabilistic flattening," and that recognizing the unalignable class is a precondition for AI that respects, rather than erases, cultural divergence. This suggests that multilingual alignment is not a single well-defined objective, but a set of mutually incompatible projections.

</details>


### [188] [Foresight-over-Graph: Reasoning Beyond Local Horizons for Knowledge Base Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08388)
*Yang Hong,Yajun Yang,Xin Wang,Liping Jing,Qinghua Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 FoG（Foresight-over-Graph），一种具备前瞻意识的证据检索框架，用于知识库问答，通过远到近反馈引导路径探索，在KBQA基准上取得SOTA，CWQ上Hit提升16.58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在知识密集型任务中仍存在幻觉，知识图谱可提供结构化、可解释、可更新的 factual grounding。但现有LLM引导的图推理方法在证据检索时通常采用逐跳贪心或beam式剪枝，这种局部决策是短视的：靠近源节点看似弱的证据可能在探索更深图上下文后才变得关键，导致关键分支被过早丢弃且推理链难以恢复。

Method: 提出 Foresight-over-Graph（FoG）前瞻感知证据检索框架。FoG迭代地构建与问题相关的证据子图，使用远到近（far-to-near）反馈引导路径探索，并维护一个紧凑的记忆子图以支持持续探索。

Result: 在广泛使用的KBQA基准上达到state-of-the-art性能，尤其在CWQ上Hit指标提升16.58%，同时减少LLM调用次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: FoG通过前瞻式、远到近反馈的证据检索，缓解了逐跳贪心/beam剪枝的短视问题，提升了KBQA的可靠性与检索效率；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in question answering, yet they still frequently suffer from hallucinations on knowledge-intensive tasks. Knowledge graphs (KGs) provide LLMs with structured, interpretable, and updatable factual grounding, making them a promising external knowledge source for reliable reasoning. However, existing LLM-guided graph reasoning methods typically rely on hop-wise greedy or beam-style pruning during evidence retrieval. Such local decision processes are inherently myopic: evidence that appears weak near the source may become crucial only after deeper graph context is explored, causing answer-critical branches to be discarded prematurely and making the reasoning chain difficult to recover. To address this limitation, we propose Foresight-over-Graph (FoG), a foresight-aware evidence retrieval framework for knowledge base question answering (KBQA). FoG iteratively constructs a question-relevant evidence subgraph and uses far-to-near feedback to guide path exploration, and maintains a compact memory subgraph to support continued exploration. Extensive experiments on widely used KBQA benchmarks demonstrate that FoG achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a particularly large improvement of 16.58% in Hit on CWQ, while also reducing LLM calls and token usage. Our code is available at https://github.com/yhong7/FoG .

</details>


### [189] [Knowing When Not to Answer: Cross-Domain and Multi-Turn Generalization of Latent Underspecification Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08413)
*Jerzy Kamiński,Ilya Galyukshev,Artem Kuznetsov,Danil Fedorov,Kirill Redko,Sergey Chuprin,Aidar Shumbalov,Stanislav Chumakov,Anna Kalyuzhnaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建多轮对话基准与评估框架，检验"不可回答性"探针的可迁移性及其在对话中的实用性，发现探针在同源不可回答性间迁移稳健，但跨类型迁移弱，且检测信号难以稳定转化为端任务收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常对信息不足的问题作答，且在对话中过早回答。已有研究表明不可回答性可从隐状态中线性解码，但尚不清楚不同形式的不可回答性是否共享同一表征，以及该信号在对话场景中是否有实际用处。

Method: 构建轮级标注的多轮对话基准（423 段对话，1661 个标注轮状态）及带模拟用户（可回答澄清问题）的评估框架；在 6 个数据集与 6 个开放权重 LLM 上测试不可回答性探针的迁移能力；对比单轮探针与结构内探针，并与词袋分类器、提示式整合、原始生成及真实标签门控作对照。

Result: 共享同一不可回答性来源的数据集间探针迁移稳健：数学缺信息 AUROC 0.77-0.97，篇章缺信息 SQuAD 2.0<->MuSiQue 0.77-0.90。认识论"已知的未知"探针迁移到数学较差，但该分离在词汇控制下减弱，并随层与坐标系变化，尚无定论。单轮探针零样本无法检测对话何时变为可回答，结构内探针可恢复但不及词袋分类器。校准探针门控在欠指定轮上触发精度远超随机，端任务成功率与真实标签门控相差 0.08 以内，但在四个模型上均未能稳定优于原始生成或提示式整合。

Conclusion: 不可回答性信号在同源类型间可迁移，检测本身并非瓶颈；剩余差距主要在于模型如何利用澄清信息，即检测到欠指定后难以有效整合澄清以改善端任务表现。

Abstract: Large language models routinely answer questions that cannot be answered from the information given, and in dialogue they answer before enough has been said. Unanswerability is linearly decodable from hidden states, but it is unclear which of its forms share a representation and whether the signal is useful in dialogue. We contribute a turn-labeled multi-turn benchmark (423 conversations, 1,661 labeled turn-states) and an evaluation harness with a simulated user who answers clarifying questions, and use them with six datasets and six open-weight LLMs to test how far probes for unanswerability carry. Probes transfer robustly between datasets that share a ground of unanswerability: missing information in math (AUROC 0.77-0.97) and in a passage (SQuAD 2.0<->MuSiQue, 0.77-0.90). Probes for epistemic "known-unknowns" transfer poorly to math, but this separation weakens under lexical controls and changes with layer and coordinate system, so it remains unresolved. Single-turn probes fail zero-shot to detect when a conversation becomes answerable; in-structure probes recover it, but no better than a bag-of-words classifier. A gate on the calibrated probe, with no model fine-tuning, fires on underspecified turns far more precisely than chance, and its end-task success comes within 0.08 of a gate given the true labels. Yet across four models it does not reliably beat vanilla generation or prompted consolidation. The remaining gap lies mostly in how models use a clarification, not in detection.

</details>


### [190] [Rethinking Cross-Tokenizer On-Policy Distillation: From Alignment Coverage to Supervision Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08448)
*Bingxi Hou,Guochao Jiang,Guofeng Quan,Weiqing Li,Wenfeng Feng,Guohua Liu,Yuewei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文考察跨分词器的On-Policy Distillation中扩大对齐覆盖是否提升学习效果，发现严格1:1对齐位置已保留绝大部分概率质量，紧凑监督比更广覆盖更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD中学生需基于自身生成并借助教师反馈训练，当师生分词器不同时，需要同时做序列级和词表级对齐。作者想检验：扩大这种对齐覆盖范围是否真能改善学习，从而判断是否应一味追求更大的对齐覆盖率。

Method: 在三组异构师生配对（数学推理与代码生成）上开展实验。先统计严格1:1对齐组对中学生生成token的覆盖率；在蒸馏前学生采样响应上测量共享词表在严格对齐位置保留的师生概率质量。将reverse KL限制在严格位置上学生选出的共享词表top-16子集，与全共享词表OPD及跨分词器基线对比。再在mismatch组加入span对数概率的MSE监督以获得完全覆盖，并在仅用严格损失的checkpoint上比较span梯度与严格梯度的方向一致性及幅度。

Result: 尽管词表失配显著，严格1:1组已覆盖大部分学生生成token；共享词表在严格对齐位置平均保留几乎全部师生概率质量。严格位置top-16子集上的reverse KL精度与全共享词表OPD相当，并优于所评估的跨分词器基线；而加入span的MSE监督虽实现完全监督覆盖，却降低精度。仅用严格损失训练的checkpoint上，span梯度与严格梯度方向一致性弱或为负，且相对幅度增大。

Conclusion: 应把重心从“最大化对齐覆盖”转向“优先保证监督可靠性”：严格位置上的紧凑监督可能比引入弱对齐或冲突信号的更广覆盖更有效。span梯度诊断可解释加入span监督导致的精度下降。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own generations using teacher feedback. With different tokenizers, comparing teacher and student predictions requires alignment at both sequence and vocabulary levels. In this paper, we examine whether expanding this alignment coverage improves learning. Across three heterogeneous teacher--student pairs on mathematical reasoning and code generation, strict 1:1 groups already cover most student-generated tokens despite substantial vocabulary mismatch. On responses sampled from the students before distillation, the shared vocabulary retains nearly all teacher and student probability mass at strictly aligned positions on average. Restricting reverse KL to a student-selected top-16 subset of the shared vocabulary at each strict position achieves accuracy comparable to full shared-vocabulary OPD, outperforming the evaluated cross-tokenizer baselines. Adding mean squared error supervision on span log-probabilities in mismatch groups gives complete supervision coverage, yet reduces accuracy. At checkpoints from training with only the strict loss, the span gradients show weak or negative directional agreement with the strict gradients and grow in magnitude relative to them. These diagnostics may help explain the accuracy drop from adding span supervision. Our findings motivate a shift from maximizing alignment coverage to prioritizing supervision reliability: compact supervision at strict positions can be more effective than broader coverage that introduces weakly aligned or conflicting training signals.

</details>


### [191] [Agentic AutoRAG: RAG Pipeline Optimization through Reasoning-Driven Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08452)
*Lasse B. Strand,Robert Jakob,Kevin O'Sullivan,Markus Kreft*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Agentic AutoRAG：一种带检索-生成失败归因的LLM智能体优化器，用于多目标RAG超参优化，在三个多跳QA基准上超越基线，并以更低成本匹配或超过其精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG流水线配置是涉及分块、嵌入模型、重排与生成等多个相互影响选择的昂贵超参优化问题。现有优化器（贪心搜索、贝叶斯优化）将每次试验简化为聚合分数，不建模配置为何如此表现，尽管检索到的文档块已提供失败发生在检索阶段还是生成阶段的证据。

Method: 提出Agentic AutoRAG，一个LLM智能体优化器，实现多目标RAG超参优化与检索-生成失败归因。它在语料库的冻结考卷上对候选配置打分；每次试验后，Diagnoser将每个失败问题归因于检索或生成，Proposer基于模型排名与定价知识库选择下一配置，在精度与成本间权衡以刻画帕累托前沿。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上，其LLM评委精度高于所有对比基线，并在前10次试验内即达到或超过统计基线全部30次试验的评委精度。在真实医疗语料库的成本感知模式下，中位考卷精度达77%，高于最强基线的71.5%，而每查询成本约为该基线的58%；以约22%的成本即可匹配其71.5%的精度。

Conclusion: 通过引入失败归因与智能体式配置提议，Agentic AutoRAG在精度与成本上均优于现有RAG超参优化方法，显著提升搜索效率并实现更优的精度-成本权衡。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is a widely used approach for grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge. However, configuring a pipeline is an expensive hyperparameter optimization problem over many interacting choices, from chunking and embedding model to reranking and generation. Existing optimizers, from greedy search to Bayesian optimization, reduce each trial to an aggregate score and search without modeling why a configuration performed as it did, even though the retrieved chunks already provide evidence about whether each failure occurred during retrieval or after it. We introduce Agentic AutoRAG, an LLM-agent optimizer for multi-objective RAG hyperparameter optimization with retrieval-versus-generation failure attribution. It proposes configurations scored on a frozen exam from the corpus: after each trial a Diagnoser attributes each failed question to retrieval or generation, and a Proposer, grounded in a knowledge base of model rankings and pricing, selects the next configuration, weighing accuracy against cost to trace a Pareto frontier. On three multi-hop QA benchmarks it reaches higher LLM-judge accuracy than every baseline we compare, and within its first 10 trials it matches or beats the statistical baselines' full 30-trial judge accuracy. In its cost-aware mode on a real-world healthcare corpus it reaches a median exam accuracy of 77%, above the strongest baseline's 71.5%, at about 58% of that baseline's cost per query, and it matches that 71.5% at about 22% of the cost.

</details>


### [192] [UNREAL: Unifying Retrieval and Long-Context with a Single Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08463)
*Edan Kinderman,Elad Hoffer,Yochai Blau,Brian Chmiel,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UNREAL 是一个模型原生的证据选择框架，统一了语料检索与长上下文推理：直接从冻结 LLM 内部表示生成查询并编码文本块，仅新增不到 50 万可训练参数，在 21M 块的维基百科索引及 128K/256K 长上下文任务上显著超越现有检索器-重排器方案，并降低 FLOPs 与首 token 时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理与 RAG 在不同尺度上处理证据选择（从单条长提示到整个语料库），本文探究是否存在单一模型内部机制能够跨此范围完成证据选择，从而实现统一。

Method: 提出 UNREAL：模型原生证据选择框架。它直接从冻结 LLM 的内部表示中编码文本块并推导检索查询，主干网络保持不变，仅增加少于 500K 可训练参数。

Result: 在 3B token、21M 块的维基百科索引上，四种稠密与混合 UNREAL 主干均优于 SOTA 检索器-重排器系统。最佳模型将 HotpotQA 召回率从 49.1% 提升至 73.2%，2WikiMultiHopQA 从 31.7% 提至 60.1%，MuSiQue 从 8.8% 提至 14.4%。长上下文任务中，同一选择机制在生成前去除干扰项，使 NoLiMa 在 128K 最大上下文长度下准确率从 1.0% 升至 24.83%，LV-Eval 在 256K 下 F1 从 49.97% 升至 54.66%；约 32K token 起相对全上下文推理减少 FLOPs 与首 token 时延，且上下文越长收益越大。

Conclusion: 结果表明，模型内部证据选择可作为语料检索与证据稀疏长上下文推理的共同基础。

Abstract: Long-context inference and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) handle evidence selection at vastly different scales, from a single long prompt to an entire corpus. We ask whether a single model-internal mechanism can select evidence across this range. We introduce UNifying REtrieval And Long-Context with a Single Model (UNREAL), a model-native evidence selection framework to span corpus retrieval and long-context inference. UNREAL encodes chunks and derives retrieval queries directly from the frozen LLM's internal representations. It adds fewer than 500K trainable parameters and leaves the backbone unchanged. On a 3B-token, 21M-chunk Wikipedia index, all four dense and hybrid UNREAL backbones outperform state-of-the-art retriever-reranker systems. The best model raises recall from 49.1% to 73.2% on HotpotQA, from 31.7% to 60.1% on 2WikiMultiHopQA, and from 8.8% to 14.4% on MuSiQue. Applied to long-context tasks, the same selection mechanism removes distractors before generation, raising NoLiMa accuracy from 1.0% to 24.83% at its maximum context length of 128K tokens, and LV-Eval's F1 score from 49.97% to 54.66% at 256K. UNREAL also reduces FLOPs and time-to-first-token relative to full-context inference from roughly 32K tokens onward, with larger gains as context grows. Together, these results establish model-internal evidence selection as a common foundation for corpus retrieval and evidence-sparse long-context inference.

</details>


### [193] [Language-model ratings of depression reflect the rater more than the patient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08501)
*Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究预注册880个语言模型评分者（11个开源模型×提示与计分方式组合），对189份访谈按PHQ-8进行抑郁筛查评分。模型选择解释30.0%的总症状分方差，被试稳定差异仅10.5%；两个AUC≥0.70的随机评分者在40%被试上筛查结论不一致。用86份新访谈的锁定分析重现了主要结果；用40名有标签被试做探索性校准可将准确率从约60%提升至75%、分歧减半，但仍有五分之一被试结论不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症缺乏诊断性血液检测，语言模型被视为可提供不知疲倦且一致的评估工具。作者由此追问一个核心问题：即使评分者各自准确，是否仍会在个体层面产生分歧？研究意在检验语言模型作为抑郁筛查评分者的评分者间可靠性及其对个体决策的影响。

Method: 预注册880个语言模型评分者，交叉11个开源模型与多种提示和计分选择，应用于189份访谈，并对照八条目患者健康问卷（PHQ-8）。通过方差分解量化模型选择与被试稳定差异对总分方差的贡献；以AUC≥0.70的评分者配对计算筛查决策分歧率。随后对86份新访谈进行锁定分析以验证预注册发现，并用40名有标签被试进行探索性重校准。

Result: 模型选择解释总分方差的30.0%，被试稳定差异解释10.5%。两个AUC≥0.70的随机评分者平均在40%的被试上筛查决策不一致。平均过度评分决定被标记人数，但能力相当的评分者约每五名被试中就有一名给出不同结论。锁定分析在86份新访谈中重现了主要预注册结果。探索性重校准将准确率从约60%提高到75%，分歧减半，但仍有五分之一被试被不同地判定。

Conclusion: 语言模型评分者虽可达到可接受的总体准确度，但在个体层面的筛查决策上存在显著分歧；校准能修复相当一部分评分者依赖性、提高准确率并减少分歧，却无法确保对个体的判定一致。因此，仅靠准确率指标不足以支撑语言模型用于个体化抑郁筛查。

Abstract: Depression has no diagnostic blood test. Language models promise tireless, consistent assessment, but can accurate raters disagree about individuals? We pre-registered 880 language-model raters, crossing 11 open models with prompting and scoring choices, and applied them to 189 interviews against the eight-item Patient Health Questionnaire. Model choice explained 30.0% of summed-symptom score variance, stable participant differences 10.5%. Two randomly drawn raters with area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) >= 0.70 disagreed on screening decisions for 40% of participants, on average. Average over-rating governed how many were flagged, yet equal-capacity raters chose differently for about one participant in five. A locked analysis of 86 new interviews reproduced the main pre-registered findings. Exploratory recalibration with 40 labelled participants raised accuracy from about 60% to 75% and halved disagreement, leaving one participant in five decided differently. Calibration repaired much of the rater dependence without securing agreement about individuals.

</details>


### [194] [Wiki-Talkie: Multilingual Benchmarking of Persona-Based Agents on Real-World Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08513)
*Dennis Fucci,Andrea Bacciu,Dong Liu,Weronika Łajewska,Saab Mansour*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出多语言真实对话数据集 Wiki-Talkie，用于评估 LLM 智能体模拟人类交互的行为保真度，发现交互行为示例优于显式人格信息，且模型存在向善性与正性偏差，跨语言稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地作为自主智能体部署于社交环境，需要其忠实模拟人类交互。核心在于将智能体锚定在真实用户人格上，但现有数据集依赖虚构人格、语言种类少，缺乏跨人群评估行为保真度的实证基础。

Method: 构建 Wiki-Talkie：来自 Wikipedia 讨论页的真实对话多语言数据集，覆盖五大语言、两大语系（日耳曼语系：德语、英语；罗曼语系：西班牙语、法语、意大利语），并配以来自真实用户社群的人格信息，包含社会人口属性、自我描述与基于行为的互动特质。基于该数据集，在下一轮生成任务上评估不同人格条件化策略下的智能体交互行为，判断智能体能否在集体层面再现人类讨论中的分布式行为模式。

Result: 用户的评论历史（体现交互行为）始终优于显式人格信息。模型系统性地少产生负面或极端情绪，同时过量产生引用与建议，表现出向善性与正性偏差。这些模式在不同语言间稳健成立，跨语言差异较小。

Conclusion: 真实行为示例是人格锚定的关键，显式人格描述效果有限；LLM 在社交交互模拟中存在系统性情绪与行为偏差，且该偏差跨语言普遍存在，需在行为保真度评估中加以关注。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in social environments, making it critical to study their ability to faithfully simulate human interactions. Central to this is grounding agents in realistic user personas, yet existing datasets rely on fictional personas and are limited to a handful of languages, lacking the empirical grounding necessary to evaluate behavioral fidelity across diverse populations. We introduce Wiki-Talkie, a multilingual dataset of real-world conversations from Wikipedia Talk pages across five languages spanning two language families: Germanic (German, English) and Romance (Spanish, French, Italian), paired with personas derived from real user communities and encompassing sociodemographic attributes, self-descriptions, and behaviorally grounded interaction traits. Using Wiki-Talkie, we evaluate agent interactional behavior on a next-turn generation task across various persona conditioning strategies. Our evaluation assesses whether agents collectively reproduce the distributional behavioral patterns observed in human discussions. Results show that user's comment history exemplifying interaction behavior consistently outperforms explicit persona information. In addition, models systematically underproduce negative or extreme sentiments, while over producing references and suggestions, revealing biases toward agreeableness and positivity. Crucially, these patterns hold robustly across languages, with small cross-lingual differences.

</details>


### [195] [How High Is 0.6? Floors, Ceilings, and Headroom in Interpretability Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08544)
*Pranjal Garg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出以“地板”（简单输入的可预测性）和“天花板”（完整输入的可预测性）为参照解读探针分数，其差值“余量”（headroom）表示模型超出简单输入的计算能力，并证明余量消失的两种机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探针是可解释性的主力工具，但探针分数缺乏固定含义：R²=0.6 可能只是反映输入本身已提供的信息，且同一分数在不同数据上含义不同，因此需要一个标准化参照框架来判断模型是否真正“计算”了目标变量。

Method: 将每个探针分数对照两个参考点：地板（declared set of simple inputs 已能预测的量）与天花板（full input 能预测的量）；以两者之差（headroom）作为探针可证明模型超出简单输入的计算范围。理论上证明余量消失的两种情形：目标不再依赖模型必须推断的隐藏变量，或输入不再泄露该变量。实验上在用于 in-context meta-analysis 的 transformer 上验证（需推断研究间隐藏异质性以正确加权，且两个参考点已知），并分析 scGPT 单细胞基础模型，以及重新审视四个有影响力的 LLM 探针研究（地理、Othello 棋盘状态、真伪、用户人口统计）。

Result: 在分布偏移下，探针分数下降、预测误差上升 12–15 倍，但模型恢复的余量份额相近，说明损失来自数据信息而非表征。scGPT 仅部分编码生物学变异。四个 LLM 探针研究中，对照仅由输入文本计算的地板，部分结论成立，另一些则很大程度上可由文本自身解释。

Conclusion: 探针分数应相对于地板与天花板解读，余量刻画了模型超出简单输入的真实计算；余量可在目标依赖隐藏变量消失或输入不再揭示该变量时归零。该框架可将“数据丢失信息”与“表征丢失信息”区分开，并为 LLM 探针结论提供更严格的评估基准。

Abstract: Probes are the workhorse of interpretability. If a model's hidden states predict a variable, the model is said to represent it. But a probe score has no fixed meaning. An $R^2$ of 0.6 may only reflect what the input already gives away, and the same score can mean different things on different data. We propose reading every probe score against two reference points: a floor, what a declared set of simple inputs already predicts, and a ceiling, what the full input can predict. The gap between them, the headroom, is the range in which a probe can show that a model computes something beyond the simple inputs. We prove that headroom vanishes in two ways: the target stops depending on a hidden variable the model must infer, or the input stops revealing it. We test this on transformers trained for in-context meta-analysis, which must infer the hidden heterogeneity between studies to weight them correctly, and where both reference points are known. Under distribution shift, probe scores fall and prediction error rises $12$--$15\times$, yet the model recovers a similar share of the headroom, indicating that the data lost information, not the representation. We then analyze the real models. The single-cell foundation model scGPT encodes biological variability only partially. We also revisit four influential LLM probing studies, which claim that models represent geography, the state of an Othello board, truth, and the demographics of their users. Against a floor computed from the input text alone, some of these claims hold, while others are largely explained by the text itself.

</details>


### [196] [Latent space bias directions in LLMs capture confidence, not fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08559)
*Stephanie Buttigieg,Maeve Madigan,Parameswaran Kamalaruban,Stuart Burrell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析激活引导（activation steering）中线性去偏方向的实际含义，发现该方向主要编码模型置信度而非偏差表征；沿此方向引导会降低模型置信度、促使模型拒答，从而"看似"降低偏差并改善公平性指标，因此基于引导的去偏结果需谨慎解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导作为一种轻量级推理期去偏技术广受关注，但已有工作报告其引导向量泛化性差：会带来非预期的性能副作用，且难以迁移到新数据集。作者希望弄清用于激活引导的去偏方向究竟编码了什么，以解释其性能不稳定的原因。

Method: 通过对比"反偏见提示"与"有偏见提示"的激活，得到线性去偏方向；将该方向作为引导干预，在偏见基准与通用知识基准上评估；并分析该方向在激活空间中所编码的主要成分，考察偏置与反偏置提示在隐藏空间中的主导区分因素。

Result: 该方向主要由模型置信度主导，指向激活空间中从高概率到低概率词元的区域，而非偏差的有意义表征；沿此方向引导确实降低测得的偏差，但这是降低模型置信度的结果：在问答基准上引导会驱使模型拒答，其副作用是公平性指标改善。模型置信度是隐藏空间中区分偏置与反偏置提示的主导因素。

Conclusion: 将线性偏差表征与模型置信度解耦是困难的；引导式去偏并非纠正模型底层偏好，而是通过使模型更不自信（即使在无关偏差的任务上）来"减少"偏差，因此此类去偏结果应谨慎解读。

Abstract: Activation steering has gained popularity as a lightweight inference-time debiasing technique for large language models. However, prior work reports that steering vectors generalise poorly, with unintended effects on model performance and limited transfer to new datasets. Our work analyses what the debiasing direction used for activation steering actually encodes, in order to shed light on its inconsistent performance. We study the linear debiasing direction obtained by contrasting the activations of anti-biased and biased prompts, and evaluate it as a steering intervention across bias and general knowledge benchmarks. We find that this direction is dominated by model confidence, pointing from regions of high to low-probability tokens in activation space rather than encoding a meaningful representation of model bias. Steering along it does reduce measured bias, but this is a consequence of reducing model confidence: on QA benchmarks we find that this steering drives the model to abstain from answering, with a side effect of improving fairness metrics. Our experiments show that model confidence is the dominant separating factor between biased and anti-biased prompts in hidden space, indicating that isolating a linear representation of bias which is disentangled from model confidence is difficult and steering-based debiasing results should be interpreted with care. In short, steering appears to reduce bias, not by correcting the model's underlying preferences, but by making it less confident, even on tasks unrelated to bias.

</details>


### [197] [Incidental information contaminates patient notes and disrupts clinical reasoning in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08585)
*Krithik Vishwanath,Brandon Ye,Anton Alyakin,John E. Markert,Aaron Hsieh,Michał Mańkowski,Eric K. Oermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了大语言模型在临床文档生成与临床推理中对"偶发信息"(寒暄闲聊、背景语音)的敏感性:前沿模型在35%的笔记中插入闲聊内容,背景语音在48.2%的转录中泄漏,并据此提出"临床推理与干扰的双重编码假说"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益被用于环境文档记录与临床推理,而这两类应用共享一个潜在失效模式:对患者就诊过程之外偶发信息的敏感性。作者希望量化这种敏感性的实际影响,并检验其对临床安全性的风险。

Method: 研究分两部分。其一,分析576段患者-临床医生对话,测量前沿模型将闲聊内容写入笔记的比例、笔记质量评分变化,以及是否将闲聊内容误归属或用于临床判断。其二,构建57段模拟录制的问诊音频,在-10 dB下混入另一患者就诊的背景语音,检测转录泄漏率及四个开放权重模型生成的后续笔记污染率。在此基础上提出并初步验证"临床推理与干扰双重编码"假说。

Result: 在576段对话中,前沿模型在35%的笔记里插入了闲聊内容,而笔记质量均值在五分制量表上变化不超过0.20分;3.7%的前沿模型笔记出现闲聊内容误归属或被临床化使用。在57段模拟音频中,-10 dB的背景语音泄漏进48.2%的转录文本,四个开放权重模型生成的后续笔记中有5.3%被检出污染。初步证据表明,LLM中易受偶发信息干扰的组件同时也支撑临床推理。

Conclusion: 偶发信息可导致临床笔记内容污染,且该失效模式难以通过常规质量评分察觉。作者主张在临床部署前应评估模型对偶发信息的抵抗力,并设计既能防止污染又能保留临床推理能力的防护措施,同时以双重编码假说为后续机制研究提供方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly relied upon to support ambient documentation and clinical reasoning. Here we examine the impact of a failure mode shared between these two applications by assessing their sensitivity to information incidental to the patient encounter. In 576 patient-clinician dialogues, we found that frontier models inserted small-talk exchanges into 35% of notes, while mean quality scores changed by at most 0.20 points on five-point scales. In 3.7% of frontier notes, models misattributed the asides or used them clinically. In 57 mock recorded consultations, background speech from a separate patient encounter at -10 dB leaked into 48.2% of transcripts, with contamination detected in 5.3% of downstream notes generated by four open-weight models. We propose a dual encoding hypothesis of clinical reasoning and distraction in LLMs, with preliminary evidence that LLM components associated with disruption by incidental information also support clinical reasoning. These findings support evaluating resistance to incidental information before clinical use, with safeguards that prevent contamination while preserving clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [198] [Generative AI translations in high-stakes emergency messaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08601)
*Nune Ayvazan,Anthony Pym,Yu Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨生成式AI在紧急信息（如地震指令）翻译中的应用，发现领域特定提示词可显著提升译文可理解性与可操作性，但译文仍需人工审核以确保准确性与伦理责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急信息（如极端天气报告、地震指令）翻译错误可能导致严重后果，因此通常不建议使用机器翻译或生成式AI。但若AI能节省初译时间，可将资源投入审校与授权流程，并覆盖更多目标语言。

Method: 开展实验，将一篇地震指令文本从英语翻译为中文和西班牙语，使用生成式AI并测试不同提示词策略，重点考察领域特定提示词对译文质量的影响。

Result: 使用领域特定提示词（discourse-specific prompts）可显著提升译文的可理解性（understandability）与可操作性（actionability）；但译员对AI译文仍缺乏信任。

Conclusion: 人工审核仍然必要：既用于检测错误，也出于伦理要求——必须有人对紧急信息中的错误或延误承担责任。

Abstract: Emergency messaging such as extreme-weather reports and earthquake instructions can involve high stakes, to the extent that translation errors can lead to tragic consequences. The use of machine translation or generative artificial intelligence might therefore not be recommended. On the other hand, time savings in the initial translation can allow greater investments of resources in revision and authorization processes, as well as a wider range of target languages. An experiment with generative AI translations of an earthquake instruction text from English into Chinese and Spanish shows that use of discourse-specific prompts can considerably improve understandability and actionability, although the translations may still not be trusted by translators. Human revision is still required, not only to detect errors but also because of the ethical need for someone to take responsibility for any errors or delays in such messaging.

</details>


### [199] [InterCorrect: Intersection-Aware Correction of Demographic Model Merging for Fair ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08604)
*Ashley E. Bravo-Bravo,Yuchen Zhang,Haralambos Mouratidis,Ravi Shekhar,Monorama Swain*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究面向公平语音大模型ASR的人口统计学感知模型合并方法，通过仅微调connector并对子群体适配的connector进行合并，再对关键交叉人口统计对施加校正向量，在Fair-Speech上整体WER从7.38%降至5.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统在不同人口统计群体上表现不均衡，尤其对同时属于多个人口统计群体的说话人而言，误差难以解决，因此需要一种兼顾整体性能与子群体公平性的建模方案。

Method: 基于SLAM-ASR模型，仅微调connector；在人口统计特定子集上获得子群体适配的connector并合并为全局模型；利用子群体WER与任务向量冲突识别关键交叉人口统计对，再对全局合并模型施加交叉特定校正向量。

Result: 在Fair-Speech上，全局人口统计合并相比基模型改善整体WER，交叉校正对多种合并策略带来额外收益；其中TIES结合基于WER的校正取得最佳整体WER，从7.38%降至5.13%。子群体与差异分析显示方法在各人口统计轴上均有提升，同时表明平均WER更低并不总意味着子群体差异更小。

Conclusion: 人口统计感知的模型合并与交叉校正可同时提升Speech-LLM ASR整体性能并改善多个人口统计轴表现，但公平性评估不能仅依赖平均WER，需结合子群体与差异指标。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) systems often show uneven performance across demographic groups, and errors can be especially difficult to address for speakers belonging to multiple demographic groups. This work studies demographic-aware model merging for fair Speech-LLM-based ASR. Starting from a SLAM-ASR-based model, we fine-tune only the connector on demographic-specific subsets and merge the resulting subgroup-adapted connectors into a global model. We then identify critical cross-axis demographic pairs using subgroup WER and task-vector conflict, and apply intersection-specific correction vectors to the global merged model. Experiments on Fair-Speech show that global demographic merging improves overall WER over the base model, while intersection correction provides additional gains for several merging strategies. In particular, TIES with WER-based correction achieves the best overall WER, reducing it from 7.38\% to 5.13\%. Subgroup and disparity analyses further show that the proposed approach improves performance across demographic axes, while highlighting that lower average WER does not always imply reduced subgroup disparity.

</details>


### [200] [Towards In-Parameter Memory Augmentation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08630)
*Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Jiaxin Bai,Yisen Gao,Hong Ting Tsang,Wuganjing Song,Huihao Jing,Yufei Li,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该综述聚焦"参数化记忆"，梳理在推理时以参数对象承载可复用记忆并接入前向计算的LLM增强方法，提出参数放置位置与参数获取时机两条正交分类轴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM及智能体需融入预训练后获得的知识（领域事实、用户偏好、文档、交互经验）。上下文学习（ICL）灵活，但占用上下文容量，且离散化编码成本随上下文长度增长、需重复计算。参数化记忆作为互补载体，可将可复用记忆写入参数、适配器等对象并在推理时组合进前向计算。

Method: 以综述形式界定参数化记忆的边界，并采用两条正交分类轴组织现有工作：(1) 参数放置（Parameter Placement），含Embedding、Attention、FFN层及混合（Hybrid，使用两层及以上）；(2) 参数获取时机（Parameter Acquisition Time），区分部署期间获取（online）与部署前获取（offline）。据此对方法进行分类、比较与边界澄清。

Result: 构建出基于"参数放置×参数获取时机"的方法分类体系，明确各类方法的定位与差异，并澄清参数化记忆与ICL等相关概念的边界。

Conclusion: 参数化记忆是部署阶段增强LLM的可行补充路径；未来开放方向包括参数记忆间的干扰、安全性、与ICL的协同设计以及递归自我改进。

Abstract: Recently Large Language Models (LLMs) and LLM-based agents increasingly need to incorporate knowledge acquired after pretraining, e.g., domain facts, user preferences, documents, and interaction experience. In-context learning (ICL) and ICL-based agent harness remain flexible, but they consume context capacity and incur repeated discretized encoding cost that grows with context length. \textbf{In-parameter memory} offers a complementary substrate: reusable memory information is represented in model parameters, adapters, or other parameter-like objects that are composed into the forward pass at inference time. This survey focuses on methods that augment LLMs with such parametric memory at deployment: a memory-bearing parameter object is plugged into the forward pass during inference, whether it is acquired before or during deployment. We organize the landscape with two orthogonal axes: \textbf{Parameter Placement}, which includes Embedding, Attention, FFN layers, or Hybrid when two or more layers are used; and \textbf{Parameter Acquisition Time}, which distinguishes methods whose memory object is acquired during deployment (online) from those acquired before it (offline). We clarify boundaries, conduct comparisons, and discuss open directions in interference, safety, co-design with ICL, and recursive self-improvement.

</details>


### [201] [Evidence-Bound Reasoning: Neuro-Semantic Verification of Biomedical AI in Glioblastoma Radiogenomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08660)
*Mariya Miteva,Maria Nisheva-Pavlova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出神经语义验证框架，将影像组学测量转化为可寻址证据记录与可机器校验的声明，实现与预测性能解耦的可验证性评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学AI常能生成看似合理的解释，却无法可靠核验每条陈述是否有患者特异性证据支持，即缺乏可验证性（verifiability）。作者希望将"可验证性"从预测性能中独立出来，进行工程化设计与评估。

Method: 将UPenn-GBM影像组学与独立多中心队列中经标准化MRI及专家验证分割的从头CaPTk提取结果对齐；共享特征空间包含T1、T1GD、T2、FLAIR四个序列、三个肿瘤区域的1,728个特征；以611例UPenn病例推导参考定义的语义状态。评估内容包括跨队列可迁移性、模型关联溯源、确定性验证、受控预测退化，以及LLM声明抽取预试验；MGMT预测仅作为迁移压力测试。

Result: 语义状态一致性中位数为0.786（加权kappa 0.709），形态学特征达0.918，强度特征仅0.252。外部证据账本含331例患者的1,655条模型关联记录。在6,620条声明污染基准中，验证器达到100%精确集合准确率。24例预试验中，GPT-5.6 Sol复现72/72预设原子声明，冻结验证器恢复24/24预期条件。受控退化时ROC AUC由0.899降至0.500，而验证准确率保持1.000。外部MGMT区分能力弱（ROC AUC 0.543）。

Conclusion: 可验证性可独立于预测性能被工程化与评估；LLM可负责组织解释结构，而最终的证据一致性检查仍应由确定性方法完成。

Abstract: Background: Biomedical AI can generate plausible explanations without reliably verifying whether each statement is supported by patient-specific evidence. We developed a neuro-semantic verification framework that converts radiomic measurements into addressable evidence records and machine-checkable claims. Methods: UPenn-GBM radiomics were aligned with de novo CaPTk extraction from standardized MRI and expert-validated segmentations in an independent multicenter cohort. The shared space comprised 1,728 features from T1, T1GD, T2, and FLAIR MRI across three tumor regions. Reference-defined semantic states were derived from 611 UPenn cases. We evaluated cross-cohort transportability, model-linked provenance, deterministic verification, controlled predictive degradation, and an LLM claim-extraction pilot; MGMT prediction served only as a transport stress test. Results: Median semantic-state agreement was 0.786 (weighted kappa 0.709), ranging from 0.918 for morphologic to 0.252 for intensity features. The external evidence ledger contained 1,655 model-linked records for 331 patients. The verifier achieved 100% exact-set accuracy in a 6,620-claim corruption benchmark. In a 24-case pilot, GPT-5.6 Sol reproduced 72/72 prespecified atomic claims, and the frozen verifier recovered 24/24 expected conditions. During controlled degradation, ROC AUC declined from 0.899 to 0.500 while verification accuracy remained 1.000. External MGMT discrimination was weak (ROC AUC 0.543). Conclusions: Verifiability can be engineered and evaluated independently of predictive performance. LLMs may structure explanations, while final evidence-consistency checking remains deterministic.

</details>


### [202] [Same-Number Citation Swaps: Stress-Testing Jev as a Financial Evidence Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08675)
*Chuhong Xu,Bo Su,Ziyao Chen,Ruiyang Xu,Shimeng Dai,Xinyu Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该文提出一种受控评估方法，用概率型验证器（Jev）核查LLM财务计算引用的"角色"支持度，在同数不同角色的引用对比下，考察其在数值匹配固定时还能区分什么。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 财务报告在多个期间、指标和会计科目间重复出现相同数值，LLM生成的计算可能在数值上正确，却把数字归到了错误的财务角色（科目/期间/指标）。仅靠数值比对无法发现此类"引对数字、引错角色"的问题，因此需要评估概率证据验证在数值匹配之外还能提供什么。

Method: 以Jev作为"来源支持验证器"，检验GPT-4.1-mini的计算轨迹；建立带符号数字-指针的基线；为隔离角色识别问题，固定操作数与算术运算，将引用在同数值单元格之间移动，并保留表达等价事实的对照项；进一步在36个新来源页面上构造后续评估，由非作者评审员标注。

Result: 带符号数字-指针基线即可解释大部分相对精确引文检查的恢复效果；对比显示既存在通过验证的错误角色引用，也存在被拒的合法替代引用。显式列标签能改善部分错误角色判定，但同时降低对某些等价证据的支持，形成"检出角色错误"与"保留合法引用"之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 贡献在于一套受控评估，揭示数值匹配置固定时概率型财务验证器实际区分的对象；使LLM财务助手的数值正确性、引用角色支持度与接受结果可分别评估。

Abstract: Financial reports repeat values across periods, metrics and accounting lines, allowing an LLM-generated calculation to be numerically correct while citing the wrong financial role. We evaluate what probabilistic evidence verification adds beyond number matching using Jev as a source-support verifier for GPT-4.1-mini calculation traces. A signed-number-at-pointer baseline explains most recovery over exact quotation checks. To isolate the remaining role-recognition problem, we hold operands and arithmetic fixed, move citations between same-number cells, and retain controls that express equivalent facts. These contrasts reveal both wrong-role citations that pass and valid alternative citations that are withheld. Explicit column labels improve selected wrong-role decisions while also lowering support for some equivalent evidence. A constructed follow-up on 36 new source pages, labeled by a non-author reviewer, extends this evaluation and exposes the same tradeoff between detecting role errors and retaining valid citations. The contribution is a controlled evaluation that identifies what a probabilistic financial verifier distinguishes when numerical matching is held fixed. For LLM-based financial assistants, it makes numerical correctness, cited-role support and acceptance outcomes separately assessable.

</details>


### [203] [Agreement Is Not Validity: Cross-Model LLM Consensus in Diagnosing Student Failure Modes in K-12 Math Tutoring Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08703)
*Clayton Cohn,Joyce Fonteles,Kirk Vanacore,Gianni Mazza,Candida Crawford,Tom Hooper,Gautam Biswas,Rene Kizilcec*

Main category: cs.CL
