TL;DR: 该研究检验了LLM对K-12数学辅导对话中五类学生失败模式分类的效度，发现人机一致性仅中等，而模型间一致性远高，说明模型共识可能造成"正确性的假象"，不能替代独立的效度证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12数学辅导对话蕴含丰富的学生问题解决过程与困难来源信息，学习分析研究日益依赖大语言模型从对话中提取此类信息，用于知识追踪、行为建模和推理错误诊断等下游任务。然而，模型生成解释的效度仍缺乏充分理解，尤其"多模型共识是否等于有效解读"这一假设尚未被检验。

Method: 探索性研究。基于一套可操作的诊断编码手册，界定五类学生失败模式：不确定性（uncertainty）、错误归因（misattribution）、算子选择（operator selection）、概念缺口（conceptual gap）、程序失误（procedural slip）。在数学辅导对话数据上，比较人类与LLM的分类一致性，并计算多个模型之间的一致性（kappa与Krippendorff's alpha）。

Result: 各模型的人机一致性为中等水平（kappa = .524–.597）；模型间一致性显著更高（kappa = .755–.781；alpha = .769）。

Conclusion: 跨模型一致性可能制造出误导性的"正确性外观"，挑战了"LLM共识可作为有效学习者解读证据"的假设。对学习分析的启示是：可扩展的标注只有在所推断的构念本身有效时才有用，模型共识不能替代对该效度的独立证据。

Abstract: In K-12 mathematics tutoring, student-tutor dialogue provides rich evidence of learners' problem-solving processes and sources of difficulty. Learning analytics research increasingly relies on large language models (LLMs) to extract such information from dialogue for a variety of downstream tasks, including knowledge tracing, behavioral modeling, and diagnosis of student reasoning errors. However, the validity of these model-generated interpretations remains insufficiently understood. In this exploratory study, we examine the validity of LLM classifications of five student failure modes in mathematics tutoring dialogue using an operational diagnostic codebook: uncertainty, misattribution, operator selection, conceptual gap, and procedural slip. Across models, human-LLM agreement was moderate (kappa = .524-.597), while cross-model agreement was substantially higher (kappa = .755-.781; alpha = .769). These findings show that cross-model agreement can create a misleading appearance of correctness, challenging the assumption that consensus among LLMs constitutes evidence of valid learner interpretation. For learning analytics, the implication is clear: scalable labeling is useful only if the inferred constructs are valid, and model consensus cannot substitute for independent evidence of that validity.

</details>


### [204] [When Forgetting is not Catastrophic: On the Mechanics of Spurious Forgetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08718)
*Vedant Palit,Florent Draye,Nicolas Zucchet,Zhijing Jin,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型微调中的"伪遗忘"现象，通过一个最小联想记忆模型揭示遗忘由两部分构成：一是共享且可逆的访问丧失，二是单条事实的缓慢侵蚀，只有后者才是灾难性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调时语言模型看似遗忘的知识往往仍被存储且可恢复，甚至出现"自我撤销"的遗忘动态。作者希望理解这种遗忘在何种条件下不构成灾难性遗忘。

Method: 构建一个最小联想记忆模型，包含三个要素：具有共享结构的键、集中的新值、以及网络中的归一化；并在此分析基础上，在合成数据训练的 Transformer 及预训练语言模型上进行验证（减去共同位移、从每个权重更新中移除单一方向）。

Result: 微调将所有旧表示沿一个共同方向移动，掩盖旧事实但保留其相对几何结构；归一化在新事实学完后撤回该位移，而事实特定的变化不断累积并导致侵蚀。减去共同位移可消除合成数据 Transformer 中的崩塌，移除单一方向可在预训练语言模型中恢复旧事实。

Conclusion: 遗忘是共享、可逆的访问丧失与单条事实缓慢侵蚀的叠加，仅后者是灾难性的；二者孰占主导取决于新数据是让旧记忆"一起移动"还是"彼此分离"。

Abstract: Knowledge that a language model appears to forget during finetuning often remains stored and can be recovered, a phenomenon called spurious forgetting. Finetuning on new facts can even produce forgetting that undoes itself: recall of the old facts collapses, recovers as training continues on new facts alone, and only then erodes for good. We seek to understand when such forgetting is not catastrophic. A minimal associative memory reproduces these dynamics with three ingredients: keys with shared structure, concentrated new values, and normalization in the network. Finetuning moves all old representations along a common direction, hiding the old facts while preserving their relative geometry; normalization withdraws this shift once the new facts are learned, whereas fact-specific changes accumulate and cause the erosion. Moreover, subtracting the common shift eliminates the collapse in a Transformer trained on synthetic data, and removing a single direction from each weight update restores old facts in a pretrained language model. Forgetting thus combines a shared, reversible loss of access with a slow erosion of individual facts, and only the second is catastrophic. Which one dominates depends on whether the new data move old memories together or apart.

</details>


### [205] [Holdout Best-of-N: Unbiased Evaluation and Its Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08719)
*Shrey Shah,Yinheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究 Best-of-N 胜者选择中"复用评分"导致的期望奖励高估问题，给出无偏估计的充要条件（J<K）、极小极大风险率，并提出可在 O(MK log M) 内计算的循环平均（cyclic）估计器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用同一批评分挑选 Best-of-N 胜者后再用这批分数估计期望奖励，会因胜者选择偏差（winner's curse）系统性高估奖励。论文旨在明确：在何种条件下可基于固定评分矩阵构造精确无偏估计，并刻画其最优风险与选择器深度 K 的关系。

Method: 设定：N 个候选中进行选择，策略用 J 个"新鲜"评分选择，评估基于每个候选 K 个独立评分的固定矩阵，池大小 M≥N≥2。先刻画"仅依赖该矩阵的单估计量对所有独立稳定评分分布族均精确无偏"的充要条件；在独立同方差高斯评分下推导无偏极小极大风险阶及常数，并对比允许偏差的估计量；对 N=2 给出已知方差下的最小方差无偏估计量（MVUE）与尖锐渐近无偏极小极大常数；提出对子集与并列做循环平均的估计器并分析复杂度与风险。

Result: 无偏估计存在的充要条件为 J<K（对所有 M≥N≥2）；J=K-1 时选择器随 K 增大而加深。高斯同方差、固定 M≥N≥2 下，无偏极小极大风险阶为 σ²/√K，Holdout 可达；允许偏差后提升至 σ²/K。N=2 时，已知方差下给出 MVUE，尖锐渐近无偏极小极大常数为 1/(π√2)，Holdout 无需知道方差即可达到。循环平均可在 O(MK log M) 内计算；固定选择器深度下，有界评分的循环评估风险关于池大小为一致的 O(K⁻¹)。不可能性结论针对固定矩阵：只需额外一个新胜者评分即可对 all-K 策略实现无偏评估。

Conclusion: 评分复用与无偏评估之间存在本质张力：仅当评估所用新鲜评分少于选择所用评分（J<K）时，单一矩阵估计量才可能精确无偏；Holdout 在高斯设定下达到最优速率与常数，循环平均估计器兼具计算可行性与一致风险界。

Abstract: Reusing the scores that select a Best-of-$N$ winner can overstate its expected reward. We study evaluation from a fixed matrix of $K$ independent scores per candidate for a policy that selects using $J$ fresh scores. A single estimator based only on this matrix is exactly unbiased for expected judge reward under every independent, stable collection of candidate-specific score laws if and only if $J<K$, for every pool size $M\ge N\ge2$. At $J=K-1$, the selector deepens as $K$ grows. For independent Gaussian scores with common variance and fixed $M\ge N\ge2$, the unbiased minimax risk in this regime is of order $σ^2/\sqrt K$, attained by Holdout; allowing bias improves the rate to $σ^2/K$. For two candidates, we derive the minimum-variance unbiased estimator at known variance and the sharp asymptotic unbiased minimax constant $1/(π\sqrt2)$, which Holdout attains without knowing the variance. The cyclic average over subsets and ties can be computed in $O(MK\log M)$ operations. At fixed selector depth, cyclic evaluation of bounded scores has $O(K^{-1})$ risk uniformly in pool size. The impossibility result concerns the fixed matrix: one additional fresh winner score permits unbiased evaluation of the all-$K$ policy.

</details>


### [206] [Denoising Hierarchical Representations: Joint Continuous Diffusion for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08738)
*Mathias Ollu,Nikos Komodakis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出分层连续扩散语言模型（H-CDLM），通过并行扩散多粒度模态（token 与其聚类簇）以极小的计算与参数开销显著提升连续扩散语言模型性能，实例化为 H-CoBit 与 H-FLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散与流匹配语言模型虽在 token 表示和扩散空间设计上取得进展，但仍有进一步提升空间，且希望以低计算、低参数开销增强生成质量。

Method: 借鉴离散扩散语言模型与图像扩散中的联合扩散思想，在连续扩散框架下并行扩散多种语义粒度模态：即 token 本身，以及通过聚类预训练 token 嵌入得到的粗粒度簇；并允许各模态使用独立的采样器与调度以增强模态间交互。将该通用框架应用于 CoBit 得到 H-CoBit，应用于流匹配模型 FLM 得到 H-FLM。

Result: 在数据集熵条件下，H-CoBit 提升 MAUVE，并在 LM1B 上 GenPPL 达 49.4、OWT 上达 50.4，分别较基线提升 24.2 与 20.7 点，超过同规模离散扩散语言模型；GSM8K 上达 27.4% 准确率，优于先前连续扩散与流模型。H-FLM 也获得一致提升。

Conclusion: H-CDLM 是一个简单且通用的框架，能跨连续生成范式（扩散与流匹配）带来一致增益，证明多粒度联合扩散的有效性；代码将公开。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) hold the promise of order-agnostic, parallel text generation. Recently, continuous diffusion and flow matching models have seen substantial gains, driven by carefully crafted token representations and diffusion/flow spaces. In this work, we introduce Hierarchical Continuous Diffusion Language Models (H-CDLMs), a simple framework that further improves continuous DLMs with minimal compute and parameter overhead. Drawing on the discrete DLM and continuous image diffusion literature on joint diffusion, we diffuse multiple modalities in parallel. These modalities represent tokens at different semantic granularities: in our instantiation, the tokens themselves and coarser clusters obtained by clustering pretrained token embeddings. We propose a general setup that allows per-modality samplers and schedules to enhance the interplay between modalities. Applied to CoBit, this yields H-CoBit, which delivers large empirical gains across benchmarks. At dataset entropy, H-CoBit improves MAUVE and reaches a generative perplexity (GenPPL) of 49.4 on LM1B and 50.4 on OWT, improving on the baseline by 24.2 and 20.7 points and surpassing even discrete DLMs of comparable size. On GSM8K, it reaches 27.4% accuracy, outperforming prior continuous diffusion and flow-based models. We further apply H-CDLM to the flow matching model FLM, obtaining consistent gains with H-FLM and demonstrating that the framework generalizes across continuous generative paradigms. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/matol-16/HCDLM.git .

</details>


### [207] [The Missing Minimal Pair: Stereotype Evaluation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08747)
*Nataliya Stepanova,Ivan Titov,Emily Allaway,Björn Ross*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出单对反事实句比较在LLM偏见测量中不可靠，提出"双最小对"框架：通过数据增强生成同义改写与替代属性，并设计两种评估指标，其中基于互信息的指标更适合跨语言与跨模型聚合比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM偏见评测依赖对比两个刻板印象句子的对数似然差，但作者发现仅替换同一刻板印象中的属性词，就会导致逻辑不一致的偏好判断，说明单对比较缺乏稳健性，需要更可靠的多轴评测设置。

Method: 提出双最小对（dual minimal pair）设置，引入两条比较轴。其一为数据增强框架，针对英语、俄语、西班牙语和中文的刻板印象数据集生成同义改写与替代属性，以填补现有数据的缺口；其二为两种面向双最小对结构的评估指标，其中一种通过建模社会群体与刻板属性之间的互信息（MI）来刻画偏见。

Result: 在四种语言的刻板印象集合上应用该框架，生成了补充性数据；所提出指标能够对偏见进行新的度量，MI指标在聚合上表现更好，可在不同语言与模型之间进行更稳健的刻板印象强度比较。

Conclusion: 单对反事实比较不足以稳健地衡量LLM刻板偏见，双最小对设置配合数据增强与互信息指标可提升评测的可靠性与跨语言、跨模型可比性。代码已开源于 https://github.com/stepanat/missing-minimal-pair/ 。

Abstract: A common approach to measuring bias in Large Language Models is to compare the log-likelihoods of two contrastive stereotype sentences. We argue that such single-pair comparisons are often unreliable: simply rewriting the same stereotype with an alternative attribute can yield logically inconsistent preferences. To address this, we propose a dual minimal pair setup that introduces two axes of comparison for robust stereotype evaluation. First, we present a data-augmentation framework that fills critical gaps in existing stereotype datasets by generating paraphrases and alternate attributes. We apply our framework on a set of English, Russian, Spanish and Chinese stereotypes. Second, we introduce two evaluation metrics tailored to the dual minimal pair setup. One of these metrics provides a new perspective on bias by modeling the mutual information (MI) between social groups and stereotyped attributes. This MI-based metric is better suited for aggregation and enables more robust comparisons of stereotype strength across different languages and models.
  Our code is available at https://github.com/stepanat/missing-minimal-pair/.

</details>


### [208] [AdvSim2Real : Training Web Agents Against Adaptive Prompt Injection in a Web World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08773)
*Sarim Hashmi,Mukul Ranjan,Kshitij Mishra,Mikhail Kuznetsov,Praneeth Vepakomma,Nils Lukas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AdvSim2Real，在冻结的 web 世界模型中协同演化任务课程、注入对抗者和智能体，使 4B 智能体在模拟器中训练后能力与鲁棒性同时提升，并能迁移到真实浏览器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网页智能体需读取第三方编写的页面，页面上的注入指令可劫持智能体偏离用户目标；但智能体又不能忽略页面，因为任务所需的值与控制项就在页面上。现有防御在训练前固定注入样本，面对自适应攻击者易被绕过；对抗训练虽允许攻击者自适应，却固定任务，任务被解决后便不再提供学习信号。

Method: AdvSim2Real 在冻结的 web 世界模型内同时演化三部分：任务课程、注入对抗者与智能体。课程按"任务被智能体约一半概率解决"给予奖励，对抗者仅在实现"成功翻转"（把判定为成功的任务变为失败）时获得奖励。

Result: 模拟器训练使 4B 智能体能力与鲁棒性同时增强：有无攻击时完成率均提升；能抵御从未训练过的前沿模型对抗者；能力增益迁移到真实浏览器。在 150 个网页任务上，面对该未见对抗者，AdvSim2Real 相对基线智能体将完成率提升 33.6%（相对值）。

Conclusion: 协同演化的课程与对抗者提供持续有效的训练信号，可在模拟器中同时提升智能体的任务能力与对注入攻击的鲁棒性，且迁移到真实环境。

Abstract: Web agents complete user requests by reading and acting on pages that third parties write, so an instruction planted on a page can redirect the agent away from the user's goal. The agent cannot simply ignore the page, because the page also holds the values and controls the task requires. Current defenses fine-tune the agent on injections fixed before training, and attackers that adapt to the trained model bypass them. Adversarial training lets the attacker adapt but keeps the tasks fixed, so a task stops teaching once the agent solves it. We introduce AdvSim2Real, which co-evolves a task curriculum, an injection adversary, and the agent inside a frozen web world model. The curriculum is rewarded for tasks the agent solves about half of the time, and the adversary only for a success flip, an injection that turns a judged success into a failure. Training in the simulator makes a 4B agent both more capable and more robust: its completion rises with and without attacks, holds against a frontier-model adversary it never trained against, and its capability gain carries over to a real browser. On 150 web tasks, AdvSim2Real raises completion under this unseen adversary by 33.6\% relative to the base agent.

</details>


### [209] [IdeaAnchor: Teaching LLMs to Turn Literature into Research Ideas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08781)
*Ziyu Chen,Yilun Zhao,Jiashuo Sun,Yiling Ma,Manasi Patwardhan,Arman Cohan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IdeaAnchor，一种以结构化规格作为特权信号来训练 LLM 进行文献驱动科研构思的范式，通过挖掘已发表论文中的构思脚本，结合示范学习、自蒸馏、强化学习与推理时检索，显著提升构思质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研构思通常始于综合多篇相关论文以发现空白、提出新方向，但现有基于提示或反馈的方法缺乏对"论文应如何被综合"的结构化监督，导致语言模型难以有效学习这种以文献为基础的构思能力。

Method: 提出 IdeaAnchor：每个实例以结构化规格编码各输入论文在合成成功想法时的功能角色、相互关系与目标综合标准；从已发表论文中挖掘此类实例以捕获真实想法如何由既有文献涌现；采用示范学习、自蒸馏与强化学习训练模型，并在推理阶段引入检索以增强生成。

Result: 实验表明构思质量获得一致性提升；分析揭示功能分解规律——基于锚点的训练强化创造性综合，检索提升细节铺陈，两者结合取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 以结构化规格作为特权信号训练 LLM 是提升文献驱动科研构思的有效范式，锚点训练与检索各自承担不同功能且互补，联合使用效果最优，为构建科研构思系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: Scientific research often begins by synthesizing ideas from a set of related papers to identify gaps and formulate new directions. However, training language models to perform this form of literature-grounded ideation remains challenging, as existing approaches based on prompting or feedback lack structured supervision for how papers should be synthesized. We introduce IdeaAnchor, a paradigm for training LLMs to perform research ideation using structured specifications as privileged signals. Each IdeaAnchor instance encodes how each input paper should be synthesized into a successful idea, including their functional roles, relationships, and target synthesis criteria. We build this paradigm by mining instances from published papers, capturing how real ideas emerge from prior literature. We then train models via demonstration, self-distillation, and reinforcement learning, and further enhance generation with retrieval at inference time. Experiments show consistent improvements in ideation quality. Our analysis reveals a functional decomposition: anchor-based training strengthens creative synthesis, retrieval enhances detail elaboration, and combining both yields the best performance.

</details>


### [210] [OncoNoteBERT: A Foundation Representation Model for Natural Language Processing of Real-World Outpatient Oncology Notes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03829)
*Wuraola Oyewusi,Eliana Vasquez Osorio,Goran Nenadic,Gareth Price*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究基于英国门诊肿瘤语料（290,026份记录，21,564名肺癌及头颈癌患者），开发并评估了两种肿瘤学专用BERT编码器：继续预训练的OncoNote-RadBERT与从零训练的OncoNoteBERT。结果显示继续预训练语料拟合最优（困惑度2.10），而专用分词器在分词效率和掩码词预测上更优（13项中12项合理），表明继续适应与定制分词具有互补价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界门诊肿瘤记录包含专业术语、分期表达、治疗方案、毒性描述及机构特定的去标识化标记，通用生物医学或邻近临床语言模型难以高效表征。作者希望明确专用编码器与分词策略对肿瘤NLP表征质量的影响。

Method: 使用受治理的英国门诊肿瘤语料（290,026份记录，21,564名肺癌和头颈癌患者）。对比RadBERT与PathologyBERT两种外部编码器，并设计两种本地策略：继续掩码语言模型预训练得到的OncoNote-RadBERT，以及使用肿瘤学WordPiece分词器从零训练的OncoNoteBERT。评估指标包括验证集MLM损失与困惑度、分词碎片化指标、临床术语分词、掩码词探针及探索性表征分析。

Result: 零样本评估中外部编码器拟合较差（RadBERT困惑度113.04，PathologyBERT 2035.03）；继续预训练拟合最佳（OncoNote-RadBERT 2.10）。OncoNoteBERT困惑度为2.83，但分词效率最高，子词生育率更低、归一化序列长度更短；掩码词探针中13项有12项给出临床可接受的预测，而OncoNote-RadBERT为7/13。语料级拟合与掩码词表现的分歧部分源于分词碎片化而非单纯学习到的语义。两个本地模型均将机构占位符表示为单个可学习token。

Conclusion: 继续适应与定制分词提供互补收益；在将邻近领域编码器应用于肿瘤NLP之前，表征层设计至关重要。

Abstract: Real-world outpatient oncology notes contain specialised terminology, tumour staging expressions, treatment names, toxicity descriptions, and institution-specific de-identification markers that may not be represented efficiently by general biomedical or adjacent clinical language models. We developed and evaluated oncology-specific BERT-style encoders using a governed UK outpatient oncology corpus comprising 290,026 notes from 21,564 patients treated for lung and head-and-neck cancer. We compared RadBERT and PathologyBERT with two local strategies: OncoNote-RadBERT, produced by continued masked language model pretraining, and OncoNoteBERT, trained from scratch with an oncology WordPiece tokenizer. Models were evaluated using masked language modelling loss and perplexity on the validation set, tokenizer fragmentation metrics, clinical term tokenisation, masked-token probes, and exploratory representation analysis. Both external encoders fit the oncology corpus poorly in zero-shot evaluation (perplexity 113.04 for RadBERT; 2035.03 for PathologyBERT), while continued pretraining produced the strongest fit (2.10 for OncoNote-RadBERT). OncoNoteBERT achieved perplexity 2.83 but produced the most efficient tokenisation, with lower subword fertility and shorter normalised sequence length. It also returned a clinically acceptable prediction for 12 of 13 masked-token probes, compared with 7 of 13 for OncoNote-RadBERT. This divergence between corpus-level fit and masked-token performance was partly attributable to tokenizer fragmentation rather than learned semantics alone. Both locally developed models represented the institutional placeholder as a single learnable token. These findings show that continued adaptation and bespoke tokenisation provide complementary benefits, and that representation-layer design matters before adjacent-domain encoders are applied to oncology NLP.

</details>


### [211] [How Much Do LLM-as-a-Judge Design Choices Matter? A Systematic Comparison of Prompt Designs, Rating Scales, and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05094)
*Laurène Vaugrante,Thilo Hagendorff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了10个推理模型在多种评审设计下的LLM-as-a-judge表现，发现评审整体可信但设计选择会引入显著方差，其中模型身份是方差的主导来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge被广泛用于评估模型输出，但评审的提示词、评分量表和模型选择缺乏标准，研究者多凭直觉设计。若这些选择改变评审结论，同一事实可能得出不同结论，因此需要建立评审设计的实证基础。

Method: 在两个任务上评估10个推理模型的多种评审设计：一是句子情感与毒性的标量评分（每类超过500项），二是问答对的二元准确性分类（n=600）。考察提示词、评分量表、模型身份及推理投入等设计选择的影响。

Result: 评分任务中，评审虽与人类基准存在显著分歧，但实际差异较小（1-7量表上平均绝对偏差0.11分），多数评审可信，毒性评审甚至优于标准分类器；分类任务平均准确率达96.5%。但设计选择会造成偏移：仅改变评分量表可使测得偏差移动最多0.93分；分类准确率很少受影响，但设计选择持续影响评审宽松度（详细提示使宽松度下降28.9个百分点，切换模型最多下降56.1个百分点）。反直觉的是，降低推理投入既不影响准确率也不影响宽松度。两个任务中，模型身份都是方差的主导来源。

Conclusion: LLM评审在总体上广泛可信，但设计选择是方差的实质性来源。鉴于自动化评估在LLM研究中日益重要，本研究可作为方法论参考，用于设计更稳健、可复现的LLM-as-a-judge流程。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use Large Language Models as judges (LLM-as-a-judge) to evaluate model outputs. Yet there are no standards for how to design these judges. Typically, researchers choose the prompt, rating scale, and model intuitively. If these choices change the judge's verdicts, two studies can reach different conclusions about the same facts. To address this risk and to provide an empirical basis for judge designs, we evaluate 10 reasoning models across multiple designs on two tasks: a scalar rating of sentence sentiment and toxicity (over 500 items per category), as well as a binary accuracy classification of question-answer pairs (n=600). For the rating tasks, despite judges showing significant disagreements with the human ground truth, the practical size of differences is small enough to consider most judges reliable (mean absolute deviation of 0.11 points on a 1 - 7 scale); toxicity judges even outperform standard classifiers. Judges are also highly accurate on average (96.5%) for the accuracy classification task. However, design choices can produce shifts: changing the rating scale alone can shift measured bias by up to 0.93 points (rating task), and while accuracy levels are rarely impacted, design choices consistently impact judge leniency (classification task; leniency drop of 28.9 percentage points when using detailed prompts, and up to 56.1 percentage points when switching models). Counterintuitively, lower reasoning effort affects neither accuracy nor leniency. Across both tasks, model identity is the dominant source of variance. These findings suggest that while LLM judges are broadly trustworthy in aggregate, design choices can be meaningful sources of variance. Given the growing reliance on automated evaluation in LLM research, we intend this study as a methodological reference for designing more robust and replicable LLM-as-a-judge pipelines.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [GAMEGO: Training Game-Dev Agents with Synthetic Trajectories Anchored in Real-World Assets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06910)
*Haoyue Yang,Jingyao Li,Zhengfan Wu,Jing Liu,Xuanle Zhao,Kang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GameGo框架，将简短的游戏需求种子系统性地转化为基于行业实践的产品需求文档（PRD），并引入任务特定的动态压缩以保持信息密度与指令遵循。基于此构建了55,060条开发轨迹数据集GameGoData和124条查询基准GameGoBench，训练的GameGoCoder在游戏开发基准上超越同规模基线，接近前沿模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在Web前端执行（尤其是浏览器游戏生成）能力突出，但已有工作多依赖复杂的多轮工作流或仅关注静态游戏评测基准。直接从稀疏用户查询端到端生成复杂游戏时，编码智能体被迫做出大量欠明确假设，导致机制不完整、玩法流程割裂、视觉美感有限。

Method: GameGo框架：将简短的游戏种子系统性扩展为符合行业游戏开发实践的产品需求文档（PRD）；采用任务特定的动态压缩，在不限制设计探索空间的前提下最大化信息密度并保持指令遵循。在此基础上构建数据集、基准并进行模型训练。

Result: 构建了GameGoData，包含55,060条开发轨迹，覆盖2D、2.5D、3D游戏；构建了GameGoBench基准，含124条多样化游戏查询。在GameGoData上训练的GameGoCoder在游戏开发基准上优于同等基线，并与前沿模型表现相当。

Conclusion: 该工作面向由编码智能体驱动的直接端到端真实游戏合成，通过PRD扩展与动态压缩缓解稀疏查询下的规格不足问题，并提供可复现的数据、基准与模型（代码、数据集、模型将公开）。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in web front-end execution, with browser-based game generation emerging as a particularly prominent frontier. While previous efforts frequently rely on complex multi-turn workflows or focus on static game evaluation benchmarks, this work targets direct end-to-end real-world game synthesis driven by coding agents. However, generating complex games directly from sparse user queries often forces coding agents to make underspecified assumptions, yielding incomplete mechanics, disconnected gameplay flows, and limited visual aesthetics. To resolve this issue, this paper presents GameGo, a scalable framework that systematically transforms brief game seeds into comprehensive Product Requirements Documents grounded in industry game-development practices. To retain core gameplay constraints without restricting design exploration, GameGo uses task-specific dynamic compression to maximize information density while preserving instruction following. Based on this pipeline, GameGoData is constructed with 55,060 development trajectories across 2D, 2.5D, and 3D games, alongside GameGoBench, a benchmark comprising 124 diverse game queries. Training GameGoCoder on GameGoData yields a model that outperforms matched baselines and is comparable to frontier models across gamedev benchmarks. All code, datasets, and models will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [213] [Text2Dashboard: A Governed Agent Architecture for Natural-Language Dashboard Generation over Enterprise DataBrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06914)
*Yiou Wu,Zezhi Tang,Ningwei Bai,Liuhaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Text2Dashboard——一个面向 DataBrain 的原型系统，将自然语言分析请求转化为可审查的仪表盘。系统由可安装的 Codex 插件与独立 Agent Runtime 组成，采用"模型提议、确定性软件执行"的架构。在冻结的真实任务与受控故障注入测试中，元数据与 SQL 任务严格成功率为 6/8，仪表盘任务通过 4/4 单面板、1 个双面板及 1 个既有仪表盘精修，10 个故障场景全部达到预期结果，模型推理占运行时长 97% 以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言分析请求直接转化为仪表盘存在可靠性、可审查性与可恢复性的挑战。本文动机在于构建一个可检查（inspectable）的流程：让模型只负责提出动作建议，而由确定性软件控制执行、记录状态转换，并通过 Hooks 实现审批、审计、检查点、恢复与失败处理，从而在保持灵活性的同时确保可控性与安全性（如只读 SQL 约束）。

Method: 方法上，系统采用 schema 约束的模型决策 + 类型化工具 + 持久化状态 + 确定性 Hooks（审批、审计、检查点、恢复、失败处理）的架构。流水线依次完成实体解析、元数据发现、只读 SQL 强制、仪表盘组合，并施加静态检查、动态预检与浏览器检查。评估使用冻结的真实 DataBrain 任务与受控 Hook 故障场景。

Result: 元数据与 SQL 任务严格成功 6/8：元数据选择 4/4 通过，四个 SQL 任务均满足语义标准，2/4 满足精确输出列契约。最终版本通过 4/4 单面板仪表盘任务、1 个双面板任务及 1 个既有仪表盘精修；一个参数化任务超出步数上限。10 个故障场景全部达到指定结果且无未授权外部副作用。所有报告组中模型推理占比均超过 97% 运行时长。

Conclusion: 这些结果规模小且针对 DataBrain 专用场景，不足以证明生产就绪性、通用 text-to-SQL 准确性，或相对人工构建仪表盘的效率优势。作者明确界定了结论边界，未做过度外推。

Abstract: Text2Dashboard is a DataBrain-specific prototype that turns natural-language analytic requests into inspectable dashboards. An installable Codex plugin and standalone Agent Runtime combine schema-constrained model decisions with typed tools, persistent state, and deterministic Hooks for approval, audit, checkpointing, recovery, and failure handling. The pipeline resolves entities, discovers metadata, enforces read-only SQL, composes dashboards, and applies static checks, dynamic preflight, and browser inspection. The model proposes actions while deterministic software controls execution and records state transitions.
  We evaluate the workflow on frozen real-DataBrain tasks and controlled Hook faults. Strict success was 6/8 on metadata and SQL tasks: metadata selection passed 4/4, all four SQL tasks met semantic criteria, and 2/4 met the exact output-column contract. The final release passed 4/4 single-panel dashboard tasks, one two-panel task, and one existing-dashboard refinement; a parameterised task exceeded its step limit. All ten fault scenarios met their specified outcomes without unapproved external side effects. Model inference accounted for over 97\% of observed runtime in every reported group. These small, DataBrain-specific results do not establish production readiness, general text-to-SQL accuracy, or an efficiency advantage over manual dashboard construction.

</details>


### [214] [FluidPD: In-Place Elasticity for SLO-Aware Prefill-Decode Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06917)
*Kartik Ramesh,Kaidi Fu,Zihan Zheng,Jiahuan Yu,Fabio Oliveira,Carlos Costa,Minjia Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FluidPD是一个P/D解耦的LLM服务系统，通过FluidToken（预填充计算卸载到解码工作节点）和FluidRole（工作节点角色原地转换）两种机制，实现SLO感知的原地弹性伸缩，在Azure生产trace上相比静态SGLang最多提升94.6个百分点的SLO达成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有P/D解耦服务系统通常采用固定的预填充/解码工作节点比例加请求路由，但真实负载既有短时突发也有持续的预填充-解码需求比例漂移，导致配置与实际需求快速失配，即使其他位置存在空闲容量也会引发延迟SLO违规。现有自动扩缩容机制反应慢、需要额外GPU，且无法直接解决短时间尺度的阶段失衡问题。

Method: 提出FluidPD系统，包含两个互补机制：1) FluidToken处理瞬时失衡，当解码侧有空闲时，将有界的预填充计算卸载到解码工作节点；2) FluidRole处理持续失衡，原地在预填充与解码角色间重新分配运行中的工作节点，避免模型重载和引擎重启。两者均由轻量级压力指标（pressure indices）引导，可在资源压力演变为SLO违规之前暴露预填充和解码侧的压力。

Result: 在Azure生产trace工作负载上，FluidPD相比静态SGLang将整体SLO达成率最多提升94.6个百分点。

Conclusion: SLO感知的原地P/D弹性伸缩能够在不额外提供工作节点的情况下提升服务质量。

Abstract: Prefill-decode disaggregation is becoming a common architecture for LLM serving because it separates two phases with distinct execution patterns and SLO objectives. Existing systems typically combine a fixed prefill/decode worker ratio with request routing across workers. However, real-world workloads exhibit both short bursts and sustained shifts in the prefill-to-decode demand ratio. As a result, a configuration that is well provisioned at one time may quickly become mismatched, causing latency SLO violations even when idle capacity exists elsewhere. Existing autoscaling mechanisms can add capacity, but they react slowly, require spare GPUs, and do not directly address short-timescale phase imbalance.
  We present FluidPD, a P/D-disaggregated serving system that provides SLO-aware in-place elasticity. FluidPD introduces two complementary mechanisms. FluidToken handles transient imbalance by offloading a bounded portion of prefill computation to decode workers when decode-side slack is available. FluidRole handles sustained imbalance by reassigning running workers between prefill and decode roles in place, avoiding model reload and engine restart. Both mechanisms are guided by lightweight pressure indices that expose prefill and decode-side resource pressure before they appear as SLO violations. Across production Azure trace workloads, FluidPD improves overall SLO attainment over static SGLang by up to 94.6 percentage points, demonstrating that SLO-aware in-place P/D elasticity improves service quality without provisioning additional workers.

</details>


### [215] [Anchor Divergence for Semantic Geometry in Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06919)
*Akash Kannan,Kiho Park,Victor Veitch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出"锚点散度"(Anchor Divergences)，通过在对比学习、指数族与信息几何之间建立对应关系，将锚点上的概率分布映射为表示空间上的Bregman几何，从而在固定表示上定义上下文相关的语义相似度几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统余弦相似度只提供单一固定几何，但语义相似度本质上是上下文相关的：两张图像可能因同一物体、相似视觉风格或同一临床发现而相似。因此需要一种能在固定表示上指定上下文特定语义几何的方法。

Method: 利用对比学习、指数族与信息几何之间的相互作用，建立"锚点上的概率分布"与"表示空间上的Bregman几何"之间的对应关系；据此定义Anchor Divergences，即通过建模锚点分布来指定上下文特定的语义几何（建模锚点分布即建模几何本身）。

Result: 检索实验表明，锚点散度能有效且高效地指定上下文相关的语义相似度。

Conclusion: 对比表示天然包含一族可针对特定语义结构特化的几何；通过锚点分布与Bregman几何的对应，可用锚点散度在固定表示上灵活、高效地实现上下文特定的语义相似度度量。

Abstract: This paper concerns how semantic context determines geometry in learned vector representations. Similarity is typically measured using cosine similarity, which provides a single fixed geometry. Semantic similarity, however, is inherently context dependent: two images may be similar because they depict the same object, share a visual style, or are relevant to the same clinical finding. We show that contrastive representations naturally encompass a family of geometries that can be specialized to particular semantic structure. The key idea is to use an interplay between contrastive learning, exponential families, and information geometry to establish a correspondence between probability distributions over "anchors" and Bregman geometries on the representation space. We use this correspondence to define "Anchor Divergences", a method for specifying context-specific semantic geometries on fixed representations. Under this correspondence, modeling the anchor distribution models the geometry itself. Experiments on retrieval show that anchor divergences provide an effective and efficient way to specify context-specific semantic similarity.

</details>


### [216] [RadOnc-Agent: An LLM-Orchestrated Framework for AI Workflows Across the Radiotherapy Care Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06923)
*Caiwen Jiang,Shuoyang Wei,Songlin Zhao,Junyu Li,Jingyuan Chen,Wei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出RadOnc-Agent，一个将放疗全流程形式化为四个临床阶段、通过对话界面调用26个功能的智能体AI框架，并在2600条单功能请求、200个跨阶段合成场景及60份真实患者记录共120个流程实例上进行了执行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI虽在单点放疗任务上表现出色，但能力分散于不同临床阶段、软件环境和数据模态，与放疗从治疗决策到随访的纵向连续工作流相割裂，缺乏跨阶段协调机制。

Method: 提出RadOnc-Agent智能体框架：将放疗流程形式化为四个临床阶段，提供26个可调用函数及对话式接口；由大语言模型控制器将临床意图映射为受模式约束（schema-constrained）的函数调用，维护患者与工作流上下文，并将请求路由至专科服务。评估采用三类设置：2600条单功能请求（7800次重复执行）、200个预设的跨四阶段合成场景（600次执行），以及来自60份去标识患者记录的120个流程实例（360次干净执行），覆盖"决策到计划"与"计划到自适应"环节；并开展消融对比。

Result: 单功能执行中正确选择目标函数的比例为98.79%；脚本化跨阶段工作流完成率96.50%；真实患者工作流执行完成率96.67%。消融实验中，移除纵向状态使跨阶段完成率从96.50%降至84.00%；禁用模式与身份校验使后端错误派发率在回放/测试评估中从0%升至95.28%。

Conclusion: 结果证明了以LLM编排架构跨纵向工作流协调异构放疗能力与信息的技术可行性，但并未验证临床正确性、临床效用或前瞻性获益。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence has advanced individual radiotherapy tasks, yet these capabilities remain separated across clinical stages, software environments and data modalities. This fragmentation contrasts with the longitudinal radiotherapy workflow from treatment decision-making through follow-up. Here we present RadOnc-Agent, an agentic artificial-intelligence framework that formalizes radiotherapy into four clinical phases and provides 26 callable functions through a conversational interface. A large-language-model controller maps clinical intent to schema-constrained calls, preserves patient and workflow context, and routes requests to specialist services. We evaluated system execution using 2,600 single-function requests (7,800 repeat executions), 200 prespecified synthetic cross-stage scenarios spanning four phases (600 executions), and 120 workflow instances from 60 de-identified patient records (360 clean executions) representing decision-to-planning and planning-to-adaptation. RadOnc-Agent selected the intended function in 98.79% of single-function executions, completed 96.50% of scripted cross-stage workflows, and completed 96.67% of real-patient workflow executions. In comparative ablations, removing longitudinal state reduced cross-stage completion from 96.50% to 84.00%, while disabling schema and identity validation increased mismatched backend dispatch from 0% to 95.28% in a replay/test evaluation. These findings establish the technical feasibility of an LLM-orchestrated architecture for coordinating heterogeneous radiotherapy capabilities and information across longitudinal workflows; they do not establish clinical correctness, clinical utility or prospective benefit.

</details>


### [217] [Metonymic Circuits for Abstract Concept Grounding in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06928)
*Jing Ding,Ziqiao Ma,Jiayuan Mao,Joyce Chai,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出并验证了视觉Transformer识别抽象概念时的"转喻式接地"机制：抽象预测由具体、可解释的锚概念（如用fire锚定angry）驱动，并在CLIP与DINO中定位出相应电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究Vision Transformers在训练数据缺乏直接指称证据的条件下，如何将视觉信号接地到抽象概念（如angry）。作者假设存在一种转喻式接地机制，即抽象语义并非直接由视觉特征映射，而是通过具体、可解释的锚概念（如fire）作为中介来桥接。

Method: 方法上，作者在CLIP和DINO视觉编码器上应用Transcoders，恢复可与具体概念语义标签相关联的中间特征，并追踪这些特征在支撑抽象概念识别的电路中的贡献；同时构建了一个精心挑选的图标数据集进行实验，并采用因果干预（causal interventions）检验中介特征的功能性作用。

Result: 结果显示存在结构化的转喻电路：感知原语（perceptual primitives）在浅层占主导，类物体锚概念先于抽象目标出现；含渲染文本的图像则调用一条独立的"感知—文本"路径。因果干预进一步证实转喻式中介特征确实功能性参与抽象概念的接地过程。

Conclusion: 结论是Vision Transformers对抽象概念的接地可通过转喻式中介机制解释：具体锚概念在电路中充当视觉信号与抽象语义之间的桥梁，且该机制具有因果有效性。

Abstract: We study how Vision Transformers ground abstract concepts (e.g., angry) when training data provide limited direct referential evidence. We hypothesize a metonymic grounding mechanism in which abstract predictions are driven by concrete, interpretable anchor concepts (e.g., fire) that bridge visual signals to abstract semantics. By applying Transcoders on CLIP and DINO vision encoders, we recover intermediate features that can be associated with semantic labels for more concrete concepts, and trace their contributions in circuits underlying abstract concept recognition. Experiments on a carefully curated icon dataset reveal structured metonymic circuits, in which perceptual primitives dominate early layers and object-like anchors precede abstract targets. Images containing rendered text instead recruit a distinct perceptual-to-textual route. Causal interventions further validate that metonymic intermediates are functionally involved in grounding abstract concepts.

</details>


### [218] [Principles that Guide, Actions that Inform: Agent Evolution via Knowledge Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06964)
*Bowen Ye,Yongchao Xu,Junkai Ma,Xiang Yin,Wenzhao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SAGA框架，将LLM智能体的交互轨迹抽象为情景描述、可复用流程及带适用条件的原理，通过执行—抽象反馈回路实现知识的泛化与持续进化，在ScienceWorld和ALFWorld上提升任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在交互环境中表现强劲，但持续从经验中进化能力有限。微调依赖参数访问且计算成本高，对大规模和闭源模型不灵活；外部记忆虽无需改参数，但现有方法侧重经验表示与组织，知识仍与特定任务和上下文紧耦合，泛化性差。核心挑战在于如何将具体交互转化为抽象、可复用的知识，以指导超越个体经验的未来决策。

Method: 提出SAGA（Self-evolving Agents through Experience-Grounded Abstraction）框架，用于基于经验的知识抽象与利用。逐步将交互轨迹转化为情景描述、可复用流程和带明确适用条件的原理，同时保持与执行证据的链接。检索到的原理被实例化为任务特定指导，并通过纠正反馈与重采样精炼候选动作，形成执行—抽象反馈回路，使积累的知识指导未来交互，新经验持续更新层次化记忆。

Result: 在ScienceWorld和ALFWorld实验中任务性能得到提升；消融研究凸显了上下文实例化和动作调节对利用原理级知识的重要性。

Conclusion: SAGA通过经验落地抽象和反馈回路，在不修改模型参数的前提下实现LLM智能体的持续进化与知识泛化，验证了层次化抽象知识的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capabilities in interactive environments, yet their ability to continually evolve from experience remains limited. Although fine-tuning enables adaptation, its dependence on parameter access and high computational costs restrict its flexibility, especially for large-scale and closed-source LLMs. External memory offers an alternative by allowing agents to accumulate experience without modifying model parameters. However, existing methods mainly focus on experience representation and organization, while the acquired knowledge remains tightly coupled with specific tasks and contexts, limiting generalization. A key challenge is how to transform concrete interactions into abstract and reusable knowledge that guides future decisions beyond individual experiences.
  To address this challenge, we propose SAGA (\underline{\textbf{S}}elf-evolving \underline{\textbf{A}}gents through Experience-\underline{\textbf{G}}rounded \underline{\textbf{A}}bstraction), a framework for experience-grounded knowledge abstraction and utilization in LLM agents. SAGA progressively transforms interaction trajectories into episodic descriptions, reusable procedures, and principles with explicit applicability conditions, while maintaining links to execution evidence. Retrieved principles are instantiated into task-specific guidance and used to refine candidate actions through corrective feedback and resampling. This creates an execution--abstraction feedback loop, where accumulated knowledge guides future interactions and new experiences continuously update hierarchical memory. Experiments on ScienceWorld and ALFWorld demonstrate improved task performance, with ablation studies highlighting the importance of contextual instantiation and action regulation for leveraging principle-level knowledge.

</details>


### [219] [AegisFlow: A Multi-Agent Agentic AI Framework for Autonomous Remediation and Self-Healing in Fragile Data Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06971)
*Muhammad Bilal Awan,Zubair Hussain,Abdul Shahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AegisFlow 是一个基于 LLM 智能体的自愈框架，通过 Watchdog 与 Repair 双智能体及 Parallel Shadow Patching 执行模型，实现数据管道故障的自动检测与修复，将 MTTR 从 170 分钟降至 3.2 分钟，补丁成功率 92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据管道脆弱，常因上游 schema 漂移、API 契约变更或网站 DOM 修改而失败。现有可观测性工具仅能告警，需人工工程师介入，导致 MTTR 高、运维疲劳严重。作者希望打通"检测—解决"闭环。

Method: 提出 AegisFlow（Agentic Engine for Intelligent Self-healing and Graph-driven Operations for Workload remediation）智能体框架：Watchdog 智能体采集运行时遥测，Repair 智能体基于 LLM 自动创建、测试并部署代码补丁；核心执行模型为 Parallel Shadow Patching，基于 MAPE-K 循环，在数字孪生环境中生成与验证补丁。

Result: 在五类常见故障场景中评估：MTTR 改善 98.1%（平均每补丁 170 分钟降至 3.2 分钟），补丁成功率 92%；JSON schema 变更 96%、标点漂移 98% 表现最佳，Shadow DOM 场景最低（85%）；释放约 98% 数据工程 on-call 时间。

Conclusion: AegisFlow 将数据工程师从救火式运维中解放，转向创新工作；框架部署无关，可以插件形式集成到现有管道编排系统中，对既有系统改动极小。

Abstract: Traditional data pipelines are notoriously brittle, often failing due to upstream schema drift, API contract changes, or website DOM modifications. Present observability tools only raise alerts but for human engineers, resulting in a high Mean Time to Repair (MTTR) and operational fatigue. In this paper we propose AegisFlow (Agentic Engine for Intelligent Self-healing and Graph-driven Operations for Workload remediation), a novel agentic framework that closes the loop between detection and resolution. AegisFlow uses a Watchdog agent to collect runtime telemetry and has a Repair agent to automatically create, test and deploy code patches based on Large Language Models (LLMs). The framework presents the non-intrusive execution model called Parallel Shadow Patching, a non-intrusive execution model based on the Monitor, Analyze, Plan, Execute, Knowledge (MAPE-K) loop to generate and verify patches in digital twin environments. Through experimental testing, we have evaluated AegisFlow across five common failure scenarios, and see 98.1 percent improvement in MTTR (from an average of 170 minutes per patch to 3.2 minutes) and a patch success rate of 92 percent . In particular, the system is successful in dealing with changes in the JSON schema (96 percent ) and punctuation drift (98 percent ), and is least successful in Shadow DOM cases (85 percent ). AegisFlow frees up about 98 percent of data engineering on-call time from firefighting and reallocates it towards innovation. The framework is deployment agnostic consisting of a system that can be deployed in a plugin fashion into an existing pipeline orchestration system with minimal uplift to the existing system.

</details>


### [220] [EPOCH: Reliable Discovery through Evidence-Governed Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06986)
*Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Ruitong Li,Xinle Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EPOCH：一种"证据治理"架构，通过任务契约、类型化记忆、主动证伪、准入检查与独立复现，使AI研究智能体在提升性能的同时产出更可信的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究智能体在搜索程序、数学构造与证明时，通常只优化评估器反馈，却缺乏对反馈如何被解释、质疑和复用的治理机制。这导致脆弱但看似有前景的候选被误判为发现，并使基准提升、有限证书与定理级断言相互混淆。

Method: EPOCH 构建了证据治理式发现闭环，整合五项机制：显式任务契约（明确所支持断言的强度与范围）、类型化记忆、主动证伪、准入检查、独立复现（replay），据此对每个候选按其声称的强度与范围进行评估。

Result: 在 AlgoTune 上取得最优聚合表现，平均归一化得分 0.65 显著超过最强基线 0.53；在内部 Math14 套件取得最高均分 0.57；在官方测试复现下展现出良好的留出行为；在 AgentHPO 上取得描述性聚合领先。在十个发现问题上带来实质性任务特定进展，包括可执行构造改进、算法优化、反例以及有证明支撑的结果。

Conclusion: 证据治理是通向既能产出更强解、又能产出更可信科学发现的AI研究智能体的必要一步。

Abstract: AI research agents are increasingly used to search over programs, mathematical constructions, and proofs. However, existing systems typically optimize evaluator feedback without adequately governing how that feedback is interpreted, challenged, and reused. As a result, promising but fragile candidates can be promoted as discoveries, while benchmark improvements, finite certificates, and theorem-level claims are too easily conflated. We introduce EPOCH, an evidence-governed architecture designed to close this gap. EPOCH implements an evidence-governed discovery loop by combining explicit task contracts, typed memory, active falsification, admission checks, and independent replay, so that each candidate is evaluated against the strength and scope of the claim it supports. EPOCH achieves state-of-the-art aggregate performance on AlgoTune, substantially exceeding the strongest baseline in mean normalized score (0.65 vs. 0.53), and attains the highest mean score on the internal Math14 suite (0.57). It further shows favorable held-out behavior under official-test replay and leads the descriptive aggregate on AgentHPO. Across ten discovery problems, EPOCH delivers substantial task-specific advances, including improved executable constructions, optimized algorithms, counterexamples, and proof-supported results. These advances demonstrate its ability to convert search into concrete progress across mathematical and computational domains. Together, the results suggest that evidence governance is a necessary step toward AI research agents that produce not only stronger solutions, but also more trustworthy scientific discoveries.

</details>


### [221] [When better traffic forecasts fail to improve signal control: a layered diagnostic study of forecast-to-decision value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06992)
*Jianing Long,Xiaobin Li,Wuming Lei,Weiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 交通预测精度的提升并不必然转化为更优的信号控制决策。本文以中国宣城29天重构需求（7天留作测试）开展分层诊断，发现预测指标改善、共形区间覆盖在极端子集失效、接口泄漏与动作可辨识性不足，且因果预测与事件型先验预言机在冻结测试日反而使排队车·秒上升6.09%与3.39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有文献普遍以预测精度（如MAE、覆盖率）作为改进信号控制的依据，但精度提升与控制收益之间存在转化缺口；作者希望厘清预测改进在何种条件下无法带来运行收益，并识别导致该缺口的机制性因素。

Method: 基于宣城29天重构需求（7天冻结测试）构建分层诊断框架：逐层评估点预测、共形区间、依赖感知情景与匹配的闭环控制器；在入口级与运动级预测上与历史均值对比；对90%名义共形区间考察边际覆盖与事后高需求子集覆盖；通过接口审计检测决策时泄漏并统计可控交叉口的有效动作数；修正时间接口后，将因果预测与五秒事件预言机在穷举联合动作搜索下对比，并设置合成正控制以验证未来信息可降低内部rollout代价。

Result: 入口级与运动级预测相对历史均值分别降低MAE 4.03%与3.92%；名义90%共形区间边际覆盖达90.72%，但事后高需求子集仅75.66%；接口审计发现决策时泄漏，且9个受控交叉口中仅2个具备多个有效动作；合成正控制中未来信息使内部rollout代价降低61.5%；冻结测试日上，因果预测与事件预言机相对匹配的无未来rollout分别使排队车·秒增加6.09%与3.39%，预言机使溢流暴露降低3.78%，配对日自助法区间均跨越零。

Conclusion: 预测价值取决于四个条件：时间可观测性、动作可辨识性、动态一致性与目标对齐。所提协议可用于诊断预测改进在何处未能转化为运行收益，提示以精度为核心的评估不足以支撑控制决策的改进。

Abstract: Improved traffic forecasts do not necessarily yield better signal-control decisions. We investigate this gap through a layered diagnostic study using 29 days of reconstructed demand from Xuancheng, China, with seven dates reserved for testing. The framework evaluates point forecasts, conformal intervals, dependence-aware scenarios, and matched closed-loop controllers. Entry-level and movement-level forecasts reduce mean absolute error by 4.03% and 3.92%, respectively, relative to historical means. A nominal 90% conformal interval achieves 90.72% marginal coverage but only 75.66% on an ex-post high-demand subset. Interface audits identify decision-time leakage and reveal that only two of nine controlled intersections offer multiple effective actions. We correct the temporal interface and compare causal forecasts with a five-second event oracle using exhaustive joint-action search. A synthetic positive control demonstrates that future information can reduce the internal rollout cost by 61.5%. On the frozen test dates, however, causal forecasts and the event oracle increase queue vehicle?seconds by 6.09% and 3.39% relative to the matched no-future rollout, while the oracle reduces spillback exposure by 3.78%; paired-day bootstrap intervals cross zero. These findings indicate that forecast value depends on temporal observability, action identifiability, dynamics consistency, and objective alignment. The proposed protocol provides a practical way to diagnose where predictive improvements fail to translate into operational benefits.

</details>


### [222] [Joint upper-bound coverage and route-choice utility: an empirical evaluation on two urban proxy tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06995)
*Jianing Long,Xiaobin Li,Wuming Lei,Weiguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文用冻结协议拆解速度误差、联合候选路径上界覆盖率、路径选择与实现损失，在Beijing和成都路网离线代理任务中检验"提高不确定性覆盖率是否改善路径决策"。结果显示联合校准上界虽显著提升覆盖率，却反而增加迟到率与平均行程时间；而速度MAE降低则与迟到减少同向。结论是联合覆盖率不能作为下游路径效用的替代指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通预测文献常以预测精度（如速度误差）或不确定性覆盖率作为评价指标，但这些指标是否真正改善路径决策并不清楚。本文动机在于检验"更准确的预测"或"更高的不确定性覆盖率"是否等价于更好的下游路径选择效用，即验证常用预测指标能否作为决策质量的替代量。

Method: 采用冻结协议（frozen protocol），将速度误差、联合候选路径上界覆盖率、路径选择与实现损失四个环节显式分离。使用处理后的Beijing与成都道路速度数据，构建离线代理任务：每城市150个OD对、3条候选路径、14个测试日。对比两种方案：原始90百分位路径时间上界 vs 联合校准上界，均采用最小上界路径选择准则（minimum bound route choice）。另设成都独立预测器对比实验，考察速度MAE降低对C0下迟到的影响。

Result: 联合覆盖率提升明显：Beijing M1由83.19%升至92.26%，成都M1由75.14%升至88.33%，成都M2由74.01%升至90.64%。但C2方案分别使迟到率增加0.1633、0.7848、0.9200个百分点，平均行程时间增加0.588、3.082、4.418秒。成都预测器对比中，速度MAE降低14.91%伴随C0下迟到率下降1.4571个百分点。

Conclusion: 在这些冻结的离线代理任务中，联合覆盖率并非下游路径效用的替代指标：提升覆盖率可能以牺牲准时性和行程时间为代价，而降低速度误差则与更好决策同向。因此不应以覆盖率单独评判路径决策质量；同时这些离线结果不能推断在线或因果收益，需进一步在线或因果性验证。

Abstract: Whether more accurate traffic forecasts or higher uncertainty coverage improve route decisions is unclear. We evaluate this question with a frozen protocol that separates speed error, joint candidate path upper bound coverage, route selection, and realized loss. Using processed road speed data from Beijing and Chengdu, we construct offline proxy tasks with 150 origin destination pairs, three candidate paths, and 14 test days per city. We compare raw 90th percentile path time bounds with jointly calibrated upper bounds under minimum bound route choice. Joint coverage rises from 83.19% to 92.26% in Beijing M1, from 75.14% to 88.33% in Chengdu M1, and from 74.01% to 90.64% in Chengdu M2. Yet C2 increases lateness by 0.1633, 0.7848, and 0.9200 percentage points, respectively, and mean travel time by 0.588, 3.082, and 4.418 seconds. In a separate Chengdu predictor comparison, a 14.91% reduction in speed mean absolute error accompanies a 1.4571 percentage point reduction in lateness under C0. Joint coverage is therefore not a surrogate for downstream route utility in these frozen tasks the offline results do not establish online or causal benefits.

</details>


### [223] [Topology-Consistent Task Planning over Cellular Workflow Complexes for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07004)
*Sen Zhao,Jia Tang,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Ding Zou,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Junwei Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TopoPlanner，将工具依赖图提升为胞腔工作流复形，作为拓扑感知上下文实现拓扑一致的LLM智能体任务规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体规划器在顺序或DAG结构上表现良好，但难以处理真实工具编排中常见的验证-纠错循环、分支汇聚合并、可复用中间状态等复杂工作流模式。

Method: 通过余层（cosheaf）一致的胞腔检索获取与请求相关的闭子复形；在检索到的拓扑上进行多维结构推理；将胞腔表示与规划LLM对接以生成工具序列。

Result: 在四个工具规划基准（含拓扑引导的循环、合并、循环-合并工作流）上，跨不同本地LLM骨干均持续优于基于提示和基于图增强的基线方法。

Conclusion: 拓扑一致的胞腔复形建模范式能有效覆盖循环、汇聚与复用等复杂依赖结构，为LLM智能体工具规划提供了一种新的结构感知框架。

Abstract: Task planning for LLM agents requires workflows that satisfy both user intent and complex sub-task dependencies. While existing planners work well for sequential or directed acyclic graph (DAG)-like structures, they struggle with workflow patterns such as verification-correction loops, convergent branch merging, and reusable intermediate states that arise naturally in real-world tool orchestration. We present TopoPlanner, a topology-consistent planning framework that lifts tool dependency graphs into cellular workflow complexes and uses them as topologyaware context for LLM tool planning. TopoPlanner retrieves a request-relevant closed subcomplex through cosheaf-consistent cellular retrieval, performs multidimensional structural reasoning over the retrieved topology, and interfaces the resulting cellular representation with the planner LLM for tool-sequence generation. Experiments on four tool-planning benchmarks with topology-guided loop, merge, and loop-merge workflows show consistent improvements over prompt-based and graph-enhanced baselines across different local LLM backbones.

</details>


### [224] [When to Rethink: Learning Multi-Perspective Self-Verification for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07018)
*Ziquan Zhu,Hanruo Zhu,Si-Yuan Lu,Morris Yu-Chao Huang,Yicheng Lin,Wei Han,Tianlong Chen,Mingyuan Wu,Hanchao Yu,Gaojie Jin,Lu Liu,Bo Sun,Tianjin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOTIVE多视角自验证框架，通过学习可靠性分数决定接受或重思，提升VLM多模态推理的可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM自验证方法依赖单一验证标准或固定提示，导致可靠性估计不完整且不稳定；需系统分析验证器能力与提示设计的影响。

Method: 先分析验证器能力和提示设计对验证性能的影响；再提出MOTIVE，从互补视角评估候选答案，通过以正确性为基准的多视角验证学习得到对齐正确性的可靠性分数，推理时据此进行接受或重思决策。

Result: 在多种多模态基准和VLM骨干上，MOTIVE持续优于强自验证与自纠正基线；可靠验证改善了接受或重思决策并减少不必要的推理轮次。

Conclusion: MOTIVE无需外部评判器即可实现更可靠、更高效的自验证，提升多模态推理答案可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have achieved strong performance in multimodal reasoning, yet they remain prone to generating plausible but incorrect answers. Self-verification offers a practical way to improve answer reliability without relying on external judges, but existing methods typically depend on a single verification criterion or fixed prompt, resulting in incomplete and unstable reliability estimates. We first systematically analyze how verifier capability and prompt design affect verification performance. Our findings show that stronger verifiers provide more reliable judgments, while verification performance is highly sensitive to prompt choice, with no single prompt consistently dominating across tasks. Guided by these findings, we propose \texttt{MOTIVE}, a \textbf{M}ulti-View Self-Verificati\textbf{O}n wi\textbf{T}h Rel\textbf{I}ability-Guided Selecti\textbf{VE} Rethinking framework for reliable multimodal reasoning. \texttt{MOTIVE} evaluates each candidate answer from complementary verification perspectives and learns a correctness-aligned reliability score through correctness-grounded multi-view verification learning. During inference, this score governs an accept-or-rethink decision, allowing reliable answers to be returned directly while uncertain ones trigger history-guided rethinking. Extensive experiments across diverse multimodal benchmarks and VLM backbones demonstrate that \texttt{MOTIVE} consistently outperforms strong self-verification and self-correction baselines. Further results show that reliable verification improves accept-or-rethink decisions and reduces unnecessary reasoning turns, enabling more reliable and efficient self-verification without an external judge.

</details>


### [225] [Beyond Refusal Patterns: Safe-Role Internalization for Robust and Generalizable LLM Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07023)
*Jinghao Pang,Jitai Hao,Qiang Huang,Zhaochun Ren,Jun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SSRFT框架，将安全对齐重构为对预设"安全角色"的内化，通过构建安全角色问答数据集训练模型，在鲁棒性、泛化性上优于标准SFT，并减少过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐方法（SFT、RLHF）需大量攻击专属监督和算力，且存在浅层安全对齐与过度拒绝问题，亟需更鲁棒、泛化的对齐方案。

Method: SSRFT将安全对齐重构为对预定义安全角色的内化：基于心理测量题目、少量越狱提示和安全角色描述构建SRQA数据集，合成、验证并扩展角色一致的回答至多样场景，使模型内化安全价值观与原则而非显式拒绝模式。

Result: 在多种Base与Instruct模型上，SSRFT比标准SFT实现更鲁棒、更可泛化的安全对齐；对预填充攻击鲁棒性显著更强，对未见越狱域泛化更好，同时减少对良性查询的过度拒绝并保持通用能力。

Conclusion: 安全角色内化是替代以拒绝为中心的安全对齐的有效途径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that elicit harmful or unsafe outputs. Existing safety alignment approaches, including Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF), often require substantial attack-specific supervision and computational resources, while remaining susceptible to shallow safety alignment and over-refusal. To address these challenges, we introduce SSRFT(Supervised Safe-Role Fine-Tuning), the first framework that reformulates safety alignment as the internalization of a predefined safe role. SSRFT constructs a Safe-Role Question-Answer (SRQA) dataset from psychometric questions, limited jailbreak prompts, and a safe-role description. Role-consistent responses are synthesized, validated, and expanded into diverse scenarios, enabling models to internalize safety-oriented values and principles rather than explicit refusal patterns. Experiments across multiple Base and Instruct models show that SSRFT achieves more robust and generalizable safety alignment than standard SFT. SSRFT shows substantially greater robustness to prefilling attacks and better generalization to unseen jailbreak domains, while reducing over-refusal on benign queries and preserving the model's general capabilities. These results establish safe-role internalization as an effective alternative to refusal-centric safety alignment. Warning: This paper contains examples of harmful and toxic language.

</details>


### [226] [Offline AI Modules: Voice-First Offline Architecture, Hardware Reference Stack, Quantization and Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07026)
*Sunday Afariogun,Odunolaoluwa Jenrola,Zeinab Nezami*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对面向非洲语言社区的离线语音优先AI模块栈进行首次端到端基准评测，覆盖Jetson Orin NX与Raspberry Pi 5两级硬件、三种2-5B指令微调模型、四种量化格式，结论是Q4_K_M在体积与质量间权衡最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲语言社区以语音为主要交互方式，且网络连接不可靠或缺失，因而需要完全离线、低功耗、社区可负担的语音优先AI系统；该工作流旨在提供模块化离线架构、低成本硬件参考物料清单以及可复现的量化与基准评测流水线。

Method: 构建模块化语音优先离线架构、低成本硬件参考BOM及面向2-5B指令微调模型的可复现量化与基准流水线；在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX（TierB）与Raspberry Pi 5（TierA）上，对三种指令微调模型、四种量化格式进行部署指标评估（解码吞吐、对话延迟、内存、功耗），并用MasakhaNEWS（英语、豪萨语、伊博语、尼日利亚皮钦语、约鲁巴语）评估主题分类准确率与各语言困惑度漂移，用Ethio-ASR在阿姆哈拉语和奥罗莫语上评估语音识别。

Result: Q4_K_M量化在两个硬件层级上均呈现最佳体积-质量权衡；在TierB上gemma-4-E2B-it于Q4_K_M达到28.8 t/s解码吞吐与89.2%主题分类准确率；三种模型在TierA上均运行于16GB内存预算之内。

Conclusion: 针对完全离线的非洲语言语音优先AI部署，Q4_K_M是推荐的量化方案，可在两级硬件上兼顾低功耗、内存适配与多语言质量，为该场景的实际部署提供了可复现的基准依据。

Abstract: The Offline AI Modules workstream enables practical, low-power, and community-accessible deployment of voice-first AI systems that operate fully offline. Designed for African language communities where speech is the dominant mode of interaction and internet connectivity is unreliable or absent, the workstream delivers three reinforcing components: a modular voice-first offline architecture, a low-cost hardware reference bill of materials, and a reproducible quantization and a reproducible quantization and benchmarking pipeline for instruction-tuned language models in the 2-5B parameter class. This paper presents the first end-to-end benchmark evaluation of the stack across two hardware tiers: an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX (TierB) and a Raspberry Pi5 (TierA). Three instruction-tuned models are evaluated across four quantization formats, assessed for deployment metrics (decode throughput, chat latency, memory, power) and multilingual quality (topic classification accuracy on MasakhaNEWS across English, Hausa, Igbo, Nigerian Pidgin, and Yoruba; per-language perplexity drift). Speech recognition is evaluated using Ethio-ASR on Amharic and Oromo across both tiers. The principal finding is that Q4_K_M quantization represents the best size-to-quality trade-off for deployment on both tiers: gemma-4-E2B-it achieves 28.8t/s decode throughput and 89.2% topic classification accuracy at Q4_K_M on TierB, while all three models run within the 16GB memory budget on TierA.

</details>


### [227] [JIVEAdapter: A Multi-Task Additive Low-Rank Adapter via Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07036)
*Sara Abdali,Pashmina Cameron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: JIVEAdapter是一种受统计JIVE启发的多任务"加性"低秩适配器，将权重更新分解为共享Joint结构与任务特定Individual结构，并自适应分配秩，实现可解释的任务共享与分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩适配器多为单任务且以乘性方式表示权重更新，缺少对跨任务共享信息与任务特定信息的显式区分，难以解释和复用。

Method: 将每个权重更新分解为跨所有任务共享的Joint结构加每任务Individual结构；对Individual施加近正交于Joint的惩罚以分离共享与特定信号；在共享Joint池与任务Individual池间自适应分配秩；Joint可联合学习或增量学习后冻结，作为新任务先验复用。

Result: 在GLUE与SuperGLUE上基于DeBERTaV3-base，在匹配的每任务有效秩下，无需MoE等额外模块即与强单任务及多任务低秩基线相当；当存在相关held-in任务时，冻结Joint可通过复用任务Individual（仅需廉价的逐方向缩放）服务于held-out任务，否则训练小型新Individual。

Conclusion: JIVEAdapter以加性低秩分解实现了共享与任务特定信号的可解释分离与高效复用，在参数高效多任务适配中具有竞争力。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts pretrained models at a fraction of the cost of full fine-tuning, yet most low-rank adapters are single-task and represent each weight update multiplicatively, leaving no explicit account of what is shared across tasks and what is task-specific. We introduce JIVEAdapter, a multi-task "additive" low-rank adapter inspired by statistical Joint and Individual Variation Explained (JIVE). JIVEAdapter decomposes every weight update into a Joint structure shared across all tasks plus a per-task Individual structure, penalizes the Individual structures to be near-orthogonal to the Joint so shared and task-specific signal stay "interpretable" and separated, and allocates rank adaptively across a shared Joint pool and a per-task Individual pool. The Joint is learned once, jointly over a task group or incrementally, one task at a time, then frozen and reused as a prior for new tasks without retraining the shared part. On GLUE and SuperGLUE with DeBERTaV3-base, JIVEAdapter is competitive with strong single-task and multi-task low-rank baselines at a matched per-task effective rank, without extra modules such as MoE, and when a related held-in task exists its frozen Joint serves a held-out task by reusing that task's Individual with only a cheap per-direction scale, otherwise training a small new one.

</details>


### [228] [Inference-Time Projection for Physically Valid Biomolecular Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07037)
*Qurat-ul-ain,Yee Whye Teh,Charlotte M. Deane,Matteo Cagiada*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将物理有效性视为推理期约束问题，提出两个闭式投影算子作用于扩散模型去噪后的干净坐标估计x̂₀，无需重训即可使AF3类共折叠模型输出物理有效的全原子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaFold 3类共折叠模型虽结构精度高，但大量输出物理无效（链间重叠、配体键长键角畸变、环非平面、手性反转）。现有方案要么用物理势引导采样器，导致采样成本与显存开销倍增、大复合物无法推理；要么微调模型，耗时且绑定单一架构。作者注意到物理有效性不同于结构精度，可在推理期从采样器已有量中完全验证。

Method: 把物理有效性当作约束推理问题，在扩散模型去噪后的干净坐标估计x̂₀上施加两个闭式投影算子：（1）链间范德华投影，推开冲突最严重的原子对；（2）配体距离几何投影，恢复键长、键角、内部接触、平面性与手性。两算子均为局部、稀疏、位移受限，无需网络评估、梯度或重要性采样，且不改动去噪器及其权重，可直接嵌入任意AF3风格采样器。

Result: 在Boltz-2与OpenFold-3两个独立模型上，于CASP15、CASP16、PoseBusters单体与复合物集、Boltz物理有效性测试集五个基准上验证：方法恢复完美物理有效性，同时保持结构精度与配体定位指标，运行时与显存开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 该方法是模型无关的实用路线，可在不重训、低成本的前提下实现物理有效的全原子结构预测。

Abstract: AlphaFold 3-style cofolding models predict biomolecular complexes with high structural accuracy, yet a large fraction of their outputs are physically invalid: chains overlap at interfaces, ligand bond lengths and angles are distorted, rings are non-planar, and stereocentres are inverted. Current approaches either steer the sampler with physics-informed potentials, which multiplies sampling cost and memory overhead making inference impossible on large complexes, or finetune the model, costing time and tying the fix to one architecture. We observe that, unlike structural accuracy, physical validity is fully verifiable at inference time from quantities the sampler already holds. We therefore treat physical validity as a constrained inference problem and introduce two closed-form projection operators applied to the diffusion model's denoised clean-coordinate estimate, $\hat{x}_0$: an inter-chain van der Waals projection that pushes apart the most severely clashing atom pairs, and a ligand distance-geometry projection that restores bond lengths, angles, internal contacts, planarity and chirality. Both operators are local, sparse and displacement-capped, require no network evaluations, gradients or importance sampling, and leave the denoiser and its weights untouched, so they can be dropped into any AF3-style sampler without retraining. Applied to two independently developed models, Boltz-2 and OpenFold-3, across five benchmarks (CASP15, CASP16, the PoseBusters monomer and complex sets, and the Boltz physical-validity test set), our method recovers perfect physical validity while preserving structural-accuracy and ligand-placement metrics. These gains are achieved with negligible runtime and memory overhead, providing a practical, model-agnostic route to physically valid all-atom structure prediction.

</details>


### [229] [CuratorMAS: Automating Dataset Curation via Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07075)
*Yixin Zhang,Wenjie Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体协作框架CuratorMAS，将数据策展分解为五个可编程阶段并形成可并行工作流，实现自动化、跨领域的数据集质量评估与筛选，最高降低噪声率36.03个百分点、提升下游F1达8.88个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量数据集是可靠机器学习的必要条件，但数据策展成本高、跨领域泛化难。现有方法依赖人工设计的启发式规则或与模型强相关的信号，难以适配不同任务与用户查询，因而需要自动化的策展方案。

Method: CuratorMAS采用多智能体协作框架，将复杂策展流程拆解为五个可编程执行阶段并组织为可并行工作流：1）数据集勘探，采集文件结构、约束线索等上下文，形成对任务的整体理解；2）从在线来源检索领域知识以增强评估；3）推导评估准则并计算相应指标；4）依据结果执行过滤；5）进化模块总结评估结果并更新相关技能。

Result: 在广泛而全面的实验中，CuratorMAS最高将噪声率降低36.03个百分点（pp），同时使下游模型的F1分数最高提升8.88个百分点（pp）。

Conclusion: 通过多智能体分阶段协作与在线知识增强，CuratorMAS摆脱了对人工启发式和模型依赖信号的束缚，实现了灵活、可泛化的自动化数据策展，在数据质量与下游性能两方面均获得显著提升。

Abstract: High-quality datasets are essential for reliable machine learning, but dataset curation remains costly and hard to generalize across domains. Existing methods typically rely on manually designed heuristics or model-dependent signals, limiting their applicability across tasks and user queries. To address these limitations and automate data curation, we propose \textbf{CuratorMAS}, a multi-agent collaboration framework that orchestrates agents to evaluate and curate high-quality datasets. To achieve the goal of flexible curation, CuratorMAS decomposes the complex curation process into five programmable execution stages and forms a parallelizable workflow. Specifically, CuratorMAS first performs dataset exploration to collect contextual information such as file structures and constraint cues, thereby developing a comprehensive understanding of the given task. In order to acquire up-to-date information, CuratorMAS retrieves domain knowledge from online sources to augment the evaluation process. Next, CuratorMAS derives the necessary evaluation criteria and computes the corresponding metrics. Based on these results, CuratorMAS executes filtering accordingly. Finally, an evolution module summarizes the evaluation outcomes and updates the relevant skills. Extensive and comprehensive experiments demonstrate that CuratorMAS significantly reduces the noise rate by up to 36.03 percentage points (pp) while also improving the F1 score of downstream models by up to 8.88 pp.

</details>


### [230] [Smart Content Ingestion for Generative AI Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07091)
*Abbas Raza Ali,Muhammad Ajmal Siddiqui,Moona Zahid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向企业场景的生产级内容抽取系统，将内容抽取确立为独立生命周期阶段，通过选择性OCR路由、稀缺优先的策展引擎、确定性结构感知分块器及只读检索评估器，实现可配置、可度量的抽取与检索全流程评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI将模型与具体任务解耦，一个基础模型服务开放式下游任务，模型侧的通用性带来数据侧的异质性：企业知识以PDF、演示文稿、表格、扫描件、表单、图表等人类惯用格式撰写，同时携带文本、视觉、几何与结构信息。语言模型或检索器无法对在此接口处被错误表示的信息进行可靠推理，因此内容抽取成为独立环节，其错误无法被下游检索器或重排器修复。

Method: 系统包含四个组件：(1) 选择性OCR路由；(2) 稀缺优先的策展引擎，配基于参考的抽取评分器，衡量字符、词与表格结构准确率；(3) 确定性的结构感知父子分块器；(4) 只读检索评估器，从每页生成有依据的问题并报告Hit@k、MRR与延迟。

Result: 在180篇文档语料上，最佳抽取器得分97.4/100（字符错误率0.13%，表格相似度0.995）；分块器在25,050个生成问题上达到hit@1为68.6%、hit@10为92.8%、MRR为0.77。

Conclusion: 作者提炼出三条设计原则——结构先于语义、绝不修改被测对象、合理预算标注——并将可度量的内容抽取定位为企业级智能体系统的感知层。

Abstract: The evolution of machine learning has progressively changed where intelligence resides in an AI system. In conventional machine learning the task, data representation, labels and model architecture were tightly coupled, so data preparation was narrow, schema-bound and visible. Generative AI decouples the model from any single task: one foundation model serves open-ended downstream tasks, and the generality gained on the model side is matched by heterogeneity on the data side, because enterprise knowledge is authored in the formats people use (PDF, presentations, spreadsheets, scanned documents, forms, tables, diagrams and mixed-layout files) that carry textual, visual, geometric and structural information at once. A language model or retriever cannot reason reliably over information misrepresented at this interface, so content extraction becomes a lifecycle stage in its own right whose errors no downstream retriever or re-ranker can repair. This paper presents a production-ready content-extraction system that makes this stage explicit, configurable, and measurable. The system incorporates selective OCR routing, a scarcity-first curation engine with a reference-based extraction scorer that measures character, word, and table-structure accuracy, a deterministic structure-aware parent-child chunker, and a read-only retrieval evaluator that generates grounded questions from every page and reports Hit@k, mean reciprocal rank, and latency. On a 180-document corpus the best extractor scores 97.4 of 100 (character error rate 0.13%, table similarity 0.995) and the chunker reaches hit@1 of 68.6%, hit@10 of 92.8% and MRR 0.77 over 25,050 generated questions. We distil three design principles (structure before semantics, never mutate what you measure, budget your labels) and position measured content extraction as the perception layer of enterprise agentic systems.

</details>


### [231] [Small Language Models for Smart Data Model Classification at the Edge: A Cost-Aware Hybrid Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07093)
*Cristian Martella,Angelo Martella,Antonella Longo,Motaz Saad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估轻量级开源语言模型在资源受限环境下将IoT数据实体匹配到最合适智能数据模型（SDM）的性能，并对比TF-IDF与轻量句编码器等近零成本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT跨域异构数据源激增，SDM分类对互操作性至关重要，但现有方法资源消耗高、难以适用于算力受限的边缘环境，且文献缺乏轻量、资源高效的解决方案。

Method: 系统基准测试多类轻量级开源LM，涵盖通用型（GP）、推理专用（RS）和代码专用（CS）架构，在多个领域专用数据集上执行数据实体到最佳SDM表示的匹配任务；另设互补实验，将所调研的LLM与TF-IDF及轻量句编码器两种近零成本相似度基线在同一任务上对比。

Result: 在不同模型架构与领域数据集上获得分类性能的系统性基准结果，并通过与近零成本基线对比，为解读边缘平台上LLM分类的实际价值提供参照。

Conclusion: 研究为资源受限场景下的模型选择、任务构建与部署策略提供重要洞见，有助于在边缘环境兼顾准确率与效率，填补轻量资源高效方案的空白。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of heterogeneous data sources within the Internet of Things (IoT) across domains such as smart cities, energy management, and environmental monitoring necessitates efficient and scalable data standardization methods. Effective classification of smart data models (SDMs) is essential for facilitating interoperability. However, existing approaches are often limited by high resource consumption and lack applicability in edge environments with constrained computational capabilities. Aiming to bridge this gap, the proposed study evaluates the performance of lightweight open-source language models (LMs) to resolve an input data entity against its corresponding best fitting SDM representation under resource-constrained conditions. It systematically benchmarks a diverse array of models, including general purpose (GP), reasoning-specialized (RS), and code-specialized (CS) architectures, across multiple domain-specific datasets. Addressing the current omission of lightweight, resource-efficient solutions in the literature, the investigation provides significant and valuable insights into model selection, task formulation, and deployment strategies that optimize accuracy and efficiency. A complementary experiment also compares the surveyed large language models (LLMs) against two near-zero-cost similarity baselines (Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) and a lightweight sentence encoder) on the same task, providing a strong reference point for interpreting the practical value of LLM-based classification on edge platforms.

</details>


### [232] [Verified, not generated: expert-verified AI study materials and the distribution of learning gains in a university course](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07097)
*Canh Thien Dang,An Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在必修经济学课程中，经助教核验的AI生成学习资源使考试成绩平均提高2.34分（满分50），且效应集中在成绩分布底部：低于2:1档线（23–31分）的比例下降24.7个百分点，约四分之三的平均效应来自最低五分位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有生成式AI教育研究多报告平均效应，但实地证据显示AI既可能缩小也可能扩大成绩差距，方向不明。作者认为关键在于"判断负担"——学习者须先自行甄别AI输出的可靠性才能从中学习；若能由可问责的助教在发布前完成专家核验，这一负担便从学生转移到教师。

Method: 采用两期队列的倍差法（difference-in-differences）设计：某大学一年级必修经济学课程中，一半学生获得AI生成的播客、FAQ和测验式学习指南，内容由基于文献来源的模型生成并经具名研究生助教核验（170名学生，340个考试成绩）。同时结合36名学生的访谈与反馈分析学习行为。

Result: 资源获取与50分制组成部分中2.34分的成绩优势相关；低于upper-second档线的成绩比例相对反事实下降24.7个百分点；效应在23–31分的每个阈值上显著，在31分以上均不显著；约四分之三的平均效应来自最低五分位。阈值估计在剔除干预前队列中最低分学生后仍稳健，但平均效应则不再稳健。访谈与反馈显示，核验标签使学生有理由接触AI生成材料，同时未放弃对其内容的审视。

Conclusion: 仅报告平均效应的AI学习资源评估，无法判断资源本应帮助的学生是否真正获益；核验机制将判断负担从学生转向可问责的教师，可在不消解学生批判性审视的前提下缩小成绩差距，因此评估应关注分布性效应而非均值。

Abstract: Experimental studies of generative AI in education mostly report average effects, yet field evidence shows that AI can narrow attainment gaps or widen them. We argue that the direction depends on the judgement burden, the expertise a learner must supply to screen AI output before learning from it, and that expert verification before release moves this burden from students to an accountable tutor. We test the argument in a two-cohort difference-in-differences design in which one half of a compulsory firstyear university economics course received AI-generated podcasts, FAQs and quiz-based study guides, produced with a source-grounded model and checked by a named graduate teaching assistant (170 students; 340 examination marks). Access was associated with a 2.34-mark advantage on a 50-mark component. The share of marks below the upper-second classification boundary fell by 24.7 percentage points relative to the counterfactual, effects were significant at every threshold from 23 to 31 marks and at none above, and roughly three-quarters of the average originated in the bottom quintile. The threshold estimate is robust to removing the lowest-scoring students from the pre-intervention cohort; the average effect is not. Interviews and feedback from 36 students indicate that the verification label gave students a reason to engage with AI-generated material without ending their scrutiny of it. Evaluations of AI learning resources that report only mean effects cannot detect whether the students the resources are meant to help are the ones who gain.

</details>


### [233] [When to Remember, When to Abstain: Category-Conditioned Retention for Reliable Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07100)
*Olukunle Owolabi,Pulkit Gupta,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究智能体长期记忆的写入（保留）决策机制：用按语义类别条件化的置信度阈值替代单一全局阈值，并在100个合成人格的冷启动记忆流水线上验证其可降低无支撑断言保留率并提升覆盖度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化记忆的可靠性取决于保留决策本身：由来源弱支撑的断言可能被写入并在后续被当作既定事实复用。不同语义类别的断言在证据可靠性上存在显著不对称（价值观/信念类仅77.9%被来源支撑，其他类别达96.2%），而全局置信阈值无法区分二者——要么纳入无支撑的价值观断言，要么丢弃证据充分的断言。因此需要探究保留决策是否应由按语义类别条件化的置信门槛来支配，对证据充分的类别宽松保留，对推断不可靠的类别更积极地弃权。

Method: 在已部署的冷启动记忆流水线上、针对100个合成人格进行评估，统计4,715条候选断言的来源支撑率。将保留阈值按断言语义类别进行条件化：对价值观类断言设置更严格的置信门槛。采用重复的留出（held-out）评估，比较类别条件化阈值与单一全局阈值在同等保留水平下的表现。

Result: 仅对价值观类施加更严格门槛，可将无支撑保留比例从6.2%降至4.0%，相对减少约36%（幅度有限但跨折一致）；作为佐证，在可比保留率下，类别条件化阈值比全局阈值多保留约13个百分点的覆盖度（95% CI 9.8–16.0）。

Conclusion: 可靠的记忆保留取决于断言类型而非仅取决于置信度；按类别条件化的阈值可作为写入边界上一种简单而有效的选择性预测（selective prediction）形式。

Abstract: Persistent agent memory is only as reliable as its retention decision: an assertion weakly supported by its source can be stored and later reused as established fact. We study whether the retention decision should be governed by a confidence bar conditioned on the semantic category of the assertion rather than by a single global threshold, retaining well-evidenced categories liberally while abstaining more aggressively where inference is unreliable. We evaluate this in a deployed cold-start memory pipeline on 100 synthetic personas. The empirical evaluation is motivated by a sharp reliability asymmetry: across 4{,}715 candidate assertions, only 77.9\% of value and belief assertions are supported by their source, versus 96.2\% for all other categories. A global confidence threshold cannot separate these: it either admits unsupported value claims or discards well-evidenced ones. Conditioning the threshold on category resolves the tradeoff. In repeated held-out evaluation, a stricter bar on values alone reduces unsupported retentions from 6.2\% to 4.0\% (an ${\approx}36\%$ relative reduction, modest but consistent across folds) and, as corroborating evidence, preserves an estimated 13 percentage points more coverage (95\% CI 9.8--16.0) than a global threshold at comparable retention. Our results suggest that reliable retention depends on the type of assertion, not on confidence alone, and that a category-conditioned threshold can act as a simple, effective form of selective prediction at the write boundary.

</details>


### [234] [AMBER: Training Long-Horizon Web Agents through Append-Only Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07118)
*Chinmay Savadikar,Zhaoyu Zhang,Mingyu Zhao,Shuang Xie,Han Li,Tianfu Wu,Lingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AMBER——一种仅追加式记忆库框架，让智能体通过强化学习端到端学习推理、行动与写入自由形式记忆，仅追加规则从构造上保证信息保留。在WebArena Lite上平均成功率较覆写式记忆提升4.09个百分点，五次重复运行中任务解决比例提升4.8个百分点，并匹配需更多昂贵人工监督训练的覆写基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体在长时程、多步交互中，交互历史迅速超出上下文预算，需在长时程中保持事实信息、记住执行错误与纠正反馈并跟踪进度。已有方法（推理与行动历史、覆写式固定大小记忆、周期性摘要）各有局限：覆写式记忆原则上可保留任何信息，但需学会在每次重写中携带每条事实，从稀疏结果奖励中难以学习；在Web智能体等交互应用中，训练后的覆写记忆会删除轨迹所需关键信息及环境给出的纠正反馈。

Method: AMBER（Append-only Memory Bank for Evidence Retention）：一个简单且可扩展的框架。智能体联合学习推理、行动与写入自由形式记忆，而仅追加（append-only）规则从构造上保证信息保留。因此AMBER可仅用结果奖励通过强化学习端到端训练，无需大量精心策划的SFT数据。

Result: 在WebArena Lite上，AMBER相较基于覆写式记忆的方法：平均成功率提升4.09个百分点；五次重复运行中被解决任务的比例提升4.8个百分点；并匹配了使用显著更昂贵人工监督训练的覆写基线。同时AMBER在保持实用token预算的情况下取得这些改进。

Conclusion: AMBER在上下文效率、任务性能与可靠长时程执行之间取得了良好平衡，仅追加式记忆结合端到端强化学习可有效替代覆写式记忆与昂贵的人工监督数据，为长时程智能体记忆提供了一种简单、可扩展且高性价比的方案。

Abstract: Modern language-model agents increasingly interact with external environments over long-horizon, multi-step trajectories, where the accumulated interaction history can quickly exceed practical context budgets. To ensure reliability, agents must maintain factual information over long horizons, remember execution errors and corrective feedback, and track progress across actions. Several approaches have been proposed to achieve this without the need for maintaining the entire execution history in context, such as using the reasoning and action history, learning to maintain a fixed-size memory through an overwrite mechanism, and periodic summarization. Although overwrite memory can in principle retain anything an append-only memory can, it must learn to carry each fact through every subsequent rewrite, which is difficult to learn from sparse outcome rewards; for interactive applications like web agents, we find that trained overwrite memories delete key information required by the trajectory, as well as corrective feedback received from the environment. We introduce AMBER (Append-only Memory Bank for Evidence Retention) - a simple and scalable framework where an agent jointly learns to reason, act, and write free-form memory, while an append-only rule guarantees retention by construction. This allows AMBER to be trained end-to-end with reinforcement learning from outcome rewards without the need for extensive curated SFT data. On WebArena Lite, AMBER improves average success over overwrite-based memory by 4.09 percentage points, increases the fraction of tasks solved in five repeated runs by 4.8 percentage points, and matches an overwrite baseline trained on substantially more expensive curated supervision. AMBER achieves these improvements while maintaining a practical token budget, providing a strong balance between context efficiency, task performance, and reliable long-horizon execution.

</details>


### [235] [Is this machine playing?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07130)
*Nathan Cloos,Antonio Norelli,Daniel Durbin,Jacob Andreas,Daniela Rus,Phillip Isola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将现代AI编程助手置于未知数字岛屿中作为虚拟身体的控制心智，仅给予不含具体任务、奖励或活动的极简指令。在30小时运行中，具身智能体表现出爬山、堆叠方块、绘制曼陀罗、重新诠释运动、进行物理实验及习得技能等自发行为，13个智能体间行为模式重复但历史轨迹各异。研究检验其是否满足经典"玩耍"标准，并探讨玩耍能否成为机器发展的一种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI在无明确任务与奖励条件下是否会自发产生类似"玩耍"的开放性行为，并检验该行为是否满足经典玩耍判据，进而追问玩耍可否作为机器自身发展的机制。

Method: 将AI编程助手作为数字岛屿中虚拟身体的心智，仅输入提及无具体任务、奖励或活动的极简指令；开展长达30小时的运行，观察13个具身智能体的行为；检验其行为是否符合经典玩耍标准。

Result: 智能体自发开展爬山、堆叠方块筑塔、绘制曼陀罗、重新诠释运动、对其世界物理规律做实验，并习得可扩展后续能力的技能；这些活动在13个智能体间重复出现，但各自演化为不同的行为历史。

Conclusion: 无任务、无奖励驱动的具身AI仍可展现类玩耍的开放行为，且行为具有跨智能体的可重复性与个体差异性；研究据此讨论玩耍是否能成为机器发展的一种模式。

Abstract: We placed a modern AI coding assistant in an unintended role: as the mind of a body on an unknown digital island. With only a minimal instruction mentioning no specific task, reward, or activity, the machine started animating its virtual body. Across thirty-hour runs, the embodied AI agent climbed hills, stacked blocks into towers, drew mandalas, reinterpreted sports, ran experiments on the physics of its world, and learned techniques that later expanded what it could accomplish. These activities recurred across thirteen agents but diverged into distinct histories. We examine whether this behavior satisfies classical criteria for play and ask whether play can become a mode of machine development.

</details>


### [236] [Sim-to-Real Transfer of Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments Using an Ackermann-Steered Mobile Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07192)
*Chalindu Abeywansa,Sahan Gunasekara,Devindi De Silva,Seniru Dissanayake,Ranga Rodrigo,Peshala Jayasekara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该工作面向连续环境下的视觉语言导航（VLN），通过跨模态注意力（CMA）架构与仿真到现实（sim-to-real）迁移，使Ackermann转向机器人无需导航图与全景视图即可依据自然语言指令离线导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VLN模型依赖导航图、360度全景视图与精确定位，难以适应真实场景。作者希望将可在连续环境中运行、不依赖导航图与全景视图的VLN方法迁移到真实世界，以提升人机交互式导航的实用性。

Method: 提出集成视觉语言模型的系统，在共享嵌入空间中对齐视觉输入与语言指令；采用基于跨模态注意力（CMA）的架构，先在仿真环境已有数据集上训练，再在自建Ackermann转向机器人（配备相机与LiDAR）采集的真实数据上微调。迁移策略包括线性光度调整（photometric adjustment）与少量episode微调，并在专用硬件上离线运行。

Result: 模型成功适应真实环境并实现有效导航；以SPL（Success weighted by Path Length）与nDTW（Normalized Dynamic Time Warping）为指标评估，实验结果表明该方法具有鲁棒性与可适应性。

Conclusion: 通过CMA架构结合光度调整与少量真实数据微调，可在不依赖导航图、全景视图与完美定位的条件下完成连续环境VLN的sim-to-real迁移，并在真实机器人上离线部署取得有效导航表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) enables robots to navigate through environments using natural language instructions, making human-robot interaction intuitive. Traditional VLN models often rely on navigation graphs, 360-degree views, and perfect localization which pose significant challenges when adapting these models to real-world settings. This work addresses these limitations by performing a simulation-to-real domain shift of a VLN approach that operates in continuous environments without requiring navigation graphs or panoramic views. The proposed system integrates vision-language models that align visual inputs and linguistic instructions within a shared embedding space, facilitating natural language-driven navigation.
