Method: 设计全新文本游戏作为任务，规则新颖或反直觉，迫使智能体通过交互而非预训练知识获取信息；提供可复现反馈与自动评分，支持跨多次尝试的受控评估；并通过变换游戏实例，测试智能体能否将所学迁移到新情境。在此基准上系统考察骨干模型、自演化方法与智能体框架（harness）对学习能力的影响。

Result: 得出三项发现：(1) 经验保留：完整保留动作与反馈记录比将其总结为规则或策略更有助于有效学习；(2) 人机差距：顶尖人类玩家峰值分数高于被测智能体，人类探索策略更多样、重复动作更少；(3) 框架作用：骨干模型固定时，更换 harness 可在降低预估推理成本的同时提升性能。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 LLM 智能体从经验中学习的关键规律，为理解其学习机制及未来提升学习能力的研究提供了方向与可复现的评测基础。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for LLM agents to adapt to unfamiliar and dynmaic environments. Evaluating this ability is therefore important for understanding how effectively agents acquire and use new knowledge. Existing benchmarks have sought to evaluate this ability, but they primarily evaluate tasks whose rules are provided in the instructions or already familiar to pretrained models, making it difficult to distinguish learning from interactions from reasoning with existing knowledge. To address this, we introduce Learn2Play Bench, a benchmark of newly designed text-based games, whose rules are novel or counterintuitive, requiring agents to acquire knowledge through interaction rather than rely solely on pretrained knowledge. These games provide reproducible feedback and automatic scoring, enabling controlled evaluation of learning across repeated attempts. We also vary game instances to test whether agents can apply what they have learned to new situations. Therefore, we evaluate how backbone models, self-evolving methods, and agent harnesses affect agents' learning ability, revealing three findings: (1) Experience retention: Retaining complete records of actions and feedback can support more effective learning than summarizing these experiences into rules or strategies. (2) Human agent gap: Top-performing human players achieve higher peak scores than the evaluated agents. Human explore more varied strategies, and repeat actions less. (3) Harness matters: With the backbone fixed, changing the harness can improve performance while reducing estimated inference cost. Together, these findings provide insights into how LLM agents learn from experience and suggest directions for future work to improve their learning ability. Project website: https://liushiliushi.github.io/learn2play-bench-website/

</details>


### [337] [Quantum Entangled Multimodal Fusion Networks (QEMFN): Resource-Aware Hybrid Vision-Language Fusion via Trainable Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08216)
*Srikar Alla,Ali Shiri Sichani,Chi-Ren Shyu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出QEMFN——一种混合量子-经典的多模态融合框架，将可训练纠缠作为结构化归纳偏置引入视觉-语言融合，在参数量匹配与冻结CLIP骨干下于COCO-5k和Flickr30k检索任务上优于多种经典融合基线，并给出系统的量子中心化评估与真实硬件执行结果（不主张量子计算优势）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态视觉-语言系统通常使用拼接、注意力、双线性池化或张量交互等经典算子融合图像与文本嵌入，缺乏对融合结构中"纠缠式关联"这一归纳偏置的显式建模；作者希望探索将可训练量子纠缠作为结构化归纳偏置用于多模态融合的可行性与可解释性。

Method: 构建混合量子-经典框架QEMFN：将预训练视觉与文本特征投影到紧凑潜在空间，编码为角度参数化量子态；经模态内与成对跨模态纠缠电路处理，测量后得到融合表示用于检索。在参数预算匹配、CLIP骨干冻结一致的条件下，与MLP、张量融合、FiLM、交叉注意力、紧凑Transformer及去量子化的成对拓扑对照模型进行比较。消融实验分离量子模块与经典投影的贡献；量子中心分析报告Meyer-Wallach纠缠能力、可表达性、相对贫瘠高原界的梯度方差，以及在控制训练进度条件下的熵-性能相关性，并对纠缠组件进行干预研究。执行环境包含基于采样（shot-based）估计、噪声建模的模拟后端，以及采用零噪声外推的真实超导设备。

Result: 在参数量匹配与相同冻结CLIP骨干下，QEMFN在COCO-5k与Flickr30k检索任务上超越经典融合基线（含MLP、张量融合、FiLM、交叉注意力、紧凑Transformer与去量子化成对拓扑对照）。消融实验验证量子模块的独立贡献；量子中心指标显示可训练的纠缠能力、可表达性及受控条件下熵与性能的相关性，且梯度方差未超出贫瘠高原界。方法可在采样估计、噪声模拟后端及真实超导设备（配合零噪声外推）上执行。

Conclusion: 论文不主张量子计算优势，其贡献在于提出该融合框架并提供受控的实证与量子中心化评估，将可训练纠缠定位为一种可解释、可在当代量子设备上硬件执行的融合机制，且适用于现有设备可达的规模。

Abstract: Multimodal vision-language systems typically fuse image and text embeddings through classical operators such as concatenation, attention, bilinear pooling, or tensor interactions. We propose Quantum Entangled Multimodal Fusion Networks (QEMFN), a hybrid quantum-classical framework that introduces parameterized entanglement as a structured inductive bias for multimodal fusion. Pretrained visual and textual features are projected into compact latent spaces, encoded as angle-parameterized quantum states, processed through intra-modal and paired cross-modal entangling circuits, and measured to produce fused representations for retrieval. Under matched parameter budgets and identical frozen CLIP backbones, QEMFN outperforms classical fusion baselines on COCO-5k and Flickr30k, including multilayer perceptron, tensor fusion, FiLM, cross-attention, compact transformer, and a dequantized paired-topology analogue. An ablation suite isolates the quantum module's contribution from the surrounding classical projections, and quantum-centric analyses report Meyer-Wallach entangling capability, expressibility, gradient variance against barren-plateau bounds, and entropy-performance correlation under controls for training progress alongside an intervention study on the entangling component. QEMFN is executed under shot-based estimation, a noise-modeled fake backend, and a real superconducting device with zero-noise extrapolation. This work does not claim quantum computational advantage; the contribution is the framework together with a controlled empirical and quantum-centric evaluation that positions trainable entanglement as an interpretable, hardware-executable fusion mechanism at scales accessible on contemporary devices.

</details>


### [338] [Confidence-Ordering Reversal under Contextual Priors in Neural Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08229)
*Xinyu Zhang,Sichao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示：上下文先验在提升神经到语言解码的同时，会使融合后的置信度产生"置信度排序反转"现象，导致残留错误反而更自信，因此应分别保留局部与先验证据来评估置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上下文先验通过重塑候选分数来改善神经到语言解码，但置信度也正是从这些被重塑的分数中读取的。先验遗留的错误可能在准确率不变的情况下变得更"自信"，从而掩盖问题。作者希望研究先验如何塑造置信度，以及如何更可靠地衡量融合后的预测可信度。

Method: 在 MEG-MASC 与 MOUS 上研究语音检索：使用局部解码分数、通过加性浅融合（additive shallow fusion）结合的上下文先验，并以融合后的 top-two margin 作为置信度；作者提出分数层面的解释，并通过因果干预（仅改变融合权重）来验证。

Result: 在初始错误预测中发现置信度排序反转：当正确候选初始排名靠前时，较大 margin 更易修复；排名较低时则更不易修复。MEG-MASC 上整体正确性 AUROC 为 0.87，但区分"修复"与"残留错误"的 AUROC 从初始排名 2-3 时的 0.70 降至排名 21-50 时的 0.39。排名 20 以外（反转区）的错误占融合后总错误的 46.6%。因果干预改变融合权重可使反转按预期移向更深排名；在词级 LM 先验下，即使准确率增益已见顶，反转仍继续移动。分别读取局部与先验分数可改进选择性解码：回答窗口比例由 56.7% 提升至 74.5%，同时 92% 的输出集仍包含正确候选。

Conclusion: 上下文融合后的置信度应同时保留每个预测背后的局部证据与上下文证据，而非仅依赖融合后的分数；单一为准确率选择的融合权重并不能确定置信度的可靠性。

Abstract: Contextual priors improve neural-to-language decoding by reshaping candidate scores. However, confidence is read from the same reshaped scores, so the errors a prior leaves behind can become more confident with no change in accuracy to reveal it. We study how a prior shapes confidence in speech retrieval on MEG-MASC and MOUS using local decoding scores, a contextual prior combined by additive shallow fusion, and the fused top-two margin as confidence. Among initially incorrect predictions, we find a confidence-ordering reversal: a larger margin makes a repair more likely when the correct candidate starts near the top of the local ranking, but less likely when it starts lower. On MEG-MASC, pooled correctness AUROC is 0.87, yet AUROC separating repairs from residual errors falls from 0.70 at initial ranks 2-3 to 0.39 at ranks 21-50. Errors starting beyond rank 20, inside the reversed region, make up 46.6% of all post-fusion errors. We propose a score-level account: a repair must first close the correct candidate's initial deficit, limiting its final margin, whereas a residual error can build a large margin between two incorrect candidates. A causal intervention that changes only the fusion weight moves the reversal to deeper ranks as predicted. Under a word-level LM prior, it keeps moving after accuracy gain peaks, so a weight chosen for accuracy does not settle confidence. Reading local and prior scores separately improves selective decoding: the decoder answers on 74.5% of windows instead of 56.7%, while 92% of output sets still contain the correct candidate. Confidence after contextual fusion should retain the local and contextual evidence behind each prediction, not just the fused scores. Project website: https://confidencereversal.github.io/; Code: https://github.com/AmadeusFake/NeuDecodingConfReversal

</details>


### [339] [OSFP4: Joint Optimization of Diagonal Smoothing and Block Scales for NVFP4 Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08231)
*Neriah Ben David,Ori Meir,Or Ordentlich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OSFP4（Optimized Smoothing and Scaling for NVFP4），通过对每层线性投影的对角平滑矩阵与块缩放因子进行联合优化，最小化NVFP4下矩阵乘量化的平方误差，在保持约94-97%厂商NVFP4预填充吞吐的同时取得最优平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVFP4虽具备紧凑存储与原生张量核加速优势，适合LLM推理，但要在该数据类型下保持精度需要精细的量化方案，现有方法难以兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 提出OSFP4量化方案：对每个线性投影使用对角平滑矩阵，其元素在考虑舍入方式（最近邻舍入或GPTQ式连续干扰消除）的前提下进行优化，以最小化NVFP4下的矩阵乘量化平方误差；同时借助对"乘性抖动FP4量化器"（multiplicative-dither FP4 quantizer）的分析，实现对平滑元素与块缩放因子的联合优化。

Result: 在对应量化设置下，OSFP4在所评估的对比方法中取得最高平均精度，同时在实际测量负载上保留了约94-97%的厂商NVFP4预填充吞吐。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化平滑与缩放并引入乘性抖动分析，OSFP4在NVFP4低比特量化下实现了精度与效率的良好平衡，是一种有实用价值的LLM推理量化方案。

Abstract: NVFP4 is an attractive datatype for large language model (LLM) inference, offering compact storage and native tensor-core acceleration. However, preserving accuracy using NVFP4 requires careful quantization. In this work we develop a novel quantization scheme called Optimized Smoothing and Scaling for NVFP4 (OSFP4). For each linear projection it uses a diagonal smoothing matrix whose entries are optimized to minimize the squared matrix-product quantization error under NVFP4, taking into account the rounding procedure that is used (either round-to-nearest, or GPTQ-style successive interference cancellation). This requires performing joint optimization on the smoothing entries as well as the block scales, which is facilitated by analyzing a multiplicative-dither FP4 quantizer instead of the fixed deterministic one. Experiments show that OSFP4 achieves the highest average accuracy among the evaluated competitors in the corresponding quantization settings, while retaining approximately 94-97\% of vendor NVFP4 prefill throughput on the measured workloads. Our code is available in https://github.com/neriahbd/OSFP4

</details>


### [340] [Sensor-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08244)
*Yuekai Xu,Zitao Shuai,Yuzhe Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Sensor-Language-Action (SLA)建模范式，将多模态传感器观测、自然语言与动作统一到一个模型中，并构建大规模基准与OpenSLA统一模型，在临床预测、手术室与代谢健康任务上超越现有最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器模型大多止步于感知层面：仅识别状态或预测结果，而动作建模则被分离处理，依赖任务特定且往往是封闭的标签空间，缺乏传感器证据与动作之间的统一语义联系。

Method: 提出SLA建模范式，以语言作为感知与行动之间的语义接口，使异构动作可被表示、预测和解释，同时保持与底层传感器证据的关联。构建大规模SLA基准，覆盖超116,000名个体、79种传感器模态和60个动作组，并设计多维度描述（captioning）流水线以对齐用户上下文、传感器动态与动作证据。基于此提出OpenSLA统一模型，支持分层动作预测、状态理解与动作解释。

Result: 在临床预测、手术室和代谢健康等真实世界任务上，OpenSLA较当前最优方法表现更优；并展现出语言引导的证据定位能力以及对未见动作与人群的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: SLA为连接感知与行动提供了统一建模范式，语言作为语义接口具有可行性与有效性；OpenSLA验证了该框架在多领域真实任务中的优越性能与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Sensors are useful not only for understanding the world but also for deciding what to do next. Existing sensor models however largely stop at perception: they recognize states or predict outcomes, leaving actions modeled separately through task-specific and often closed label spaces. We introduce Sensor-Language-Action (SLA) modeling, a framework that connects multimodal sensor observations, natural language, and actions within a unified model. SLA uses language as a semantic interface between sensing and acting, allowing heterogeneous actions to be represented, predicted, and explained while remaining grounded in the underlying sensor evidence. We build a large-scale SLA benchmark consisting of datasets that span more than 116,000 individuals, 79 sensor modalities, and 60 action groups, together with a multi-faceted captioning pipeline that aligns user context, sensor dynamics, and action evidence. Building on this framework, we present OpenSLA, a unified SLA model for hierarchical action prediction, state understanding, and action explanation. Extensive experiments on real-world tasks in clinical prediction, operating rooms, and metabolic health verify its superior performance over the state-of-the-art. OpenSLA also demonstrates intriguing capabilities including language-guided evidence grounding and zero-shot generalization to unseen actions and cohorts.

</details>


### [341] [LeanPlan: Optimal Planning with LLM-Generated Heuristics and Admissibility Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08246)
*André G. Pereira,Augusto B. Corrêa,Felipe Meneguzzi,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanPlan 是首个利用 LLM 生成、并经机器验证可采纳性的启发式函数来求解最优规划的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 LLM 能生成启发式函数以在满足性规划（任意可行计划均可接受）中引导搜索并取得领先性能，但这类启发式不保证可采纳性，可能导致次优计划。因此需要一种能保证最优性的方案。

Method: 给定领域描述与训练任务，采用智能体循环，利用规划器反馈迭代改进可复用的领域特定启发式、其可采纳性证明及所需的领域假设；在 Lean 4 中实现该启发式、其证明以及具备机器验证的接地与搜索的高效规划器。

Result: 在国际规划竞赛的十个领域及三个新领域上评估，测试任务的对象数量最多为训练任务的 57 倍。使用 GPT-5.6 Sol 进行智能体循环，成功为所有领域生成启发式及其可采纳性证明。所得启发式下，LeanPlan 通常比最先进的 Scorpion 规划器扩展更少状态，且总体求解任务更多。

Conclusion: LeanPlan 首次实现了以机器验证可采纳性的 LLM 生成启发式来求最优规划，在可扩展性与求解能力上优于当前最先进规划器。

Abstract: Frontier large language models (LLMs) can generate heuristic functions that guide search to achieve state-of-the-art performance in satisficing planning, where any plan is acceptable. However, these heuristics are not guaranteed to be admissible and can lead to suboptimal plans. We introduce LeanPlan, the first planning system that finds optimal plans with LLM-generated heuristics whose admissibility is machine-checked. Given a domain description and training tasks, an agentic loop uses planner feedback to iteratively improve a reusable domain-specific heuristic, its admissibility proof and the required domain assumptions. LeanPlan implements the heuristic, its proof and an efficient planner with machine-checked grounding and search in Lean 4. We evaluate LeanPlan on ten domains from the International Planning Competition and three new domains, using test tasks with up to 57 times as many objects as the training tasks. With GPT-5.6 Sol in the agentic loop, we successfully generate heuristics and admissibility proofs for all these domains. With the resulting heuristics, LeanPlan usually expands fewer states than the state-of-the-art Scorpion planner and solves more tasks overall.

</details>


### [342] [MASC: A Multi-Agent Self-Calibration Framework with Latent Construct Alignment for Consistent Client Role-Playing in Psychological Counseling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08250)
*Shixin Peng,Kun Jiang,Jiaxing Zheng,Qihao Yang,Jingying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MASC多智能体自校准框架与CRPC-Bench基准，用于心理咨询中保持一致性的来访者角色扮演模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益被用于来访者模拟以支持咨询师培训与心理研究，但可靠模拟要求来访者在长程交互中保持心理一致性；现有角色扮演方法依赖静态画像提示，易出现人设漂移、不真实的合作性以及心理状态、沟通行为与情绪的不一致，且缺乏统一评测平台。

Method: MASC采用多智能体自校准框架，包含潜构念对齐，融合构念引导生成、协作式精修、一致性验证与基于记忆的修订，形成闭环校准机制，在对话展开过程中检测并纠正不一致。同时构建CRPC-Bench基准，涵盖会话级画像信息与大五人格特质，以及轮级心理状态、沟通行为与情绪表达，包含38个动机式访谈来访者画像并附人格与情绪标注。

Result: 实验表明MASC在画像、人格、接纳度与轮级一致性上均优于现有方法，其中异构配置取得最佳整体表现。

Conclusion: MASC与CRPC-Bench为AI辅助咨询研究与培训中开发、评估心理一致的来访者模拟提供了统一基础。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate clients for counselor training and psychological counseling research, but reliable simulation requires clients to remain psychologically coherent across extended interactions. Existing role-playing methods largely rely on static profile prompts and may exhibit persona drift, unrealistic cooperativeness, or inconsistent psychological states, communicative actions, and emotions. Existing evaluations also lack a unified testbed for both stable client characteristics and evolving psychological dynamics. We propose MASC, a Multi-Agent Self-Calibration framework with latent construct alignment for consistent client role-playing in psychological counseling. MASC combines construct-guided generation, collaborative refinement, consistency verification, and memory-based revision in a closed calibration loop that detects and corrects inconsistencies as dialogue unfolds. We further introduce CRPC-Bench, a benchmark covering session-level profile information and Big-Five personality traits, as well as turn-level psychological state, communicative action, and emotion expression. CRPC-Bench contains 38 motivational interviewing client profiles augmented with personality and emotion annotations. Experiments show that MASC outperforms existing methods across profile, personality, receptivity, and turn-level consistency, with the heterogeneous configuration achieving the strongest overall performance. MASC and CRPC-Bench provide a unified foundation for developing and evaluating psychologically coherent client simulations for AI-assisted counseling research and training.

</details>


### [343] [zkLLMPoT: Efficient Zero Knowledge Proof of Training for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08258)
*Junkai Liang,Zhanpeng Guo,Pengfei Wu,Qingni Shen,Jiaheng Zhang,Zhonghai Wu,Haiyang Xue,Shengfang Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出零知识框架 zkLLMPoT，通过对训练后检查点进行前向评估（而非验证优化轨迹）来认证审计者指定的属性，使证明成本与训练迭代次数无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型权重与训练数据均为私有，审计大语言模型训练声称的结果非常困难；在 Transformer 规模上对整个训练过程进行密码学证明则代价高得难以承受。

Method: zkLLMPoT 分两阶段：1）训练者固定架构并承诺模型权重，随后审计者选择挑战序列，防止训练者针对审计数据修改检查点；2）训练者证明该已承诺模型在这些序列上达到的目标值。方法基于 sumcheck 与 lookup 的论证来认证 Transformer 计算，并支持下一 token 损失与任务特定审计目标。

Result: 在四个模型家族上，算子级基准测试显示：1.1–1.5B 参数模型证明时间为 41–59 秒，13B 模型在覆盖算子上为 131 秒；序列长度 512 时验证时间低于半秒。

Conclusion: 该框架无需泄露模型权重、无需访问私有训练数据，即可实现与训练迭代次数无关的认证成本，为 LLM 训练结果的隐私保护审计提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Auditing the claimed outcomes of large language model (LLM) training is challenging when model weights and training data are private, while cryptographically proving the full training process is prohibitively expensive at Transformer scale. We present zkLLMPoT, a zero-knowledge framework that certifies auditor-defined properties of a trained checkpoint through forward evaluation rather than verification of its optimization trajectory. zkLLMPoT includes 2 phases: 1) The trainer fixes the architecture and the model weights are committed. Then the auditor selects challenge sequences, preventing the trainer from modifying the checkpoint in response to the audit data. 2) Then the trainer proves the objective value attained by the committed model on those sequences. This formulation makes the certification cost independent of the number of training iterations, without revealing model weights or requiring access to private training data. We build on sumcheck- and lookup-based arguments to certify Transformer computations, while supporting next-token loss and task-specific audit objectives. Across four model families, operator-level benchmarks yield proving times of 41-59 seconds for 1.1-1.5B-parameter models and 131 seconds at 13B for the covered operators, with verification below half a second at a sequence length of 512.

</details>


### [344] [The Standardization Trap: Certifying Joint Label Processing in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08314)
*Duong Nguyen,Nicolas Chesneau,Milan Bhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对预训练表格基础模型（TFM）是否遵循"固定权重"（线性回归/核平滑）解释提出检验方法，发现五个公开TFM均存在"联合处理"行为，即改变一个上下文标签会改变其他标签对预测的影响，且该行为随训练出现、主要由注意力分数承载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性回归与核平滑为上下文学习提供了可解释的固定权重框架：特征决定各上下文标签的权重。但该固定权重解释是否适用于预训练表格基础模型（TFM）尚不明确。直接用导数检验会陷入"标准化陷阱"——公开TFM包在模型看到标签前会先标准化，而普通导数还反映标准化标签集之外的行为，导致即使模型所有预测都符合固定权重映射，也会被误判为非线性。

Method: 提出两种证书（certificates），仅依赖标准化标签处的预测值，可分别拒绝两类解释：(1) 固定权重预测；(2) 独立非线性标签变换之和。在五个公开TFM上进行评估，并进一步分析联合处理与训练阶段、注意力分数之间的关系。

Result: 在评估的五个公开TFM上，证书显示改变一个上下文标签会改变其他标签对预测的影响，即存在"联合处理"（joint processing）。进一步发现联合处理随训练而出现，且注意力分数承载了大部分测得的交互。

Conclusion: 固定权重解释不足以刻画预训练表格基础模型的上下文学习行为；需要能解释"上下文标签如何改变单个样本影响力"的新解释框架。

Abstract: Linear regression and kernel smoothing offer tractable explanations of in-context learning: in both, the features determine the weight assigned to each context label. However, whether this fixed-weight account describes pretrained tabular foundation models (TFMs) remains unclear. Testing this account using derivatives runs into a standardization trap: public TFM packages standardize the labels before the model sees them, yet ordinary derivatives also reflect behavior outside the set of standardized labels, making a model appear nonlinear even when every prediction it makes agrees with a fixed-weight map. We propose two certificates that depend only on predictions at standardized labels and can reject two distinct explanations: fixed-weight prediction and sums of independent nonlinear label transformations. Across the five public TFMs that we evaluate, our certificates show that changing one context label alters how other labels influence the prediction, a behavior we call joint processing. We further find that joint processing emerges with training and that attention scores carry most of the measured interaction. Together, these findings motivate TFM explanations that account for how context labels change the influence of individual examples.

</details>


### [345] [SCOPE: Certified Theorem Proving with a Language Model as the Policy Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08319)
*Hanchao Zhou,Jialei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SCOPE，将形式化证明生成分解为算子规划、符号数值执行与编译渲染，用小模型在Lean多步数值命题上取得高认证率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Lean等证明助手中，证明需通过机器编译检查，评估无需人工打分。但直接生成在多步数值命题上失效：证明有效的必要条件是每个内容整数都正确，因此通过率受单整数准确率的k次方约束，呈幂律上限。跨2,617条参考证明的控制性破坏实验证实了该幂律。作者由此认为，限制自由生成、保持决策时信息可见，比扩大模型规模更直接有效。

Method: SCOPE（State-Conditioned Operator Planning and Execution）强制自然分工：模型仅在算子词表上进行规划；符号引擎执行数值计算；编译器将结果渲染成证明。另有两项关键设计：以当前引擎状态替代决策帧中的滞后引擎状态；并考察链末端损失加权的影响。

Result: 在218题测试集上，135M骨干模型认证191/218（87.6%）；7B的DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5-RL仅认证18/218，token消耗为27.5倍、墙钟时间为37.5倍；DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B在双向对偶测试集上认证为零。多步思考平均每题需6.12次离散决策动作，且不产生自然语言思考文本。用当前引擎状态替换滞后状态使通过率从117/218升至191/218；对链末端损失加权反而有害。在公开Lean-Workbook库的3,536道可评分可用题中认证2,132道（60.29%），主测试集无性能回退。所有读数来自版本冻结评审，含独立复核与反向验证。

Conclusion: 限制自由生成并保持决策时信息可见，是比扩大模型规模更直接的有效路径；小型模型配合规划-执行-渲染分工即可在机器可验证的数值证明任务上显著超越大模型。

Abstract: In proof assistants such as Lean, a generated proof must pass machine compilation checks, so evaluation needs no human scoring. Direct generation fails on multi-step numeric propositions: a proof is valid only if every content integer is correct, so the pass rate is bounded by the k-th power of the per-integer accuracy. Controlled corruption across 2,617 reference proofs confirms this power law. SCOPE (State-Conditioned Operator Planning and Execution) enforces the natural division of labor: the model plans over an operator vocabulary, a symbolic engine executes the numerics, and a compiler renders the proof. On a 218-problem suite it certifies 191/218 (87.6%) with a 135M backbone; the 7B DeepSeek-Prover-V1.5-RL certifies 18/218 at 27.5 times the tokens and 37.5 times the wall-clock, and DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B certifies zero on a bidirectional dual suite. Multi-step thinking costs 6.12 discrete decision actions per problem and produces no natural-language thinking text. Replacing the lagged engine state in the decision frame with the current one lifts the pass rate from 117/218 to 191/218, while up-weighting the chain-end loss hurts. On the public Lean-Workbook library, 2,132 of 3,536 gradeable admissible problems certify (60.29%) with zero regression on the main suite. All readings come from a version-frozen review with independent rechecks and reverse verification. Restricting free generation and keeping decision-time information visible is a more direct route than enlarging the model.

</details>


### [346] [MedZERO: Self-Evolving Agents for Open-Ended Medical Reasoning Through Controlled Knowledge Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08327)
*Xilin Dang,Weilin Ruan,Xue Yang,Jinghao Wang,Xiaowei Hu,Jinpeng Li,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedZERO：面向开放式医学推理的自演化框架，通过Examiner生成题目、Reasoner借助外部知识工具多轮求解，并采用受控知识积累，在5个医学推理基准上以4B/8B模型稳定超越基线，最高提升13.7个准确率点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在医学问答与临床推理中受限于静态参数知识与昂贵专家监督；已有自演化方法多针对数学、代码等易验证领域，而医学推理具开放式、知识密集、仅部分可验证的特性，难以直接套用。

Method: MedZERO将Examiner（生成前沿医学问题-选项对）与Reasoner（通过外部知识工具进行基于证据的多轮推理）耦合；并引入受控知识积累机制，在推理中同时维护临时探索性知识与经筛选的持久性知识。

Result: 在5个公开医学推理基准、4B与8B规模基模型、开放式评估设置下，MedZERO均持续优于基模型及先前自演化基线，相较次优自演化基线最高取得13.7个平均准确率点提升。

Conclusion: MedZERO证明自演化范式可有效扩展至开放式医学推理，受控知识积累与外部工具结合是持续提升的关键，为知识密集型、部分可验证领域的自演化智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in medical question answering and clinical reasoning, yet their improvement remains constrained by static parametric knowledge and costly expert supervision. Self-evolving agents offer a promising alternative by enabling models to improve through iterative task generation and problem-solving. However, most existing self-evolving methods are designed for easily verifiable domains such as mathematics and coding, where solutions can be checked by exact answers or executable programs. Medical reasoning is fundamentally different: it is open-ended, knowledge-intensive, and often only partially verifiable. We present MedZERO, a self-evolving framework for open-ended medical reasoning. MedZERO couples an Examiner that generates frontier medical question-option pairs with a Reasoner that solves them through evidence-grounded multi-turn reasoning with external knowledge tools. To support reliable, continual improvement, MedZERO adopts controlled knowledge accumulation, which maintains temporary exploratory knowledge and curated persistent knowledge in reasoning. We evaluate MedZERO on five public medical reasoning benchmarks using 4B- and 8B-scale base models under open-ended evaluation. Across all settings, MedZERO consistently outperforms the underlying base models and prior self-evolving baselines, achieving up to 13.7 average accuracy-point gains over the next-best self-evolving baseline.

</details>


### [347] [An AI-Assisted Formalization of the Poincaré Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08329)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Axel Delaval,Leheng Chen,Jinxuan Chen,Jie Xu,Yuxuan Liao,Jiedong Jiang,Chunlei Liu,Bin Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用AI辅助在Lean 4中形式化庞加莱猜想的工作，采用数学家编写的证明蓝图与里程碑式声明相结合的组织方式，实现并行智能体协作，并分析了人类介入与组织决策的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究动机在于：证明庞加莱猜想所需的几何分析缺乏可复用的形式化基础设施，形式化此类数学结果成本高昂，亟需探索可降低验证成本的工作流程与可复用基础设施。

Method: 方法上，项目将数学家准备的证明蓝图与明确的里程碑声明相结合来组织工作；里程碑支持并行智能体任务分配，并为数学家提供定位阻塞点、给出有效数学指导的明确节点；同时分析该流程中的人类介入与组织选择。

Result: 结果显示，该组织方式实现了AI智能体的并行协作，使数学家能够清晰定位阻塞并提供指导，项目产出了面向未来形式化项目的可复用基础设施的起点。

Conclusion: 结论是：该工作为几何分析领域的后续形式化项目提供了可复用基础设施的起步框架；此类基础设施一旦成熟，有望降低数学结果验证的成本。

Abstract: We present an AI-assisted Lean 4 formalization of the Poincaré conjecture. The project began with limited reusable formal infrastructure for the geometric analysis behind the proof. To organize this work, we combined a proof blueprint prepared by mathematicians with explicit milestone statements. These milestones enabled parallel agent work and gave mathematicians clear points to locate blockers and provide effective mathematical guidance. Our analysis identifies the human interventions and organizational choices behind this workflow. The project provides a starting point toward reusable infrastructure for future formalization projects; such infrastructure, once developed, could eventually reduce the cost of verifying mathematical results in geometric analysis.

</details>


### [348] [MoF: Preference-Aware Mixture Modeling for Black-Box LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08330)
*Hun Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture-of-Facets (MoF)框架，通过共享潜在偏好facet的组合与基于历史的动态路由，实现黑盒LLM的可扩展个性化，无需为每个用户单独增加参数，并具备对未见用户的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有（黑盒）大语言模型能力强大，但其输出与用户多样化偏好的对齐仍具挑战。现有黑盒LLM个性化方法多依赖用户专属打分头，导致个性化参数量随用户数线性增长，且对新用户需额外适配，可扩展性与效率受限。

Method: 提出Mixture-of-Facets (MoF) 可扩展个性化框架：将用户偏好建模为共享潜在偏好facet的组合，而非用户专属参数；通过历史条件路由（history-conditioned routing）在共享facet头上进行个性化，从而无需额外参数更新即可对训练中未见的用户实现个性化。

Result: 在多种个性化任务上，MoF相较既有方法取得更强的个性化表现，同时保持更可扩展、参数更高效的设计；额外分析显示其对未见用户具有较强泛化能力。

Conclusion: MoF以共享facet组合与动态路由替代用户专属参数，为黑盒LLM个性化提供了更可扩展、参数高效且可泛化至未见用户的解决方案。

Abstract: Proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of tasks, yet aligning their outputs with diverse user preferences remains challenging. Existing personalization approaches for black-box LLMs often rely on user-specific scoring heads, causing the number of personalized parameters to grow linearly with the number of users and requiring additional adaptation for unseen users. To address these limitations, we propose Mixture-of-Facets (MoF), a scalable personalization framework for black-box LLMs that models user preferences as compositions of shared latent preference facets rather than dedicated user-specific parameters. MoF performs personalization through history-conditioned routing over shared facet heads, enabling personalization for users unseen during training without additional parameter updates. Across diverse personalization tasks, MoF delivers stronger personalization performance while maintaining a more scalable and parameter-efficient design than prior approaches. Additional analysis indicates strong generalization to unseen users.

</details>


### [349] [Explainable Failure Prediction and Prevention in Maritime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08363)
*Dionisis Kalogeropoulos,Georgia Sovatzidi,Panagiotis G. Kalozoumis,Dimitris K. Iakovidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述海事系统中可解释故障预测与预防的关键AI技术，并提出一个支持自主或人在回路纠正措施的闭环概念架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海事系统运行于高度动态环境，设备突发故障会危及安全、可靠性与运营效率；尽管AI、机器学习、数字孪生与预测性维护已能实现主动故障预测与预防，但在安全攸关的海事应用中，保证决策的可信性与可解释性仍是重大挑战。

Method: 采用综述与概念架构设计相结合的方法：提出整合数据采集、时间序列预测、异常检测、风险评估、决策制定与可解释AI的闭环框架；并围绕各架构组件对相关海事研究进行回顾与讨论，梳理其方法、优势与局限。

Result: 给出了面向可解释故障预测与预防的概念架构，并归纳了现有海事研究在各组件上的方法特征、优势与不足，同时明确了不确定性与鲁棒性、模型泛化、可解释性、海事数据集稀缺及实际部署等当前挑战。

Conclusion: 海事领域可信AI辅助决策仍需在鲁棒性、泛化性、可解释性与数据可用性等方面突破，并指出了面向可信AI辅助海事决策的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Maritime systems operate in highly dynamic environments where unexpected equipment failures can compromise safety, reliability, and operational efficiency. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), machine learning, digital twins, and predictive maintenance enable proactive failure prediction and prevention. However, ensuring trustworthy and explainable decision-making remains a major challenge in safety-critical maritime applications. This chapter reviews key AI technologies required for explainable failure prediction and prevention in maritime systems and presents a conceptual architecture capable of supporting autonomous or human-in-the-loop corrective actions. This architecture integrates data acquisition, time-series forecasting, anomaly detection, risk assessment, decision-making, and explainable AI into a closed-loop framework. With reference to the architectural components, a review and discussion of relevant maritime studies is performed, outlining their methods, advantages, and limitations. Furthermore, it highlights current challenges, including uncertainty and robustness, model generalization, explainability, limited availability of maritime datasets, and operational deployment, and identifies future research directions toward trustworthy AI-assisted maritime decision-making.

</details>


### [350] [Transect: Retaining Observability for Long-Horizon LLM Agent Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08364)
*Toby D. Pilditch,Konstantinos Voudouris,Alexandra Abbas,Cozmin Ududec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transect 是一个基于 Inspect Scout 的开源工具包，用于对长时段、多智能体 AI 评估的冗长轨迹进行可导航、可复现的行为分析，并通过一个约 1300 万 token 的 AI 研发评估案例验证了其工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿 AI 评估日益采用开放式、智能体化、长时程任务，其轨迹可长达数百页，涉及复杂多智能体网络的输出与动作，使可观测性范围（评估者可可靠推断智能体行为的边界）不断收窄。语言模型助手虽能辅助分类与解读智能体行为，却也赋予人类评估者过大的分析自由度，威胁基于语言模型的轨迹分析的可复现性与可审计性。

Method: 提出 Transect：一个构建于 Inspect Scout 之上的开源软件包。用户在可复用的"评估族"配置中指定任务上下文与行为词汇表，另行提供评判模型与分析设置。其可导航报告将记录事件、token 使用量、子智能体活动与模型生成的行为标签对齐到统一的、以轮次（turn）为基础的时间轴上；审阅者可把握运行叙事、将任意标签或事件追溯至其来源轮次，并导出底层数据表以供跨运行分析。

Result: 在一个生成近 1300 万 token 的 AI 研发评估中演示了该工作流，将智能体工作划分为与研究技能分类对齐的行为阶段、子智能体委派与交互以及 token 使用。综合视图显示工作集中于操作性任务与稿件撰写，几乎没有持续假设生成阶段的证据——而这可以说是产出高质量科学成果的必要组成部分。

Conclusion: Transect 所提供灵活、可定制的轨迹分析流水线，将帮助评估者跟上更长、更复杂、更频繁的 AI 评估，同时支撑科学严谨性、透明度与可复现性。

Abstract: Frontier AI evaluations increasingly use open-ended, agentic, long-horizon tasks whose transcripts can span hundreds of pages of outputs and actions from complex multi-agent networks. The observability envelop-the range of what evaluators can reliably infer about an agent's behaviours-is therefore narrowing. Language model assistants can help classify and interpret agent behaviour but also afford human evaluators significant analytical degrees of freedom, threatening the reproducibility and auditability of language-model-based transcript analysis. Transect is an open source package built on Inspect Scout to help evaluators understand how a long agent run unfolded, identify behaviour worth investigating, and check interpretations against the transcript. Users specify task context and behavioural vocabulary in a reusable evaluation-family configuration, with judge models and analysis settings supplied separately. Transect's navigable reports align recorded events, token use, sub-agent activity, and model-generated behavioural labels on a common turn-based timeline. Reviewers can quickly grasp a run's narrative, trace any label or event to its source turns, and export the underlying data tables for cross-run analysis. We demonstrate the workflow on an AI R&D evaluation that generated almost 13 million tokens, dividing the agents' work into behavioural phases aligned with research-skill classifications, sub-agent delegations and interactions, and token use. The combined view shows a focus on operational work and manuscript production, with little evidence of a sustained hypothesis generation stage-arguably a necessary component for high-quality scientific outputs. Transect's flexible, customisable transcript-analysis pipeline will enable evaluators to keep pace with longer, more complex, more frequent AI evaluations while supporting scientific rigour, transparency, and reproducibility.

</details>


### [351] [EMHO: EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization via Experience Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08432)
*Hyun Jung Lee,Jungtaek Kim,Jongwon Jeong,Tae-Eui Kam,Donghyun Kim,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EMHO自进化框架，在冻结具身模型的前提下，通过分析执行轨迹与历史经验迭代优化智能体外围harness（规划、上下文、工具使用），并提出EMHO-Merge以单一共享harness服务多子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提升具身智能体的工作多聚焦于通过训练优化底层模型，而控制规划、上下文与工具使用的周边harness通常依赖人工工程化设计。作者希望探究harness能否在稀疏环境反馈下直接从经验轨迹中自我改进。

Method: EMHO保持具身模型冻结，迭代修订harness：分析执行轨迹与先前harness历史，优化范围超出技能或恢复提示，涉及智能体如何监控进度、使用视觉工具、对观察进行grounding以及响应失败。针对多子任务共享单一harness的权衡，提出EMHO-Merge，利用episode级增益与损失指导证据支持的细化，决定修订行为的应用时机与方式。

Result: 在EmbodiedBench的导航与操作任务上评估EMHO，其对Qwen 9B与27B模型的成功率均有稳定提升；定性分析表明EMHO不仅从失败与低效动作中恢复，还重塑了具身智能体解读和交互环境的方式。

Conclusion: harness本身可作为可自我进化的优化对象，在模型冻结条件下通过经验轨迹迭代改进，并在多个子任务上以共享harness实现稳定性能增益，扩展了具身智能体能力提升的路径。

Abstract: Improving embodied agents often focuses on optimizing the underlying model through training, while the surrounding agent harness that controls planning, context, and tool use is typically engineered. We ask whether this harness can instead improve itself directly from experience traces under sparse environmental feedback. We propose EMbodied Agent Harness Optimization (EMHO), a self-evolving framework that keeps the embodied model frozen and iteratively revises its harness by analyzing execution trajectories and prior harness history. EMHO optimizes beyond skills or recovery prompts, modifying how the agent monitors progress, uses vision tools, grounds observations, and responds to failures. To support multiple subtasks with a single harness, we introduce EMHO-Merge, which addresses trade-offs in jointly optimizing a single shared harness across subtasks by using episode-level gains and losses to guide evidence-supported refinement of when and how revised behaviors are applied. We evaluate EMHO on EmbodiedBench across navigation and manipulation tasks, and EMHO consistently improves task success for both Qwen 9B and 27B models. Qualitative analysis shows that EMHO goes beyond recovering from failures and unproductive actions to reshape how the embodied agent interprets and interacts with its environment.

</details>


### [352] [AssemState: Manual and Physical-State-Guided Reasoning for Zero-shot Furniture Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08446)
*Zhiyuan Qi,Jierui Li,Yifan Shen,Cheng Qian,Jiateng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AssemState，一个面向家具装配的零样本框架，结合说明书与物理状态引导，实现装配树恢复与 6D 位姿更新，在装配树恢复与局部位姿指标上显著优于基线，但 MLLM 的空间关系推理仍存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型在视觉理解上进展显著，但与物理环境结合的精确 3D 空间推理仍然困难。家具装配不仅需要从图解说明书中恢复步骤级操作，还需将语义附着关系转化为 6D 位姿更新，使零件能与环境和已装配部件发生物理交互。

Method: AssemState 采用零样本框架：(1) 利用锚点引导的边界装配状态，将说明书页面分解为单零件操作并恢复装配树；(2) 使用迭代式后状态反馈精修，引导连续的 SE(3) 更新与修正；(3) 通过基于仿真的释放测试验证位姿的物理合理性。

Result: 相比最强基线，装配树恢复的 F1 从 38.58% 提升至 62.80%，Tree Exact Match 从 28.24% 提升至 53.92%。在 243 个独立评估的零件级操作上，迭代精修将评审通过的操作从 0 提升至 5.3%，平均 Chamfer 距离从 5.4111 降至 1.7744。但仍存在碰撞、悬浮、镜像朝向错误、未完全就位及错误侧附着等问题。

Conclusion: AssemState 改善了操作结构恢复与部分局部位姿指标，但多模态大模型在空间关系推理方面仍存在明显局限。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have made significant progress in visual understanding, but precise 3D spatial reasoning integrated with physical environment remains difficult. Furniture assembly requires not only recovering step-level operations from diagrammatic manuals, but also translating semantic attachment relations into 6D pose updates that enable parts to physically interact with the environment and previously assembled components. To study this problem, we propose AssemState, a zero-shot framework for manual and physical-state-guided furniture assembly. It firstly employs anchor-guided boundary assembly states to decompose manual pages into single-part operations and recover an assembly-tree. Then, it uses iterative after-state feedback refinement to guide successive (SE(3)) updates and corrections, and validates their physical plausibility through simulation-based release tests. Experiments show that compared with the strongest prior baseline, AssemState improves F1 from 38.58\% to 62.80\% and Tree Exact Match from 28.24\% to 53.92\% for assembly-tree recovery. On 243 independently evaluated part-level operations, our proposed iterative refinement improves judge-accepted operations from 0 to 5.3\% and reduces mean Chamfer distance from 5.4111 to 1.7744. However, visually plausible candidate poses may still suffer from collision, floating, mirror-orientation errors, incomplete seating, and wrong-side attachment. These results show that AssemState improves operation-structure recovery and selected local pose metrics, while MLLMs remain limited for spatial relationship reasoning.

</details>


### [353] [Cylindrical Geodesic Flow Matching for Quasiperiodic Physiological Signal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08510)
*Onur Selim Kilic,Afra Nawar,Cem Okan Yaldiz,Michael J. Cho,Ahmet Rasim Emirdagi,Demet Tangolar,Amirali Aghazadeh,Amit J. Shah,Omer T. Inan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出圆柱测地流匹配（cylindrical geodesic flow matching）用于成对心血管波形翻译，在相位—振幅圆柱上用闭式测地路径替代标准仿射路径，消除插值失真，零样本PPG与有限支撑SCG基准上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿戴设备在不同身体部位采集的心血管信号之间需要成对翻译（由源信号恢复目标振荡信号）。这类源到目标映射具有内在几何结构：相位按周期环绕，须作为循环变量处理；振幅严格为正；逐搏对齐会在周期与受试者间不可预测地漂移。现有深度学习虽用于相位估计与复值信号建模，但未显式学习成对信号间的相位传输；因此端点监督回归与流匹配中的标准仿射路径都未考虑该相位—振幅结构。

Method: 指出流匹配中标准仿射路径在准周期信号插值时扭曲中间振幅与瞬时频率；将其替换为相位—振幅圆柱上的闭式测地路径，从而消除伪影，并将每个训练对转化为稠密、几何一致的流速监督。方法命名为圆柱测地流匹配。

Result: 在零样本光电容积描记（PPG）与有限支撑心震描记（SCG）自适应基准上，方法一致优于插值基线，并与直接监督预测持平或更优；相比最强竞争基线，Hilbert变换、L2和动态时间规整距离最多降低约15%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，桥接几何是振荡信号翻译上流匹配的关键归纳偏置。

Abstract: Paired translation between quasiperiodic physiological waveforms (i.e., recovering a target oscillatory signal from the source) is central to the interpretation of cardiovascular signals derived from wearables placed at different body locations. This source-to-target mapping in these problems carries inherent geometric structure: the phase wraps around the cycle and must be treated as a circular variable, the amplitude remains strictly positive, and the beat-to-beat alignment can drift unpredictably across cycles and subjects. While deep neural networks have been used for phase estimation and complex-valued signal modeling, prior work does not explicitly learn phase transport between paired signals. Consequently, neither endpoint-supervised regression nor the standard affine path used in flow matching accounts for this phase--amplitude structure. We introduce \emph{cylindrical geodesic flow matching} for paired cardiovascular waveform translation. We show that the standard affine path used in flow matching distorts intermediate amplitude and instantaneous frequency when interpolating between quasiperiodic signals; replacing it with a closed-form geodesic on the phase--amplitude cylinder eliminates these artifacts and converts each training pair into dense, geometry-consistent velocity supervision. On zero-shot photoplethysmography and limited-support seismocardiography adaptation benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms interpolation baselines and matches or exceeds direct supervised prediction, reducing Hilbert Transform, $L_2$, and Dynamic Time Warping distance by up to ${\sim}15\%$ over the strongest competing baseline. These results suggest that bridge geometry is a critical inductive bias for flow matching on oscillatory signal translation.

</details>


### [354] [How Much Evidence Should a Coding Agent's Self-Correction Carry? Adaptive Dirichlet Evidence for Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08514)
*Yunbo Long,Guangya Hao,Yuhan Liu,Yiting Duan,Longyan Tan,Yunchen Long,Hao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出有效证据自蒸馏（EESD），将执行修正的"转移支持"与"证据质量"分离建模，用Dirichlet后验生成不确定性惩罚权重，用于KL锚定的修正学习，并在代码智能体上验证概率估计与性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈使编码智能体能够修正程序并从自身纠错中学习，但每次修正的学习权重应当同时反映其执行所支持的转移强度以及背后证据的多少。已有做法未将这两者分开刻画，因而难以合理分配学习权重。

Method: EESD分别表示转移支持与证据质量：归一化执行相关性决定相对转移支持及有效伪计数质量；再由Dirichlet后验产生不确定性惩罚权重，用于KL锚定的修正学习。理论分析显示，对称先验下改变质量保持类别序关系，且有效质量给出的监督系数被其匹配的固定质量对应值所界定。

Result: 在四个模型—领域历史扫描中，可见观测由1增至8使未来结果NLL降低55.0–59.3%；在8个观测时，有效质量在全部四个对比中NLL均低于固定质量。主要匹配研究（DeepSeek/RunBugRun）中，3,000个样例的argmax预测完全一致，NLL增益在相关性集中时最大。一轮修正学习后，DeepSeek/CodeARC全测试Pass@1由15.0%升至20.4%，配对95%源自助区间为[+2.8, +8.0]个百分点；十二种设定的下游评测界定了该更新的模型—领域适用范围。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将证据支持与证据质量分离，能够改变编码智能体中的概率估计与修正学习效果，并带来一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Execution feedback lets coding agents revise programs and learn from their own corrections. A correction's learning weight should reflect both the transitions supported by its executions and the amount of evidence behind that support. We introduce Effective-Evidence Self-Distillation (EESD), which represents these quantities separately. Normalized execution relevance determines relative transition support and an effective pseudo-count mass; a Dirichlet posterior then produces an uncertainty-penalized weight for KL-anchored correction learning. Under a symmetric prior, changing mass preserves category ordering, and effective mass yields a supervised coefficient bounded by its matched fixed-mass counterpart. Across four model-domain history sweeps, increasing visible observations from one to eight reduces future-outcome NLL by 55.0-59.3%. At eight observations, effective mass achieves lower NLL than fixed mass in all four comparisons. In the primary matched DeepSeek/RunBugRun study, argmax predictions agree on all 3,000 examples, with the largest NLL gain under concentrated relevance. After one correction-learning round, DeepSeek/CodeARC all-tests Pass@1 increases from 15.0% to 20.4%, with a paired 95% source-bootstrap interval of [+2.8, +8.0] percentage points. The twelve-setting downstream evaluation establishes the model-domain scope of this update. These results show how separating evidence support from evidence mass changes probability estimation and correction learning in coding agents.

</details>


### [355] [AnyBottle: A Recipe to Only Keep the Concepts You Really Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08552)
*Wolfgang Stammer,Sukrut Rao,Hevra Petekkaya,David Steinmann,Bernt Schiele*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AnyBottle，一种构建紧凑、任务特定概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的统一方法，无需概念标注即可获得小而可解释的瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 CBM 需要人工概念标注；无标注变体虽免去标注，却依赖庞大且训练与推理阶段固定的概念词表，导致瓶颈远大于任务所需，难以检视与干预。

Method: 仅需冻结主干网络与无监督概念池（如稀疏自编码器）；用同主干的黑盒教师指导选择，每轮在师生分歧区域内加入最能解释当前失败的概念；按选择顺序施加嵌套 dropout 训练，使任一概念前缀均可独立预测。

Result: 在六个视觉数据集与两个文本数据集、两种教师范式下，AnyBottle 相比无标注基线使用更少概念、概念一致性更高，同时性能接近黑盒参考模型。

Conclusion: 无标注不等于大而全：一个小规模、自动发现的概念词表可具备与庞大固定词表相当的表达能力，且各阶段与模态无关，换域换任务只需替换主干与概念池。

Abstract: Concept bottleneck models (CBMs) make predictions inspectable and intervenable by routing them through human-interpretable concepts, but originally required concept annotations. Annotation-free variants remove this requirement, but typically use large concept vocabularies, static at both training and inference, producing bottlenecks larger than any task or prediction needs and harder to inspect. We propose AnyBottle, a single recipe for building compact, task-specific CBMs. AnyBottle assumes only a frozen backbone and an unsupervised concept pool, such as a sparse autoencoder. A black-box teacher trained on the same backbone then guides selection: each round adds the concept that best explains the bottleneck's current failures, with candidates restricted to regions of teacher/student disagreement. Trained with nested dropout over this selection order, the final bottleneck predicts accurately from any concept prefix, so inference spends fewer concepts on inputs it is confident about early and more on hard ones. Since no stage is modality-specific, a new domain and task requires swapping only the backbone and concept pool. Across six vision and two text datasets and two teacher paradigms, AnyBottle yields bottlenecks with fewer concepts and higher concept consistency than annotation-free baselines, while staying close to the black-box reference. Overall, AnyBottle shows that going annotation-free need not mean going large: a small, discovered vocabulary can be as expressive as a much larger, fixed one.

</details>


### [356] [Adaptive Power Sampling for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08563)
*Bingnan Xiao,Chenhao Yang,Bingcong Li,Wei Ni,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应幂采样（APS），在测试时按查询难度动态调整锐化指数，无需训练即可提升LLM推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级幂采样方法对所有查询统一锐化基座模型分布，忽略了查询难度差异以及基座模型对各查询已有处理能力的差异，因此需要为幂采样引入查询自适应性。

Method: 理论上证明进一步锐化的收益由正确与错误响应之间的自奖励差距决定；据此提出APS，在测试时利用答案一致性与模型自奖励之间的关系，按查询逐一调整锐化指数。

Result: 在MATH500、HumanEval、GPQA等多种推理任务上，APS持续优于固定锐化指数的幂采样，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 通过将锐化强度与查询相关的自奖励差距挂钩，可实现查询自适应的训练-free推理增强，验证了自适应幂采样相比固定锐化的有效性。

Abstract: Sequence-level power sampling has recently emerged as a training-free approach to reasoning by sampling from a sharpened output distribution of a base large language model (LLM). Nevertheless, existing methods typically sharpen the base model distribution uniformly across queries, overlooking variations in query difficulty and in how well the base model already handles each query. The goal of this work is to equip power sampling with query adaptivity. Theoretically, we show that the benefits of further sharpening are determined by the self-reward gap between correct and incorrect responses. Based on this insight, we propose \emph{Adaptive Power Sampling} (APS), which adjusts the sharpening exponent on a per-query basis at test time using the relationship between answer agreement and the model's self-reward. Experiments across diverse reasoning tasks, including MATH500, HumanEval, and GPQA, show that APS consistently outperforms power sampling with a fixed sharpening exponent, without additional training.

</details>


### [357] [MINDSET: Energy-based Schema Evolution for Long Conversational Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08586)
*Sujato Dutta,Sreekruthy Tummala,Shashank Vanga,Ayushmi Pavani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MINDSET 将长期对话记忆建模为受约束的状态管理：把对话存为不可变片段并组织成带版本的模式，通过最小能量状态转移决定强化/取代/分裂/创建，在 LoCoMo 与 MemoryAgentBench 上取得最优答案质量与检索排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话智能体需记住久远内容以避免用户重复说明，但指令与上下文随时间变化，记忆系统须能适应。理想记忆需同时保留当前与历史状态、区分过时信息与有效知识、按查询检索相关证据，并避免反复调用大模型重写历史交互。既有做法（持续摘要式记忆）难以满足这些要求。

Method: 提出 MINDSET 记忆控制器：将对话存储为不可变片段（immutable episodes），并通过最小能量状态转移组织为带版本的模式（versioned schemas）。每个新片段可强化、取代、分裂或新建模式；转移决策权衡表征失真、矛盾、历史损伤、碎片化与内部不一致，并用迟滞（hysteresis）机制防止孤立矛盾过早重写稳定记忆，且无需反复调用大模型重写历史交互。

Result: 在 850 题可复现样本（700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench）上与 5 个记忆系统对比：MINDSET 取得最高 LoCoMo 答案 F1，检索排序指标（Recall@8、MRR、nDCG@8）相对次优方法 LightMem 显著提升（Holm 校正后 p<0.01）；在 MemoryAgentBench 上取得最高分但相对差距较小。消融实验表明受控碎片化与模式感知分配对答案质量贡献最大。700 题跨模型评测（GLM-4.7 与 Gemma-4-31B）支持模型无关性。

Conclusion: 长期记忆更适合被处理为受约束的状态管理，而非持续摘要。MINDSET 通过不可变片段与版本化模式的最小能量转移，在保持历史一致性的同时适应上下文变化，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: Long conversational agents have become essential in our daily lives. They must remember what was said long back in order to help us efficiently complete a task without needing the user to repeat instructions and context repeatedly. However, the main issue is that instructions and context change over time and so the agents must be able to adapt accordingly. A useful memory system should preserve both current and historical states, distinguish stale information from active knowledge, retrieve evidence appropriate to the query and avoid repeatedly invoking a large language model to rewrite prior interactions. We introduce MINDSET, a memory controller that stores a conversation as immutable episodes and organizes them into versioned schemas through minimum-energy state transitions. Each incoming episode may reinforce, supersede, split or create a schema. The transition decision balances representation distortion, contradiction, historical damage, fragmentation and internal inconsistency, while hysteresis prevents isolated contradictions from prematurely rewriting stable memory. We evaluate MINDSET against 5 memory systems on a reproducible sample of 850 questions (700 LoCoMo + 150 MemoryAgentBench). MINDSET obtains the highest observed LoCoMo answer F1 while significantly improving retrieval ranking (Recall@8, MRR and nDCG@8) over the second best method LightMem (p<0.01 after Holm correction). It obtains the highest observed scores on MemoryAgentBench although the relative difference is low. Ablations identify controlled fragmentation and schema-aware assignment as the largest contributors to answer quality. Additionally, a 700-question cross-model evaluation with GLM-4.7 and Gemma-4-31B supported model independence. These results show that long-term memory can be better handled as constrained state management rather than continual summarization.

</details>


### [358] [Recursive Game Creator: An Agentic Product-Level Experience-Oriented Game Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08621)
*Jiajun Chen,Haoyu Wu,Mingda Jia,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Recursive Game Creator，一个以体验为导向的递归式智能体游戏开发框架，通过Designer、Builder、Player、Reviewer四组件闭环迭代，将粗糙原型提升为可玩性更高的游戏，在多个基准上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏设计智能体虽能生成可运行的游戏，但程序正确性并不等于玩家获得良好体验，因此需要从"能跑"转向"好玩"，构建以玩家体验为核心的智能体开发范式。

Method: 框架围绕四个组件组成递归开发循环：Designer将用户指令与Reviewer反馈转化为详细计划；Builder将计划实现为候选游戏；编码原生的Player通过程序化接口创建并执行可复用策略，高效收集多样化游戏轨迹，缓解基于GUI采集慢导致的评测偏差；Reviewer基于精心设计的轨迹指标推断玩家偏好，结合视觉证据与显式文本偏好，按游戏特定标准进行评估，接受更优版本并给出改进意见以进入下一轮。

Result: 在GameCraft-Bench上取得77.89的SOTA总体表现；在GameASG-Bench上严格任务成功率达53.2%，较同模型基线提升34.1%，并以93.4%取得对比方法中最高的平均运行时检查通过率；用户研究显示更长的游玩时长和更高评分。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将体验导向的自动评测与递归迭代引入智能体游戏开发，可显著提升生成游戏的可玩性与任务成功率，代码即将开源。

Abstract: Recent game design agents have made substantial progress in generating playable games. However, program correctness does not ensure an enjoyable experience for players. We present Recursive Game Creator, an experience-oriented harness to advance agentic game development from rough game prototypes into entertaining games. Recursive Game Creator organizes recursive development around four components: Designer, Builder, Player, and Reviewer. The Designer translates user instructions and Reviewer's feedback into detailed plans. The Builder turns these plans into candidate games. The coding-native Player creates and executes reusable policies through programmatic interfaces to efficiently collect diverse gameplay trajectories, mitigating evaluation bias caused by slow GUI-based collection. The Reviewer uses carefully designed trajectory-based metrics to induce player preferences, integrating with visual evidence and explicit textual preferences to evaluate games against game-specific criteria. Finally, the Reviewer accepts the better version and provides improvement reviews for the next round, closing the recursive loop. Our method achieves state-of-the-art overall performance of 77.89 on GameCraft-Bench. On GameASG-Bench, it achieves a strict task success rate of 53.2%, a 34.1% improvement over the same-model baseline, and the highest mean runtime-check pass rate at 93.4% among compared methods. A user study shows longer playtime and higher ratings. Code is coming soon.

</details>


### [359] [Parallel Predictive World Models for Accurate and Efficient Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08627)
*Wanjin Feng,Baobin Zhang,Ao Yu,Shibo Feng,Xi Wang,Xingyu Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PPWM 并行预测有限时域轨迹，保留因果交互但去除逐步解码状态反馈，在四项视觉控制任务中实现更低预测误差、更高 CEM 成功率及 3 倍以上规划加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域世界模型规划通常依赖自回归展开：预测状态被反复反馈回模型。这虽保留时间结构，却造成与时域长度成正比的串行路径，并使后期预测暴露于递归的解码状态反馈误差中。

Method: 提出 Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM)：并行预测有限时域轨迹，同时保留未来表示之间的因果交互。每个时域以因果动作前缀为条件，未来表示在解码前相互交互，从而将时间因果性与逐状态输出递归分离。作者将自回归展开形式化为因果轨迹映射，并识别出 PPWM 所移除的解码状态反馈路径。

Result: 在四项视觉控制任务中，PPWM 在所评估的预测接口中取得最低的长时域预测误差和最高的 CEM 模拟器成功率；相比自回归 LeWM 基线，CEM 规划平均加速超过 3 倍。

Conclusion: 精确高效的长时域世界模型规划并不需要逐状态自回归，可通过并行因果轨迹预测实现。

Abstract: Long-horizon world-model planning typically relies on autoregressive rollouts, where predicted states are repeatedly fed back into the model. This preserves temporal structure but creates a horizon-length sequential path and exposes later predictions to recursive decoded-state feedback. We introduce Parallel Predictive World Models (PPWM), which predict a finite-horizon trajectory in parallel while retaining causal interaction among future representations. Each horizon is conditioned on its causal action prefix, and future representations interact before decoding, separating temporal causality from state-by-state output recursion. We formalize this distinction by viewing autoregressive rollout as a causal trajectory map and identifying the decoded-state feedback pathway removed by PPWM. Across four visual-control tasks, PPWM achieves the lowest long-horizon prediction error and the highest Cross-Entropy Method (CEM) simulator success among the evaluated predictive interfaces. Meanwhile, PPWM achieves more than a 3$\times$ average CEM planning speedup over the autoregressive LeWM baseline. These results suggest that accurate and efficient long-horizon world-model planning does not require state-by-state autoregression, but can instead be achieved through parallel causal trajectory prediction.

</details>


### [360] [ParanoiaEval: Benchmarking Unnecessary Defensive Work in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08662)
*Hanjun Luo,Xiucheng Zhang,Zhuoning Xu,Zhimu Huang,Yingbin Jin,Xinfeng Li,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ParanoiaEval，首个面向编码智能体风险处置能力的统一评测基准，基于软件工程风险管理的 Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance 框架构建 200 个证据控制型仓库级任务对，并引入违规与证据响应性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体日益自主承担实际工作，判断其风险处置是否合理变得重要；既有工作从分散视角评估相关行为，缺乏系统性框架来统一这些行为。

Method: ParanoiaEval 以软件工程风险管理中成熟的 Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance 框架为基础，将该框架的 4 种基本处置方式操作化到编码智能体场景；包含 200 个证据控制型仓库级任务对，每对任务仅在决定处置方式的证据上存在差异；同时提出针对风险处置违规与证据响应性的专用指标，并采用经人工校准的智能体评判器进行可靠评测。

Result: 对 8 个代表性模型的大规模实验及事后人类研究显示：(I) 即便存在明确证据，不必要风险处置仍出现在 11.2%–58.7% 的运行中，且在不同智能体配置间差异显著；(II) 更强的任务能力并不保证更恰当的风险处置，而处置违规显著损害开发者体验，表明风险处置是一个独立的能力维度；(III) 智能体呈现与既有风险管理研究一致的系统性模式，说明人类实践知识可指导该能力的诊断与改进。

Conclusion: 风险处置能力独立于任务能力，需被单独评测；ParanoiaEval 提供了统一框架与指标，并表明软件工程风险管理的人类经验可用于诊断和提升编码智能体的风险处置能力。

Abstract: As coding agents increasingly undertake real-world work autonomously, judging whether their risk treatments are warranted has become important. Existing work evaluates related agent behaviors from separate perspectives, but lacks a systematic framework for unifying these behaviors. To bridge this gap, we introduce ParanoiaEval, the first benchmark for unified evaluation of risk-treatment capabilities in coding agents. Grounded in the well-established Avoidance-Transfer-Mitigation-Acceptance framework in software engineering risk management, ParanoiaEval operationalizes its 4 fundamental treatments for coding-agent settings and contains 200 evidence-controlled repository-level task pairs, each differing only in treatment-defining evidence. We further introduce dedicated metrics for risk-treatment violations and evidence responsiveness, using a human-calibrated agentic judge for reliable evaluation. Large-scale experiments on 8 representative models and a post-hoc human study reveal that (I) unnecessary risk treatment occurs in 11.2%-58.7% of runs despite explicit evidence, with substantial variation across agent configurations; (II) stronger task capability does not ensure more appropriate risk treatment, while treatment violations substantially harm developers' experience, establishing risk treatment as an independent capability dimension; and (III) agents exhibit systematic patterns consistent with established risk-management findings, suggesting that knowledge from human practice can guide the diagnosis and improvement of this capability.

</details>


### [361] [Coupled but Late: Turn-Taking Between Full-Duplex Speech Models in Unscripted Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08683)
*Lichen Zhu,Yueqian Lin,Yiheng Wang,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 双 PersonaPlex-7B 全双工语音模型在共享时钟下互相对话，其轮次交接时序相互耦合，但整体表现为"感知到对方结束后再反应"的滞后交接，缺乏人类式的轮末投射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音模型原本用于与人类对话，如今越来越多被用于模型之间的自我对弈、智能体社会与基于模型的评测。在这种闭环中，没有人类来承受时序误差——一个模型的轮次交接就是另一个模型的输入。因此需要探究这一交互闭环最终稳定在什么样的时序上。

Method: 让两个 PersonaPlex-7B 实例在共享时钟上以音频 token 进行无脚本对话，并对它们与 Switchboard 人类对话应用同一套"话轮转移"规则。通过跨对话重新配对说话人，以及人为延迟单向信道，来检验时序是耦合还是反应式的。

Result: 模型间的时序是耦合的：跨对话重新配对说话人会破坏这种时序结构。但话轮交接发生得偏晚，中位数为 400–560 ms，而人类为 137 ms；在伙伴话轮结束前最后 120 ms 这一窗口内，人类投射把约十分之一的转移放在此处，而模型仅占 1%。人为延迟单向信道会使响应一对一平移，且响应前的"助跑"区间为空。

Conclusion: 模型的交接时序符合"感知到对方话轮结束后再反应"的被动等待机制，而非人类时序所要求的轮末投射，因此在模型互相对话的闭环中会稳定在明显迟滞的交接节奏上。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech models are trained to converse with a person, but they are increasingly made to converse with each other, in self-play data generation, agent societies, and model-based evaluation. In that loop no human absorbs a timing error: each model's turn-taking is the other's input. We ask what timing the loop settles into. Two PersonaPlex-7B instances exchange audio tokens on a shared clock in unscripted conversation, and one floor-transfer rule is applied to them and to Switchboard. Their timing is coupled: re-pairing speakers across conversations destroys it. But the floor changes hands late, at a median of 400-560 ms against 137 ms for humans, and the last 120 ms of the partner's turn, where human projection places a tenth of its transfers, holds 1% of theirs. Delaying one direction of the channel shifts the response one-for-one and leaves the run-up to it empty, consistent with a reactive wait after the perceived end rather than the turn-end projection human timing requires.

</details>


### [362] [ScienceClaw: Benchmarking Continual Self-Evolution of AI-for-Science Agents Across the Natural and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08691)
*Mingda Zhang,Wenjin Liu,Tiesunlong Shen,Zikai Xiao,Zhenghong Lin,Qing Xu,Erik Cambria,Xiaoying Tang,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScienceClaw，将科学自动化中的"验证执行→持久程序改进"形式化为固定参数的程序自演化问题，并构建跨23个学科的ScienceClaw-Eval基准，通过顺序任务流与独立重置评测衡量科学正确性、演化增益、保持性、跨数据集迁移与演化成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型智能体正加速科学自动化，但经过验证的执行很少转化为持久的程序级改进；且现有评测未在自然科学与社会科学的顺序任务中考察这一过程。因此需要将任务求解、科学验证与程序更新统一起来并加以系统评测。

Method: 将ScienceClaw形式化为固定参数的程序自演化框架：通过多轮交互修复可执行工作流；将经重复执行验证的失败—成功轨迹转化为相互关联的Skill与Operator候选；仅当源任务重放能够复现修复、且独立科学任务确有提升时，才保留该更新。评测方面构建ScienceClaw-Eval，覆盖23个学科，采用顺序流与独立重置两种评测方式。

Result: ScienceClaw-Eval在23个学科上度量了科学正确性、演化增益、保持性、跨数据集迁移与演化成本，为顺序任务中的程序自演化提供了统一评测（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: 该工作把科学智能体的执行经验上升为可持久、可验证的程序级能力，并给出了跨学科、兼顾增益与保持及成本的评测范式；代码已开源。

Abstract: Large language model agents are accelerating scientific automation, yet verified executions rarely become persistent program-level improvements, and existing evaluations do not examine this process across sequential tasks in both the natural and social sciences. We formalize ScienceClaw as fixed-parameter program self-evolution that unifies task solving, scientific verification, and program updates. ScienceClaw-Eval spans 23 disciplines and measures scientific correctness, evolutionary gain, retention, cross-dataset transfer, and evolution cost through sequential streams and independent reset evaluation. Our framework repairs executable workflows through multi-turn interaction, converts re-execution-verified failure--success trajectories into linked Skill and Operator candidates, and retains an update only when source-task replay reproduces the repair and independent scientific tasks improve. Code is available at https://github.com/beita6969/ScienceClaw.

</details>


### [363] [nanoMuse: An Open-Source Personal Agent for Every Device You Own](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08699)
*Guangyi Liu,Yong Liu,Jiangning Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以 Meta 的闭源个人智能体 Muse 为对照，通过五个问题、三个时间尺度界定"个人智能体"这一软件形态，并基于 Meta 公开资料与生产提示词副本还原其构造；随后提出开源对应物 nanoMuse（GPL-3.0），实现跨设备单智能体、共享会话中继、Sentinel 动作管控、可读文件式记忆与用户自选模型，并以路线图给出开放记忆（带溯源）、手部操作评测套件与开放模型的后续方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2011 年的助手只"回答并等待"，2023 年的 agent 只"做一件事就停"。2026 年 9 月 Meta 的 Muse 展示了面向单个人的智能体：拥有账户、设备、记忆与持久会话，但完全封闭——绑定某家厂商云、单一国家。这类智能体被期待能代人操作其账户与设备、跨周记忆、在值得时主动开口、并为自身行为负责。它本质是软件而非模型，却始终没有开放对应物，因此本文要填补这一空白。

Method: 报告分两部分：其一，用五个问题与三个时间尺度给出个人智能体的定义，并依据 Meta 的公开记录与一份生产提示词副本，逐条标注来源地解读 Muse 的构造方式；其二，提出 nanoMuse，作为 GPL-3.0 下的开源对应物——在用户每一台设备上各部署一个智能体，可直接操作手机屏幕与电脑；通过任何人可自建的中继共享同一段对话；所有动作经由 Sentinel 审核；记忆以用户可读的文件形式保存；模型由用户自选。其规模与成本以估算形式给出。

Result: 文章刻画了 Muse 的封闭属性（厂商云、单国、闭源、含持久账户/设备/记忆/会话），并给出 nanoMuse 的可运行设计：多设备单智能体、可自托管中继的共享会话、Sentinel 动作关卡、文件化可读记忆、模型可替换，附规模与成本估算。

Conclusion: 个人智能体作为一种软件形态，其开放化需要四件事：开放权重之外的开放记忆（记忆带溯源）、面向"手"（设备操作）的评测套件、以及支撑这些操作的开放模型。作者将其列为路线图，作为 nanoMuse 的后续工作方向。

Abstract: Assistants from 2011 answered and waited, and agents from 2023 did a task and stopped. In September 2026 Meta's Muse showed an agent for one person, with accounts, devices, memory and a conversation that lasts, closed, in a vendor's cloud, in one country. Such an agent is expected to act on a person's accounts and devices, remember them across weeks, speak first when it is worth it, and answer for what it did. It is a kind of software, not a model, and until now had no open counterpart. This report defines the personal agent in five questions and three horizons. It reads how Muse is built from Meta's public record and a copy of its production prompt, each statement marked by its source. It then presents nanoMuse, the open-source counterpart under the GPL-3.0, one agent on every device a person owns, with hands on the phone's screen and the computer's. They share one conversation over a relay anyone can run; every action goes through a Sentinel, memory is files the person can read, and the model is their choice. Its size and cost are given as estimates. What is open, memory with provenance, an evaluation suite for the hands and an open model for them, is set out as a roadmap.

</details>


### [364] [WorldSolver: Can LLM Agents Simulate the Physical Dynamics via Solver Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08720)
*Siru Jiang,Yongzhe Lyu,Shuo Lu,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Yue Liao,Bin Wang,Jian Liang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldSolver 是一个包含168个物理仿真任务的基准，源自61篇经典计算机图形学论文，覆盖7个物理领域，用于评估LLM智能体从零构建求解器的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在科学工程问题求解上不断进步，物理仿真是其中富有挑战且实用的测试场景，可应用于具身智能、游戏和影视。作为仿真的核心，求解器需要计算动态系统状态随时间的演化，构建求解器要求具备物理理解（识别合适模型）、数学推理（形式化动力学方程）与软件工程（实现为可执行代码）三方面能力，而LLM智能体在此方面的能力仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出WorldSolver基准，包含168个仿真任务，取自61篇经典计算机图形学论文中的物理现象，覆盖7个物理领域。每个任务提供一个固定仿真环境的代码脚手架，由智能体补全求解器实现。评估从三个维度进行：执行检查（能否成功运行）、视觉保真度（渲染仿真中是否复现预期的动态行为）、物理合理性（对生成动力学进行基于物理的验证）。

Result: 前沿智能体的实验表明，生成可执行的求解器本身就很困难，满足视觉与物理正确性则更难。GPT-5.6-Sol 与 Claude-Opus-5 相对其他被测智能体表现较好，但总体得分仅为48.7%和46.7%。

Conclusion: WorldSolver 是迈向智能体自动生成求解器的早期一步，有望推动智能体朝着能忠实仿真动态物理世界的方向发展。代码已开源于 https://github.com/sirujiang/WorldSolver。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly advancing scientific and engineering problem solving, with physics simulation emerging as a challenging yet practical testbed for reproducing complex physical phenomena with application in embodied AI, games and films. As the workhorse of such simulation, a solver computes how the state of a dynamic system evolves over time. Building such solvers requires physical understanding to identify appropriate models, mathematical reasoning to formulate the underlying dynamics, and software engineering to implement them as executable code, yet this capability of LLM agents remains underexplored. To this end, we introduce WorldSolver, a benchmark of 168 simulation tasks derived from physical phenomena in 61 classic computer graphics papers, spanning 7 physical domains. Each task contains a code scaffold that provides a fixed simulation environment for the scene, with the solver implementation left for the agent to complete. Specifically, we evaluate them along three dimensions: Execution Checks for successful execution, Visual Fidelity for reproducing the intended dynamic behavior in the rendered simulation, and Physical Plausibility for physics-grounded verification of the generated dynamics. Experiments on frontier agents reveal that producing executable solvers is difficult itself, and satisfying visual and physical correctness is even harder. GPT-5.6-Sol and Claude-Opus-5 perform comparatively better than the other evaluated agents, yet achieve overall scores of only 48.7% and 46.7%, respectively. WorldSolver is an early step toward agentic solver generation, and we hope it helps drive progress toward agents that can faithfully simulate the dynamic physical world. Code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/WorldSolver.

</details>


### [365] [Does an Agent's History Tell You When Compaction Will Hurt? A Modest, Bounded Effect on the TRACE Paired-Replay Corpus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08722)
*Egor Pakhomov,Erik Nijkamp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究检验"代理近期行为能否预测压缩(compaction)何时有害",发现边界前历史对压缩后危害的预测力很弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程代理通常基于全局规则(如token预算)压缩上下文,忽视代理正在做什么。作者追问:代理的近期行为能否预测一次压缩何时会造成伤害。

Method: 利用TRACE公开语料库中590个由harness触发的AppWorld压缩边界,从重执行的前缀状态出发,分别在压缩前上下文与摘要条件下重放每个边界,并记录后续动作的负担(报错的调用或重复已做过的调用)。此外采用预设对比、扩展协议触发器与冻结可解释触发器进行评估。

Result: 边界前历史对压缩后危害的预测力很弱;按前缀位置的预设对比为宽零效应,其背后的"has-written"标签实际测量的是轨迹阶段。最佳扩展协议触发器达到留出AUROC 0.66(在复现自身标签上为0.64),而同一边界复现为0.72。最佳冻结可解释触发器避免了21%的有害(正负担)边界,同时保留84%的压缩机会,在计数上超过随机规则期望,但在负担质量上未超过(事后比较)。在匹配保留率下,最佳触发器能否胜过token预算规则无法在本次发布上评估。

Conclusion: 边界前历史对压缩后危害的预测力有限,现有触发器仅带来适度收益,且能否优于token预算规则尚不可判定。作者明确指出语料库应提供哪些内容以回答该问题。

Abstract: Many long-horizon agents compact their context on a global rule, usually a token budget, blind to what the agent was doing. We ask whether the agent's recent behaviour predicts when a compaction will hurt. TRACE's public corpus of 590 harness-triggered AppWorld compaction boundaries replays each boundary from a re-executed prefix state under the pre-compaction context and under the summary, and records the burden of the next actions: calls that error or repeat a call already made. We find that pre-boundary history predicts post-compaction harm only weakly. An internally prespecified contrast by prefix placement is a wide null, and the naive "has-written" label behind it turns out to measure trajectory phase. The best extension-protocol trigger reaches held-out AUROC 0.66 (0.64 on the replicate's own label) against a same-boundary replicate of 0.72; the best frozen, interpretable trigger avoids 21% of harmful (positive-burden) boundaries while keeping 84% of compaction opportunities, and exceeds the random-rule expectation on count but not on burden mass (a post hoc comparison). Whether the best trigger beats a token-budget rule at matched retention cannot be evaluated on the release. We state what corpora should ship to answer it.

</details>


### [366] [VeriFine: Scaling Verification for Self-Improvement in Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08761)
*Zewei Zhou,Rachel Luo,Yulong Cao,Chaowei Xiao,Chensheng Peng,Boyi Li,Thomas Tian,Zheng Lian,Yan Wang,Jiaqi Ma,Boris Ivanovic,Marco Pavone,Wenhao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 VeriFine 框架，通过策略、课程与评判器（judge）的协同演化来扩展验证能力，实现具身推理中的持续自我改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自改进策略不断暴露新的失败模式，而固定评判器限制了优化反馈与有用训练样本的发现，制约进一步自我改进；具身推理中该挑战更突出，因为可靠评估需兼顾空间定位、因果推理与安全决策。

Method: VeriFine 采用包含两个循环的 agent harness 框架。策略改进循环用评分标准评判器诊断反复出现的失败、构建自适应课程并优化策略；当进展停滞、验证成为瓶颈时，评判器改进循环选择性查询人类对信息量大的失败案例的指导，并通过协同交互式校准（coactive calibration）精炼评判器，即人与智能体解决分歧并向物理推理的客观评分标准收敛。修订后的评判器再指导下一阶段的数据选择与策略优化。

Result: 在驾驶与机器人导航任务上的实验表明，无论在强化学习还是监督微调下，策略与评判器能力均实现持续自我改进。

Conclusion: 结果说明扩展验证能力可支撑策略失败模式演化过程中的持续自我改进。

Abstract: Self-improving policies continually expose new failure patterns, changing what their judges must be able to verify. However, current fixed judges constrain both optimization feedback and the discovery of useful training examples, limiting further self-improvement. This challenge is even more acute in embodied reasoning, where reliable evaluation must account for spatial grounding, causal reasoning, and safety-aware decision-making. We introduce VeriFine, an agent harness framework that scales verification through the co-evolution of the policy, training curriculum, and judge. The Policy Improvement Loop uses a rubric judge to diagnose recurring failures, construct an adaptive curriculum, and optimize the policy. When progress plateaus and verification becomes a bottleneck, the Judge Improvement Loop selectively queries human guidance on informative failure cases and refines the judge through coactive calibration, in which humans and agents resolve disagreements and converge toward the objective rubric of physical reasoning. The revised judge then guides the next stage of data selection and policy optimization. Experiments on driving and robot navigation tasks demonstrate continuous self-improvement in both policy and judge capability across reinforcement and supervised fine-tuning. These results show how scaling verification supports continuous self-improvement as policy failure patterns evolve.

</details>


### [367] [Agent in a Bottle: Can LLM Agents Turn Their Capabilities Into Cheap, Scalable Artifacts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08775)
*Ankit Sonthalia,Haritz Puerto,Alexander Rubinstein,Martin Gubri,Seong Joon Oh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BOTTLED 基准，评估 LLM 智能体将通用能力"装瓶"为任务专用、可摊销低成本方案的能力。结果显示零样本性能强并不代表装瓶能力强，但装瓶可显著节省成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 虽能解决许多窄任务，但对数百万相关实例逐一查询成本高昂。作者希望探究智能体能否自主创建更廉价的解决方案，即把通用能力转化为兼顾答案质量与摊销成本的任务专用方案，这一能力被命名为"bottling（装瓶）"。

Method: 提出 BOTTLED 基准：智能体获得完整未标注工作负载，须在固定时间、算力和 LLM API 预算下完成。智能体可自主选择方法，如训练小模型或编写可复用程序。在 10 个模型和 3 个任务上进行评估。

Result: 强零样本任务性能不能可靠转化为强装瓶能力；零样本分数相近的模型装瓶后差异显著，60 次装瓶运行中有 48 次低于其零样本性能 95% 置信区间下界，31 次低于相同 token 预算下两种小模型蒸馏基线中较强者的表现。但装瓶可带来显著节省：在查询-商品相关性分类上，Opus 5 保留约 82% 的零样本 macro-F1，报告成本降低约 657 倍；同任务下 Opus 5 达到 Jev（专为廉价重复推理构建的"系统一"模型）约 94% 的 macro-F1，成本仅为 Jev 预测全工作负载成本的四分之一。

Conclusion: BOTTLED 为评估和改进智能体在大型重复工作负载中投入有限资源构建可复用解决方案的能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can solve many narrow tasks, but querying them separately for millions of related instances can be prohibitively expensive. Can LLM agents autonomously create cheaper solutions for such workloads? We call this ability "bottling": the ability to turn general capabilities into task-specific solutions that balance answer quality and amortised cost. We introduce BOTTLED, a benchmark in which agents receive an entire unlabelled workload and must complete it under fixed time, compute and LLM API budgets. Agents choose their own approach, such as training a small model or writing a reusable program. Across ten models and three tasks, we find that strong zero-shot task performance does not reliably translate into strong bottling capabilities. Models with similar zero-shot scores can differ substantially after bottling, and 48 of 60 bottling runs score below the lower bound of the 95% confidence interval of their model's zero-shot performance. Moreover, 31 of 60 runs underperform the stronger of two small-model distillation baselines with the same token budget. Nevertheless, bottling can yield substantial savings: on query-product relevance classification, Opus 5 retains about 82% of its zero-shot macro-F1 at roughly 657 times lower reported cost. Bottling is also competitive with Jev, a "system one" model built especially for cheap, repetitive inference: Opus 5 on the same task recovers about 94% of Jev's macro-F1 at a quarter of Jev's projected full-workload cost. BOTTLED provides a basis for evaluating and improving agents' ability to invest limited resources in reusable solutions for large, repetitive workloads.

</details>
